On MedQA, 13.4% of unanimous votes are wrong, and no agreement-based signal can flag them. Process signals raise the discrimination of correct from incorrect consensus from chance to 0.696 AUROC. The certified rule answers six in ten unanimous-layer questions at an observed selective risk of 9.0%, and nine in ten once in-domain calibration data accumulate.

</details>


### [63] [Cooperating with Future Collaborators: Multi-Agent RL under Staggered Participation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07578)
*Jianglin Qiao,Siyi Hu,Thien Hoang Nguyen,Zehong Cao,Salah Sukkarieh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对错开参与问题，SPL通过对先行者的前瞻性信息获取监督与后行者的接收结果监督，作为训练增强显著提高了多智能体协作性能，跨多种环境与骨干均表现出稳定收益（平均+14.1%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实任务中存在错开参与——部分智能体先行动并留下与任务相关的信息，而随后参与的智能体能利用该信息改进决策。此类跨时、跨智能体的因果依赖要求同时学会（1）早期提供何种信息有利于未来，以及（2）后期如何利用这些信息。

Method: SPL是一种训练时增强：对早期智能体施加‘prospective acquisition supervision’以引导其采取能为未来参与者提供有用信息的动作；对后期智能体施加‘outcome-supervised receiver learning’以学习如何基于早期留下的信息做出更优决策。SPL可与多种基于策略的MARL骨干结合。

Result: 在60个MPE/RWARE骨干设置比较中，SPL在每种情况下都获得更高的平均任务完成率，平均改善幅度为14.1%。此外结果扩展到八智能体团队和Isaac Lab的UAV-UGV物理环境，表明对算法、时间结构与实体环境均有改进效果。

Conclusion: 提出的SPL在训练阶段通过为早期参与者提供前瞻性獲取監督和为后期参与者提供结果监督的接收学习，有效解决了“错开参与”（staggered participation, SP）下的信息供给与利用问题，从而显著提升了多智能体协作任务的完成率。

Abstract: In cooperative Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning (MARL), agents are often trained under concurrent participation, while in many tasks some agents act earlier and leave task-relevant information that becomes useful to agents participating later. We study this setting as staggered participation (SP), which introduces a cross-time, cross-agent learning dependency because an early action may affect the return through the information it provides and the later policy that uses it. Learning under SP therefore requires both identifying what information is useful for future decisions and learning how later agents should use it. We propose Staggered Participation Learning (SPL), a training-time augmentation that addresses these two parts with prospective acquisition supervision for earlier agents and outcome-supervised receiver learning for later agents. We evaluate SPL across multiple policy-based MARL backbones, environments, and staggered-participation patterns. Across 60 MPE/RWARE backbone setting comparisons, SPL achieves higher observed mean task completion in every case, with an average difference of 14.1%. The gains also extend to eight-agent teams and a physics-based UAV-UGV environment in Isaac Lab, providing evidence across algorithmic, temporal, and embodied settings.

</details>


### [64] [LOGIC: An LLM Benchmark for Intent-Grounded Change Impact in Aerospace Electrical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07580)
*Muhammad Faraz Shoaib,Muhammad Qasim,Raisulhaq Mohammed Rizwan,Rahmatullah Safdar,Muzammil Adnan Shaik,Abdul Aleem Mohammed*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LOGIC isolates grounding from propagation for aerospace electrical-design change selection. Literal/structured evidence methods excel on anchored cases; LLMs outperform on paraphrase-relational cases. Trade-offs exist: strict evidence gating cuts false positives but hurts semantic selection and coverage; combine evidence + LLM reasoning + engineering review.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Aerospace electrical-design revisions often include multiple true changes but engineering requests may authorize only a subset; propagating all detected differences creates overly broad impact reports. Need controlled evaluation to measure grounding (selecting intended changes) separately from propagation.

Method: LOGIC: a controlled benchmark/framework where local LLMs ground a request against a deterministic candidate-change inventory before propagating selected changes through a typed electrical traceability graph; separates candidate-selection errors from downstream propagation errors; evaluates three 7--8B models and compares intent-agnostic, lexical, structured-evidence approaches and an oracle upper bound.

Result: 168 scenarios (144 selection, 24 abstention). On 96 anchored cases, gate-only structured evidence achieved candidate F1=1.0000 vs token-lexical 0.9677. On 12 relational-paraphrase cases, token-lexical F1=0.1772, gate-only F1=0.0000, LLMs 0.5000–0.6400. Grounding degrades as candidate inventory grows (4→64), while affected-element and typed-path accuracy stable when replayed over graphs ~1K–100K nodes. Strict evidence gating reduces false positives but can eliminate correct semantic matches. Evidence-empty abstention policy increased strict abstention accuracy to 0.6667 and reduced unsafe-report rates to 0.1667, but decreased answerable-case coverage by 16.0–27.1 points. Four of six conflicting requests remain unsafe per model.

Conclusion: Combining literal evidence matching with language-model reasoning and engineering review is advisable when intent cannot be reliably established; strict evidence gating reduces false positives but can remove correct semantic selections; model grounding degrades with larger candidate inventories while propagation accuracy remains stable across graph sizes.

Abstract: Aerospace electrical-design revisions can contain multiple genuine changes, although an engineering request may authorize only a subset. Propagating every detected difference can therefore produce overly broad impact reports. We present LOGIC, a controlled benchmark and evaluation framework in which locally deployable language models ground a request in a deterministic candidate-change inventory before selected changes are propagated through a typed electrical traceability graph. This separation permits candidate-selection errors to be distinguished from downstream propagation errors. LOGIC contains 168 scenarios, including 144 selection and 24 abstention cases. We evaluate three 7--8B models against intent-agnostic, lexical, and structured-evidence methods, with an oracle-root upper bound. On 96 explicitly anchored selection cases, gate-only structured evidence achieves candidate F1 of 1.0000, compared with 0.9677 for token-lexical matching. On 12 relational-paraphrase cases, token-lexical F1 is 0.1772 and gate-only F1 is 0.0000, compared with 0.5000--0.6400 for the large language models. Model grounding degrades as candidate inventories grow from 4 to 64 changes, while affected-element and typed-path accuracy remain comparatively stable when frozen selections are replayed over graphs of approximately 1K to 100K nodes. Strict evidence gating suppresses false positives but can remove correct semantic selections. An exploratory evidence-empty abstention policy raises strict abstention accuracy to 0.6667 for all three models and reduces unsafe-report rates to 0.1667, while decreasing answerable-case coverage by 16.0--27.1 percentage points. Four of six conflicting requests remain unsafe for each model. These findings support combining literal evidence and language-model reasoning with engineering review when intent cannot be established reliably.

</details>


### [65] [Representation Bias, Correction Transfer, and Resolution Sensitivity in Three-Dimensional Mitochondrial Morphometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07582)
*Farouk Ganiyu Adewumi,Timothy Oladunni*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 作者量化了三维线粒体测度在不同处理与分辨率下的系统偏差、展示了受控校正与回归校准的效果与局限，并警示不应自动将校正或不确定性转移到不同来源或子群。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 定量成像流水线在不同实现或处理参数下会产生可复现但系统性的表征偏倚，须建立实证性可靠性评估以区分处理引入的变化与潜在生物学差异，并探索可行的校准与修正策略。

Method: 使用3D Mitochondria Shape Library与MitoEM数据集：比较占据体素(volume from occupancy)与参考网格体积(mesh volumes)、边界位移分析、对原标注流水线的受控重实现（去除深度偏移）、训练并冻结一个基于占据特征的回归模型以在新对象上校正，以及通过改变体素平面间距评估分辨率敏感性。统计指标包含均值百分比偏差、ICC、绝对百分比误差、中位数、置信区间覆盖率及秩相关。

Result: 占据体素体积比参考网格平均膨胀3.665%，ICC=0.994；边界平均向外位移0.00304归一化单位；移除深度偏移在55个对象上将体积误差平均减少1.57个百分点（约占45%再现性膨胀）；冻结回归在2728个对象将中位绝对百分比误差从3.481%降到0.664%，但校准误差界覆盖率仅为92.1%整体且在低占据子组仅49.2%；将平面间距从8nm粗化到24nm在550个对象上使中位表面积下降10.60%、球形度上升11.76%，秩相关仍为0.994。

Conclusion: 三维线粒体形态度量在不同处理流程下会产生系统性偏差；通过边界位移分析与受控重实现可以部分消除这类偏差，而基于占据特征的回归校准能显著降低平均误差但校准覆盖不完备，分辨率粗化对表面积与球形度有实质性影响，说明精确性、校准不确定性与分辨率敏感性需分别评估，且跨源修正不可默认迁移。

Abstract: Quantitative imaging pipelines can produce precise but systematically different measurements of the same object. We present an empirical reliability assessment of three-dimensional mitochondrial morphometry that connects representation bias, a controlled processing intervention, correction transfer, and resolution sensitivity. Using 2,720 development objects from the 3D Mitochondria Shape Library for Optical Microscopy, we find that occupancy-derived volumes exceed reference mesh volumes by 3.665% on average despite an intraclass correlation coefficient of 0.994. Boundary analysis identifies an outward label displacement of 0.00304 normalized units. In a controlled label-pipeline reimplementation, removing the depth offset reduces volume error in all 55 analyzed objects by a mean of 1.57 percentage points, approximately 45% of mean reproduced inflation; the source of the remainder is not isolated. A frozen regression using occupancy-derived features reduces median absolute percentage error from 3.481% to 0.664% in 2,728 previously unused objects from the same resource. However, its calibrated error bound covers only 92.1% overall and 49.2% in a low-occupancy subgroup, demonstrating that accuracy and uncertainty transfer must be evaluated separately. In 550 rat-cortex objects from the MitoEM resource, coarsening in-plane spacing from 8 to 24 nanometers changes median surface area by minus 10.60% and sphericity by plus 11.76%, despite a rank correlation of 0.994. These results provide quantitative checks for distinguishing processing-induced descriptor changes from candidate biological differences, without establishing biological invariance or cross-source correction transfer.

</details>


### [66] [Personal-Agent Mediated Recommendation with Cross-Platform User History](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07588)
*Yu Xia,Jiangfan Zhang,Jun Xiao,Julian McAuley,Xiangjun Fan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出个人代理中介推荐范式与MediateRec基准，提出PAMO通过反事实遮盖历史估计个人支持并在平台相对价值下重新分配优势质量，理论保证并在实验中改进救援-损害平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Shift recommender systems from platform-centric personalization to user-governed personalization where personal LLM agents can act across services; need principled methods to mediate between platform rankings (with platform-local signals) and agent-authorized cross-platform history to balance rescues and harmful overrides.

Method: Introduce Personal-Agent Mediated Recommendation formalism; construct MediateRec benchmark with proxy and real cross-platform tests; propose Personal Attribution Mediation Optimization (PAMO) which uses counterfactual masking of user cross-platform history to estimate personal mediation support and reallocate rank-aware advantage mass under a platform-relative value floor; provide theoretical proof of property and empirical evaluation vs outcome-only RL and LLM agents.

Result: On MediateRec, PAMO consistently outperforms matched outcome-only RL baselines across seen and unseen platforms and in a real cross-platform test, achieving better rescue-harm trade-offs; experiments also show that even strong LLMs can introduce non-negligible harmful overrides.

Conclusion: PAMO enables personal-agent mediation that preserves cutoff-level advantage mass and is locally optimal among first-order reallocations preserving that mass without lowering average platform-relative value, improving rescue-harm balance in cross-platform recommendation.

Abstract: Modern recommendation is shifting from platform-centric personalization toward user-governed personalization, where a personal LLM agent can act on the user's behalf across services. We formalize this emerging paradigm as Personal-Agent Mediated Recommendation: a platform recommender ranks a candidate set using platform-local information, and a personal agent uses user-authorized cross-platform history to mediate the resulting ranking and produce the final top-K slate. Such mediation is nontrivial: the platform ranking can encode strong population evidence that the personal agent cannot observe, so effective mediation must therefore balance beneficial rescues against harmful overrides. To study this trade-off, we introduce MediateRec, a benchmark that includes scalable proxy cross-platform environments and a real cross-platform test under a controlled platform-agent information boundary. To train the agent to use cross-platform history effectively, we further propose Personal Attribution Mediation Optimization (PAMO), which counterfactually masks that history to estimate personal mediation support and reallocates rank-aware advantage mass under a platform-relative value floor. We theoretically prove that PAMO preserves cutoff-level advantage mass and is locally optimal among first-order reallocations that preserve this mass without lowering average platform-relative value. Experiments on MediateRec show that personal-agent mediation enables meaningful platform corrections, yet even strong proprietary LLMs introduce non-negligible harmful overrides. PAMO consistently improves over matched outcome-only RL across seen and unseen target platforms and on the real cross-platform test, while achieving a better rescue-harm balance.

</details>


### [67] [LSC-DPO: Learning-Signal-Controlled Direct Preference Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07592)
*Yang Qu,Yusheng Han,Chengjia Feng,Handan Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 识别并控制DPO中sigmoid项作为关键学习信号，提出LSC-DPO及补偿规则，能稳定目标信号并提升偏好优化效果，同时降低初始化敏感性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在训练过程中，随着偏好边际增长，标准的logistic DPO损失对后续变化变得不敏感；需要一种方式保持学习信号在对模型更新有效的区间，从而持续利用偏好数据进行对齐。

Method: 从损失函数几何视角出发，将DPO中的sigmoid因子视为局部灵敏度（学习信号），提出对该信号进行动态调控的LSC-DPO方法；基于对数空间分析给出稳定跟踪目标信号区间的条件，并设计信号预算补偿规则以修正瞬态轨迹差异。

Result: 在AlpacaEval 2、MT-Bench和Anthropic-HH数据集/基准上，LSC-DPO持续优于原始DPO和其他强基线；信号预算补偿显著减少了不同系数初始化引起的性能方差。

Conclusion: LSC-DPO通过对DPO中“sigmoid学习信号”进行动态控制，能稳定保持目标学习信号区间并提高首选项优化效果；加入的信号预算补偿规则能显著降低不同系数初始化带来的性能波动。

Abstract: Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) has become a standard reward-model-free approach for aligning language models with preference data. However, as the scaled preference margin grows during training, the logistic DPO loss becomes progressively less sensitive to further changes. We study DPO from a loss-level geometric perspective and identify the sigmoid factor as a learning signal that characterizes the local sensitivity of the objective. Based on this view, we propose Learning-Signal-Controlled Direct Preference Optimization (LSC-DPO), which dynamically regulates the learning signal near a target regime. A log-space analysis establishes conditions for stable tracking of the target learning-signal regime. Experiments on AlpacaEval 2, MT-Bench, and Anthropic-HH show that LSC-DPO consistently improves over DPO and strong preference-optimization baselines. We further find that different coefficient initializations induce distinct transient learning-signal trajectories even when their later signal levels become similar. Based on this observation, we derive a signal-budget compensation rule that adjusts the target learning signal to compensate for these transient differences. The resulting compensation substantially reduces performance variation across coefficient initializations.

</details>


### [68] [Beyond Scalar IoU: Structured Verification from Rollout Groups for Video Temporal Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07601)
*Youngjae Cho,Won Young Jhoo,Jongsuk Kim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SUTURE通过对回滚组条件化的验证器（利用分歧与覆盖）重分配奖励，实现了IoU+协方差修正的精确分解，显著改善视频时序定位的RL适配效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的重叠验证器通常独立评分每个回滚，忽略回滚组的联合信息，而时序定位任务天生可直接将生成区间与真值比对，利用组信息有望提供更有信息量的奖励信号。

Method: 提出基于回滚组的验证器，在两个尺度上利用群体结构：回滚间的分歧用于控制目标重加权，位置处的覆盖度决定奖励质量的重新分配；证明该验证器可分解为标准IoU项与由回滚组决定的协方差修正项，并引入局部梯度诊断分析模型偏好。

Result: 在五个时序定位基准上，SUTURE在所有报告的IoU阈值上均带来性能提升；训练策略在推理痕迹中较少出现视频开头锚定现象，后期事件的首个时间提及更常与标注目标重叠。

Conclusion: SUTURE通过在回滚组上条件化验证器并利用其群体结构，提高了视频时序定位中对生成时间区间的评估质量，从而提升了下游基于可验证奖励的强化学习适配性能。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) provides a natural framework for adapting pretrained models to video temporal grounding, where generated temporal intervals can be scored directly against ground truth intervals. Yet existing overlap verifiers typically score each rollout independently, leaving the joint structure of the rollout group unused. We introduce SUTURE, which conditions verification on the rollout group and exploits its structure at two complementary scales: disagreement across rollouts controls how strongly the target is reweighted, while coverage at each position determines where reward mass is redistributed. We show that the resulting verifier admits an exact decomposition into the standard IoU term and a covariance correction determined by the rollout group. A local gradient diagnostic finds a preference for responses covering relatively less supported target regions in the analyzed groups. Across five temporal grounding benchmarks, SUTURE improves grounding performance at every reported IoU threshold. Its trained policy also shows less video-start anchoring in reasoning traces: for later events, the first temporal mention more often overlaps the annotated target. Together, these results show that the joint structure of a rollout group can support a more informative temporal verifier.

</details>


### [69] [VALSE: Vertical Adaptive Layer Skipping for Efficient Inference in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07606)
*Jia-Dong Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 给出纵向按样本跳层的理论框架与证明，提出VALSE实现按样本非连续跳层，用以在保持性能的同时降低平均计算量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 减少深层模型按样本激活的深度（垂直稀疏性）以节省计算资源，同时保持对困难样本的足够表达能力；补全现有关于按样本跳层、早退出与MoE之间理论联系的空白。

Method: 提出轻量难度估计器在前几层为每个样本打分，并以分层门控按层概率选择性跳过任意层（非连续），同时给出闭式期待FLOPs表达式与函数空间性质证明。

Result: 得到三个基础性理论结果（期待FLOPs公式、函数空间的包含与严格包含定理、与MoE的对偶性命题），并在原型规模上验证了基于难度估计器的VALSE可行性。

Conclusion: 本文提出并理论化了纵向自适应跳层（vertical adaptive layer skipping）的框架，证明了期待FLOPs公式、跳层模型与全层函数空间的包含/严格包含关系，以及与MoE的结构对偶性，并基于此提出了可按样本跳过任意中间层的VALSE方法。

Abstract: This paper establishes a theoretical framework for vertical adaptive layer skipping, proving three foundational results: (i) an Expected FLOPs formula (theorem 2) giving a closed-form expression for the computational cost of arbitrary per-sample skip schedules as a function of layer-wise skip probabilities; (ii) function-space superset (theorem 10) and strict inclusion (theorem 11) theorems showing that skip-layer models are strictly contained in---yet meaningfully approximate---the full-layer function space, with an explicit separating example; and (iii) a structural duality between VALSE and Mixture-of-Experts architectures (proposition 6), positioning vertical depth-wise sparsity as the orthogonal counterpart to horizontal width-wise sparsity. Building on this theory, we propose VALSE (Vertical Adaptive Layer Skipping for Efficiency), a per-sample, non-contiguous layer skipping method: a lightweight difficulty estimator scores each input from the first few layers, and per-layer gates selectively skip redundant layers---including arbitrary middle layers while retaining deeper ones---so that only the necessary depth is activated for each input, whose feasibility is preliminarily assessed at prototype scale.

</details>


### [70] [BioStudyBench: Evaluating Agents on Post-Cutoff Biomedical Studies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07614)
*David Li,Shaamil Karim,Christian Gensbigler*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: BioStudyBench shows large gains from giving agents access to data and tools, and that closed-weight models currently outperform open-weight counterparts on reproducing biomedical study findings.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: To evaluate whether AI agents can match reported findings of published biomedical studies using only public data and date-restricted literature search, and to isolate the benefit of real-data analysis from retrieval of known published answers.

Method: Constructed BioStudyBench: 25 long-horizon analysis tasks sampled from studies published after model knowledge cutoffs (semi-automatically filtered from 404,019 PubMed records). For each task, agents receive a neutral research question but no data files and must find/download public data, search literature via tools limited to records before model cutoffs, and perform data analysis. Each task was run twice (with and without access to data/tools) across eight models to separate gains from new evidence vs prior knowledge.

Result: Access to data and tools increased average pass rate by 47 percentage points versus the no-data baseline. The best open-weight model passed 81.3% of tasks, while the best closed-weight model passed 94.7%.

Conclusion: Access to public data and date-restricted literature-search tools substantially improves AI agents' ability to reproduce published biomedical findings; closed-weight models outperform open-weight models on this benchmark.

Abstract: We evaluate whether AI agents can match the reported findings of published biomedical studies using public data. Existing evaluations do not consistently separate analysis from prior knowledge or retrieval of the published answer. We introduce BioStudyBench, a benchmark of 25 long-horizon analysis tasks drawn from studies first published between July and September 2026, after the developer-reported knowledge cutoffs of the models we evaluate, semi-automatically filtered down from 404,019 PubMed records. In each task, the agent receives a neutral research question but no data files, so it must find and download the relevant public data, search the literature through tools that return only records dated before its cutoff, and report findings through data analysis. To measure gains over prior knowledge, we run every task both with and without access to data and tools. Across eight models, access to data and tools raises the pass rate by 47 percentage points on average over the no-data baseline. Open-weight models across sizes trail closed-weight models, with the best open-weight model passing 81.3% of tasks against 94.7% for the best closed-weight model.

</details>


### [71] [Explore, Then Commit: Measurement-Efficient Scientific Law Discovery with Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07620)
*Kautik Mandve,Dileepa Fernando*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Complete protocol cuts measurements and numerical error substantially, but symbolic recovery, causal sources of improvement, and robustness beyond noiseless direct-equation tasks remain open and require targeted ablations and broader evaluation.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: To make scientific law discovery measurement-efficient by combining LLM-driven hypothesis generation with programmatic experiment planning and constrained measurement acquisition, reducing the number of measurements needed to recover governing equations.

Method: An LLM proposes hypotheses; a programmatic planner executes structured probes with restricted measurement batches, automatic numerical diagnostics, and optional interpreter access; a fresh prompt synthesizes a final symbolic law from a fixed set of observations. Eight configurations were compared across 576 NewtonBench trials on 12 physics modules with GPT-4.1-mini and a medium-difficulty GPT-4.1 replication.

Result: Interpreter-enabled planners used far fewer measurements (e.g. 8.6 vs 22.5 for GPT-4.1-mini; 8.9 vs 43.0 for GPT-4.1) and reduced magnitude-based RMS logarithmic error dramatically (from 2.514 to 0.202 and from 0.626 to 0.149 respectively). Symbolic-accuracy gains were inconsistent across modules; random acquisition was competitive with disagreement-based scoring. Measurement savings occurred in every module but unequal batch constraints confound attribution. An audit retained incomplete/invalid submissions for a coverage-sensitive view.

Conclusion: The proposed explore-then-commit protocol is a promising, measurement-efficient pipeline for discovering numerical laws: it substantially reduces measurement counts and numerical error when interpreter-enabled planners are used, but symbolic recovery is inconsistent and causal attribution of gains (acquisition vs planner vs synthesizer) remains unresolved; generalization to noisy or indirect tasks is not demonstrated.

Abstract: Scientific law discovery requires selecting measurements and converting evidence into a governing equation. We evaluate an explore-then-commit protocol in which a large language model proposes hypotheses, a programmatic planner gathers measurements, and a fresh prompt synthesizes the final law from fixed observations. The protocol combines structured probes, automatic numerical diagnostics, restricted measurement batches, and optional interpreter access. Across 576 NewtonBench trials, we compare eight configurations on 12 physics modules using GPT-4.1-mini and a medium-difficulty GPT-4.1 replication. On medium tasks, interpreter-enabled planners use 8.6 versus 22.5 measurements per trial for GPT-4.1-mini and 8.9 versus 43.0 for GPT-4.1. Their mean magnitude-based root-mean-squared logarithmic error falls from 2.514 to 0.202 and from 0.626 to 0.149, respectively. An additional audit retains incomplete and invalid submissions in a coverage-sensitive analysis. Observed symbolic-accuracy gains are less consistent across modules, and random acquisition is competitive with disagreement scoring. Measurement savings occur in every module, but unequal batch constraints prevent attributing them solely to acquisition quality. These results support the complete protocol as a promising measurement-efficient configuration, while leaving its causal components and generalization beyond noiseless direct-equation tasks unresolved.

</details>


### [72] [Learning to Outgrow a Theory: Experimental Discovery Beyond the Initial Hypothesis Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07627)
*SiYuan Ma,Albert Gao,Chunzheng Zhu,Xin Yan,Wenlong Zhang,Wenxin Zhang,Luqi Gong,Tianlin Li,Qixin Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出并学习一种联合提出结构编辑与诊断实验的策略，通过类级可区分性和任意时间有效证据触发修正，在有限实验预算下显著提高模型恢复与库不充分检测，并在多项基准上展现迁移性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统科学发现系统在固定假设空间内优化实验，当所有候选模型都缺失相同机制时会出现失败（候选间分歧消失但模型类系统性错误）。需要一种能同时提出模型类扩展和验证性实验的机制，以检测并修正库的不充分性。

Method: 提出“实验性模型类修正”（experimental model-class revision）：策略在每一步同时提出一个结构性编辑候选与一个诊断性实验。目标由类级可区分性（要求单一共享参数化必须解释所选实验）与任意时间有效的序贯证据检验组成，只有在当前模型类被拒绝后才进行结构修正。通过在受控动力学环境上训练/学习此联合策略并评估其在未见环境上的迁移性。

Result: 在400个持出动力学环境上，联合策略在32次真实实验预算下实现89.5%精确恢复，比最强基线高10.0个百分点，同时减少执行的实验次数和候选拟合次数。学习到的修正—实验配对在未见机制组合、持出但可表达的原语、参数外推和实验成本变化下均能迁移；当真实机制不在编辑语法内时，能在88%案例中检测出库不充分，虚假支持率为5.5%。此外，修正收益可迁移到ODEBench、ODEBase和DiscoverPhysics任务。

Conclusion: 将结构编辑提议与诊断实验设计联合起来，可以显著提高在模型类缺失机制时恢复真实机制的能力，并能在检验到当前模型类被拒绝时触发结构修正，支持把‘应该使理论可表达哪些机制’与‘在哪里收集证据’作为单一的序贯决策问题来看待。

Abstract: Scientific discovery systems typically optimize experiments within a fixed hypothesis space. This creates a failure mode when all available candidates omit the same missing mechanism: candidate disagreement can collapse even while the model class is systematically wrong. We formulate experimental model-class revision, in which a discovery policy jointly proposes a structural edit and a diagnostic experiment that tests whether that edit is necessary. The method couples a class-level distinguishability objective, in which one shared parameterization must explain all selected experiments, with anytime-valid sequential evidence that triggers structural revision only after the current class is rejected. On 400 held-out controlled dynamical environments, the joint policy reaches 89.5% exact recovery with a budget of 32 real experiments, improving the strongest matched baseline by 10.0 percentage points while requiring fewer executed experiments and candidate fits. The learned revision-experiment pairing transfers across unseen mechanism combinations, held-out but expressible primitives, parameter extrapolation, and shifted experiment costs; when the true mechanism is outside the edit grammar, it detects library insufficiency in 88% of cases with a 5.5% false-support rate. Revision gains also transfer to ODEBench and ODEBase model-library tasks, as well as DiscoverPhysics worlds. These results support a view of scientific discovery in which deciding what mechanisms a theory should make expressible and where to collect evidence are treated as a single sequential decision problem.

</details>


### [73] [Measuring climate backlash in Twitter and Reddit archives: Lexical definitions, recorded responses and participant turnover](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07634)
*Wentao Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Measurement decisions, platform response fields, and archival boundaries substantially determine what social-media archives can (and cannot) tell us about climate-action backlash; the paper documents these consequences using full Twitter and Reddit archives and conservative lexical rules.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Social-media archives are widely used to study resistance to climate action, but lexical occurrences, response counters, and observed participant lists capture different social processes. The paper aims to expose how measurement choices and archive constraints shape what can legitimately be claimed about backlash and resistance in these datasets.

Method: The authors processed all supplied Twitter and Reddit archive files without sampling, applied transparent, non-exclusive lexical/frame rules, linked frame co-occurrence to source-specific temporal and response models, and decomposed event-period changes into returning-author expression versus participant turnover. They also compared cross-discourse history for forecasting, analyzed platform-specific response associations (likes, Reddit responses), and examined endpoint/archival effects on migration inferences.

Result: Key findings: (1) Renewable-energy terms on Reddit co-occur with cost-related language; (2) Using narrower backlash phrases reduces cross-source contrasts and reverses the Paris Agreement contrast in submissions; (3) Cross-discourse history does not improve primary-context forecasts; (4) Denial/hoax terms correlate with higher Twitter likes, while Reddit response associations vary by frame, outcome, and author specification; (5) Around the 2019 global climate strike both returning-author expression and participant turnover raised protest-language shares; (6) An archive endpoint prevents inferring a Climate Twitter migration; (7) Many crossed-cluster estimates lack released intervals and joint author/month covariance estimates fail, constraining formal inference.

Conclusion: The paper concludes that inferences about climate-action resistance drawn from social-media archives are highly contingent on operational definitions, platform-specific response fields, and the boundaries of archival observation. The study provides an archive-based account of these measurement consequences rather than a direct estimate of individual opposition, persuasion, or advocacy effects, and highlights that incomplete released intervals and failed joint author/month covariance estimates limit formal statistical inference.

Abstract: Social media archives are often used to study resistance to climate action, but words, response counters and observed participants do not measure the same social process. We examine four supplied Twitter and Reddit archives by processing all registered files without sampling and applying transparent, non-exclusive lexical rules. The study links frame co-occurrence to source-specific temporal and response models, then separates event-period changes among returning authors from participant turnover. Renewable-energy terms accompany cost-related language on Reddit, yet narrower backlash phrases sharply reduce cross-source contrasts and reverse the sign of the Paris Agreement contrast in submissions. Cross-discourse history does not improve eligible primary-context forecasts. Denial/hoax terms are associated with higher recorded Twitter likes, whereas Reddit response associations depend on frame, outcome and author specification. Around the 2019 global climate strike, returning-author expression and participant turnover both contribute to increased protest-language shares. An archive endpoint prevents the corresponding Climate Twitter migration inference. Most crossed-cluster estimates lack released intervals, and joint author/month response covariance estimates fail, restricting formal inference. These results show how operational definitions, platform-specific response fields and observation boundaries shape what can be claimed about climate backlash. The contribution is an archive-based account of these measurement consequences, rather than a measure of individual opposition, persuasion or advocacy-induced backlash.

</details>


### [74] [Learning Explainable Representations of Complex Game-playing Strategies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07638)
*Abhijeet Krishnan,Colin M. Potts,Arnav Jhala,Harshad Khadilkar,Shirish Karande,Chris Martens*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种从博弈行为序列自动合成可执行策略程序的方法，能在国际象棋和网格环境任务上学得有效策略并用于RL代理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Humans abstract strategies from experience to explain and predict others' actions and to guide their own play; mimicking this can improve RL agents' sample efficiency and interpretability.

Method: Automatically synthesize programs representing strategies and policies from sequences of gameplay actions; applied to chess and grid-based environments to generate executable procedures used by RL agents.

Result: Learned program-like strategies produce effective actions in experiments on chess and grid tasks, demonstrating feasibility of learning such executable strategies from gameplay data.

Conclusion: Paper proposes learning executable procedural strategies from gameplay action sequences; learned strategies are effective for chess and grid tasks and can be inferred from gameplay data.

Abstract: As part of learning to play complex games, human players develop develop abstractions for concepts and strategies of gameplay consistent with game rules to improve their performance. These concepts are applied to explain other players' actions, and to inform their own actions in-game. Understanding other players' strategies is a crucial part of such improvement, but requires time and effort. In this paper, we propose a strategy similar to human cognition for training RL agents to synthesize learned strategies and policies as executable procedures based on sequences of gameplay actions. We present methods to automatically learn such programs to play chess and to solve tasks in a grid-based environment. We show that the learned strategies produce effective actions, and can be learned from gameplay data.

</details>


### [75] [Towards the Automatic Synthesis of Interpretable Chess Tactics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07640)
*Abhijeet Krishnan,Chris Martens*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于PAL的符号子策略用于棋类战术解释，初步验证可生成与初学者相当的走子与评估框架，但尚需更严格的对比、扩展与人类可用性评测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 顶尖强化学习代理在棋类上优于人类，其采用的策略具有可学习价值。目标是用可解释的符号化战术模式抽取与展现这些策略，从而为人类玩家提供可理解的改进建议。

Method: 受棋类战术启发，作者用归纳逻辑编程系统PAL学习棋盘模式并构建符号子策略；提出一个散度指标（divergence metric）用于将模型输出与随机基线比较；并通过将该子策略嵌入现成棋力引擎来进行计算评估。

Result: 作者找到一组由PAL导出的战术模式，能给出与人类初学者相当的落子建议；散度指标显示该模型相较随机基线具有一定判别力；并提出了通过引擎整合进行计算性评估的方案。

Conclusion: 本文的符号子策略模型能够以可解释的战术模式建议与人类初学者相近的走子，表明将归纳逻辑编程学到的战术模式用于棋类策略解释是可行的，但当前工作仍为初步验证，覆盖度和竞技水平有限。

Abstract: State-of-the-art reinforcement learning agents are capable of outperforming human experts at games like chess, Go and StarCraft II. These agents do not simply take advantage of their digital hardware in being able to react and calculate faster than humans, but employ better strategies that lead to more victories. Interpreting these strategies would give human players valuable insight into how to improve their play. In this preliminary work, we propose a symbolic sub-policy model for playing chess. Inspired by chess tactics, our model attempts to incorporate domain knowledge to improve interpretability. We adapt patterns learned by an inductive logic programming system called PAL to derive our model. We contribute a divergence metric to evaluate our model against a random baseline, and find a set of tactics that is able to suggest moves of similar playing strength to a human beginner. Finally, we propose a computational evaluation scheme for the model by augmenting an off-the-shelf engine with it.

</details>


### [76] [Matching Object or Relation? Tracing Abstract Reasoning Inside VLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07646)
*Gouki Minegishi,Hiroki Furuta,Takeshi Kojima,Yusuke Iwasawa,Yutaka Matsuo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 作者用受控RMTS任务+逐层RSA与因果消融在多模型上定位了实现VLM抽象推理的“关系电路”，并展示了若干可调因素可促成类似人类的关系转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准揭示了VLM在抽象推理上的系统性失败，但不能指出失败原因或缺失的认知能力；希望通过实验与内部可解释性分析来揭示实现抽象推理的机制。

Method: 采用来源于比较与发展心理学的Relational Match-to-Sample (RMTS)范式，构建参数化受控刺激集；在前沿闭源API（GPT、Claude、Gemini）与三类开源模型（Qwen3.5、Gemma-4、InternVL3）上评估；做逐层表征相似性分析（RSA）并结合因果中介（causal mediation）与头级别消融以识别并验证实现抽象推理的子电路；额外在ARC-AGI-1上验证消融效果的泛化。

Result: 发现四个影响因素能重塑性能并产生发展样式的关系转变；证明存在两个竞争表征电路（早期表面、后期关系）；定位到若干“关系头”，消融这些头对RMTS与更复杂模型任务均有显著负面影响，说明这些组件在抽象推理中承担因果角色。

Conclusion: 论文给出了一个机制层面的解释：视觉-语言模型（VLMs）在抽象推理上受两条竞争电路驱动——早期电路以表面物体特征组织表征，后期电路以抽象关系组织表征；通过控制四个“杠杆”（能力层级、模型尺度、场景物体数、每物体噪声）可使模型出现类似人类的“关系转变”发展轨迹；在更大模型上定位并消融这些“关系头”会显著损害任务与泛化性能，表明该电路在更宽任务上被调用。

Abstract: Vision Language Models (VLMs) excel on visual benchmarks but fail systematically on tasks requiring abstract reasoning. Existing benchmarks document this failure but cannot say \emph{why} it happens or which cognitive capability is missing. We close this gap by adopting the Relational Match-to-Sample (RMTS) paradigm from comparative and developmental psychology and pairing it with a mechanistic analysis of the model's internals. On a parametrically controlled stimulus set evaluated across frontier API models (GPT, Claude, Gemini) and three open-source families (Qwen3.5, Gemma-4, InternVL3), we identify four levers that shift VLMs toward the relational match---capability tier, model scale, the number of objects per scene, and the absence of per-object stimulus noise---together producing a developmental-like trajectory that mirrors the human \emph{relational shift}. Opening up the model, a per-layer representational similarity analysis and a causal mediation analysis reveal that VLM abstract reasoning is implemented by two competing circuits: an early circuit that organises images by their surface object features, and a late circuit that organises them by their abstract relation. Extending the analysis to ARC-AGI-1, we find that ablating the relational heads identified on RMTS degrades performance more than ablating random heads, indicating that the relational circuit is recruited beyond our controlled stimuli. We hope this mechanism-level view serves as a step toward understanding how abstract reasoning is implemented in VLMs.

</details>


### [77] [Where Rules End and Judges Begin: Measuring the Judgment Boundary in Multi-Agent Systems Security](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07657)
*Shaswata Mitra,Raj Patel,Subash Neupane,Sudip Mittal,Md Rayhanur Rahman,Shahram Rahimi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DEFER1通过“确定性优先＋人类/多模型裁判”显著降低MAS中的攻击成功率，但风险评分与人工裁判的局限仍需解决以提高准确性与可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前多智能体系统面临多样化对抗性输入，但现有防御通常单一评估、片段化实施，难以综合应对复杂攻击且难以审计与保证安全性。

Method: 提出并实现DEFER1：基于五项防御原则的工程化实现，包含28项序列化的确定性检查（优先阻断）与一个四人裁判面板负责无法被规则决定的提案；在四个独立领域上评估。

Result: 在四个领域的独立测试中，攻击成功率从≈30.0%降至≈3.0%；其中78%的被阻断攻击由确定性检查完成；在安全运维领域仅约25%的提案会进入裁判面板；但风险评分批准门控错误地批准了多数攻击提案，且很少批准合法提案，暴露出风险评估的可靠性问题。

Conclusion: 将确定性规则优先与“剩余判断”面板相结合，可显著降低多智能体系统中对抗性攻击成功率，但仍存在评估门槛误判、人工裁决开销与可扩展性问题。

Abstract: LLM-based multi-agent systems (MAS) engage tools, share memory, and delegate tasks, often encountering adversarial content. Current defenses for MAS are typically evaluated in isolation, focusing on one attack type at a time, which can lead to costly and hard-to-audit outcomes. This study organizes defenses into five principles, implementing them as DEFER1 (DEterministic-First Enforcement with Residual judgment), which includes a cascade of 28 checks that blocks what it can and refers the rest to a panel of four judges. In independent testing across four domains, attack success rates drop from about 30.0% to approximately 3.0%, with 78% of blocked attacks handled by deterministic checks. Only a quarter of proposals reach the judges in the security-operations domain, illustrating that the rules provide security for attacks violating clear policies, while judges manage those that only misrepresent intent. Both systems have weaknesses, such as a risk-score approval gate that inaccurately approves most attack proposals but few legitimate ones, highlighting the challenges in assessing threats accurately.

</details>


### [78] [Massive Activation Gating Channel in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07661)
*Minjia Mao,Shi Chen,Bowen Yin,Xiao Fang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 发现并命名了控制 massive activations 的单一输入通道（MAGC），在多个 LLM 上验证其作用，并提供了基于输出约化为二次形式的理论解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机是解释为什么少数隐藏通道会呈现异常大幅度激活（massive activations），以及这些激活如何在预训练 LLM 中沿着计算流传播形成，进而为模型解释性与控制提供机制性的理解。

Method: 作者通过在六个不同的 LLM（覆盖四个模型家族和不同规模）上定位并验证了 MAGC 的存在与作用，结合理论推导分析在 MAGC 极端时 spike FFN 的输出如何约化为混合下投影矩阵若干列的二次形式，从而解释 massive activations 的来源。

Result: 经验上发现：对所考察的多个 LLM，存在位置固定的 MAGC；操纵该通道（使其取极端值）能触发 spike FFN 输出的 massive activations。理论上给出当 MAGC 极端时输出约化为二次形式并混合若干下投影列，从而导致 massive activations。

Conclusion: 论文结论是：大语言模型中“massive activations” 的出现由输入嵌入到某个“spike”前馈网络（FFN）的一条固定通道单独控制，该通道被称为 massive activation gating channel (MAGC)。当 MAGC 的数值足够大或足够小（取决于模型）时，spike FFN 的输出会出现大幅激活。理论上，当 MAGC 极端时，spike FFN 的输出渐进地简化为一个二次形式，混合了下投影矩阵的少数列，这些列本身具有 massive activations 的形态，因此输出也呈现出 massive activations。

Abstract: Massive activations, a phenomenon in which a small number of hidden channels exhibit exceptionally large magnitudes, are pervasive in large language models (LLMs). However, the mechanism by which a token develops massive activations as it propagates through a pretrained LLM remains poorly understood. In this paper, we find that the emergence of massive activations is controlled by a single channel in the input embedding to a spike feed-forward network (FFN). The position of this channel is fixed for a particular LLM. We name this channel the massive activation gating channel (MAGC). When the value of the MAGC is sufficiently large (or small, depending on the LLM), the output of the spike FFN exhibits massive activations. Examining six LLMs across four model families and different model sizes, we verify the existence and effect of MAGC. We further provide a theoretical explanation of the mechanism by which MAGC induces massive activations. When the value of MAGC is sufficiently large (or small), the output of a spike FFN asymptotically reduces to a quadratic form that mixes a few columns of the down-projection matrix of the FFN. Since these columns exhibit the shape of massive activations, the output therefore exhibits massive activations.

</details>


### [79] [EIO-Agents: The Missing Semantic Layer for AI Agent Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07675)
*Fouad Bousetouane*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: EIO-Agents为AI代理评估提供了一个两层规范：EIO语义本体定义证据与声明的意义与推导规则，PER作为内容寻址的便携记录保存整个证据—决策链，目的在于实现可验证、可复现和可解释的评估工件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI代理在关键环境中部署时，使用的评分、轨迹和陪审意见缺乏统一语义，无法明确说明证据支持何种声明、声明能建立哪些结论以及如何驱动决策，导致评估结果难以解释和复核。

Method: 设计并定义了两层规范：1) EIO：基于类型化证据、版本化行为谓词、证据合同、声明、见证规则、证明状态、复现性以及可计算的指标和结论推导的语义本体；2) PER：基于内容可寻址的便携评价记录，作为保存证据—决策链的系统化记录，可重新派生和验证。

Result: 提出并描述了EIO-Agents规范，明确了如何用EIO对证据和声明进行语义化建模，并用PER保存评价记录，从而使评估成为可检验的工件，支持独立检查、复现和解释。

Conclusion: EIO-Agents通过在语义层（EIO）和记录层（PER）之间建立互操作规范，解决了AI代理评估中证据、声明与决策链条不透明的问题，能够支持可解释、可验证和可复现的评估结果，从而提高评估在生产环境中的问责性和可靠性。

Abstract: AI agents are entering production in increasingly consequential environments without a shared semantic standard for what their evaluations actually mean. Scores, traces, judge outputs, and multi juror findings are increasingly used to justify readiness and release decisions, yet they often do not specify what evidence supports a claim, what that evidence can establish, or how the claim leads to a decision. We introduce EIO-Agents, an open specification for interoperable AI agent evaluation built on two layers. The Evaluation Intelligence Ontology (EIO) provides the semantic layer through typed evidence, versioned behavioral predicates, evidence contracts, claims, witness rules, proof status, recurrence, and computable derivations for metrics, findings, controls, and PASS, REVIEW, or BLOCK decisions. The Portable Evaluation Record (PER) provides the system of record: a canonical, content addressed representation of one evaluation that preserves the evidence to decision chain and can be re derived, explained, and verified. Scores summarize, juries interpret, and traces record, but none of them define what the evidence means or what it can prove. EIO provides that missing semantic contract, while PER preserves the resulting evaluation as a portable and verifiable system of record. As AI agents assume greater operational responsibility, evaluation must become more than a collection of scores and verdicts; it must become an accountable artifact whose meaning, evidence, limitations, and decisions can be independently checked.

</details>


### [80] [BluffJAX: Adversarial Imperfect Information Games in JAX](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07686)
*Aryaman Reddi,Jan Peters,Carlo D'Eramo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: BluffJAX 在 JAX 上实现了一套高吞吐、可扩展的非完美信息博弈环境，包含传统与未充分研究的博弈，能在 GPU 上实现数亿样本/秒的仿真并提供基线算法评测，便于后续研究比较与发展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前研究缺乏种类丰富且对 GPU 友好的非完美信息博弈基准；通过提供多样化的游戏机制和难度，旨在引入新的挑战并推动博弈论方法在强化学习中的研究方向。

Method: 在 JAX 中实现了若干规范化的博弈环境（如 Texas Hold'Em、Kuhn Poker 以及较少研究的 Bluff、Stud Poker、Kemps），针对 GPU 并行与高吞吐量进行了代码与数据布局优化；并在单/多 GPU 上对吞吐量与内存使用进行了基准测试，同时在 JAX 中实现并评测了强化学习、树搜索与解博弈算法以提供基线结果。

Result: 在单/多 GPU 设置下实现了到数亿样本/秒的扩展性，内存占用表现良好，相较于现有 CPU/GPU 库在吞吐与扩展性上具有明显优势；同时提供了 RL、树搜索与解算器在这些环境上的基线性能数据。

Conclusion: BluffJAX 是一个开源的、面向 GPU 加速的非完美信息博弈套件，能够在高并行度下提供极高的仿真吞吐量，为强化学习与博弈论算法的比较与开发提供了可靠基础设施。

Abstract: We introduce BluffJAX: an open-source suite of adversarial imperfect information games in JAX. We provide canonical implementations of games designed for high simulation throughputs and parallelization on GPU accelerators. Our suite consists of well-studied benchmarks such as Texas Hold'Em Poker and Kuhn Poker, as well as games that have not been previously studied in reinforcement learning research, such as Bluff, Stud Poker, and Kemps. We hope that implementing a variety of game mechanics and difficulties will introduce new challenges and foster novel research directions in game-theoretic methods for RL. We benchmark the throughput performance and memory usage of our environments in single and multi-GPU settings, demonstrating scaling of up to hundreds of millions of samples per second, and motivating the usage of BluffJAX over related GPU and CPU-based libraries. We benchmark reinforcement learning, tree search, and game-solving algorithms in JAX in order to provide users with baseline results and facilitate future comparisons.

</details>


### [81] [On the Boundary of Admission Gates: An Injected-Truth Study of Falsification-First Selection in Quantitative Strategy Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07701)
*Tianlun Zheng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 引入随机录取对照的注入真实验证法可以评估门槛的信息价值：门槛在弱信号情形下有效但代价巨大，在强信号时无效；且应基于超额收益而非绝对收益构造门槛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 策略研究需要区分两类问题：找到能盈利的规则，和证明该发现不是搜索运气（search luck）。现有工作多将两者混淆，因而需要统计性入门门槛来阻止过拟合结论，但门槛是否有效以及它的代价尚未定量评估。

Method: 提出并使用“注入真实”（injected-truth）协议：对候选策略施加与门槛同等通过率的随机录取对照组，比较门槛与随机对照的通过情况来判断门槛是否传递信息。实验覆盖合成数据与基于真实市场校准的数据面板。

Result: 在弱信号区间，门槛能显著降低虚假发现率但将策略采纳率降至极低（1–7%）；在强信号区间，门槛并没有带来附加信息；使用绝对收益作为判据会导致所有候选被拒绝。

Conclusion: 引入的结论是：统计入门门槛（admission gates）在弱信号情形下确实能消除虚假的发现，但代价很高（通过率仅1–7%），在强信号情形下没有附加信息价值；另外，当检验基于绝对收益而非超额收益计算时，会静默地拒绝所有候选（包括真实信号）。

Abstract: Strategy research conflates two problems: finding a profitable rule, and establishing that the finding is not search luck. The latter calls for admission gates -- statistical criteria that must be satisfied before a conclusion is adopted -- yet whether gates work, and at what cost, remains untested. We introduce an injected-truth protocol with a random-admission control that adopts at the same rate as the gate; only if the gate beats this control does it carry information rather than merely raise a threshold. Across synthetic and real-calibrated panels, gates eliminate false discoveries in the weak-signal regime but cut adoption to 1--7%, and add nothing when signals are strong. Most importantly, criteria computed on absolute rather than excess returns silently reject every candidate, including true signals. Keywords: multiple testing, backtest overfitting, strategy admission, injected-truth validation, excess returns, false discovery rate

</details>


### [82] [AgentMemGate: Addressing Speculation Contamination in Conversational Assistant Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07707)
*Chirag Sharma,Benjamin Fowlersmith,Karime Maamari*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 通过在写入时对抽取断言分类并拦截推测性信息，AgentMemGate 有效消除了大量记忆污染并显著提升任务性能，但仍受限于字段匹配覆盖度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期对话助手会把用户话语抽取为记忆条目，但把未决的计划或推测当作既成事实写入会造成记忆污染（speculation contamination），现有基准漏测此类问题。

Method: 在 profile-store 写入阶段加入一个分类器（写时门），将抽取的断言分为：推测（speculation）、已完成事件、纠正或其他；把推测性断言暂不写入，并给出后续提升或删除的条件。并构建包含多会话中计划被确认、放弃或未决的数据集用于评估。

Result: 在147个会话的 held-out 集上，基线 Mem0 与 Graphiti 对未决计划分别在35.2%与27.3%情形下错误断言为当前状态。AgentMemGate 在核心基准上将最易受影响的抽取方式的污染率从87.5%降为0，并使任务准确率由65%升至95%；在更难的 held-out 集上，污染率降至3.4%–5.7%，任务准确率提升9–13个百分点。

Conclusion: 提出的 AgentMemGate 能在写入时拦截“推测性”信息，显著降低记忆污染并提升下游任务准确率，但字段匹配仍是主要瓶颈。

Abstract: Conversational AI assistants with long-term memory extract facts from user messages into a store consulted in later conversations. A stated plan can enter that store as fact: a user who might move to Seattle may be recorded as already living there. We call this speculation contamination. Final-state memory benchmarks miss this error because they do not probe intermediate state and include few unresolved speculations. We present AgentMemGate, a write-time gate for profile-store memory that classifies extracted statements as speculation, completed event, correction, or other. Speculations remain outside memory, with conditions governing later promotion or deletion. We also contribute a dataset of multi-session conversations in which plans are confirmed, abandoned, or left unresolved. On our 147-conversation held-out set, Mem0 and Graphiti assert unresolved plans as current state for 35.2% and 27.3% of pending plans. On the core benchmark, AgentMemGate eliminates all observed contamination relative to the identical ungated pipeline (87.5% to zero for the most exposed extraction style) and raises task accuracy from 65% to 95%. On the harder held-out set, gated contamination is 3.4% to 5.7% and task accuracy rises by 9 to 13 percentage points. Our analysis identifies field matching as the main remaining bottleneck: realistic speculations often match no profile field and never reach the gate. We release our datasets, prompts, and evaluation code.

</details>


### [83] [Evidence Before Sampling: Interpretable Implicit Negative Candidate Discovery for Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07708)
*Shreya Rajpal,Sonia Sharma,Swapnil Parekh,Lisa Li,Jeyendran Balakrishnan,Nagaraj Janardhana,Andrew Mattarella-Micke*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出基于符号规则+LLM解释的隐式负样本发现框架，增强负样本证据性与可解释性，在多数据集上提升候选质量与下游PR-AUC。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 显式负反馈常缺失，随机或未经解释的负采样会误标未观测项，影响模型训练与业务可解释性。需要一种可证据化且与业务目标一致的隐式负样本发现方法。

Method: 通过挖掘观测交互模式并将其编码为符号规则，基于支持度、信息量和产品相关性对规则打分并按证据排序，保留规则后由大模型（LLM）结合业务目标与领域知识解释规则，最后将统计证据与解释合并形成报告用于负样本选择。

Result: 在工业B2B场景及五个公开数据集上验证，工业及三个公开集在候选质量评估上比基线呈现更高精度；在工业任务中，使用符号选择的负样本使下游测试集PR-AUC较随机选择提高约12.5%（每正例四个负例）。同时提出把负候选有效性与下游性能分离评估。

Conclusion: 提出的方法能在稀疏、偏斜的推荐场景下，通过基于观测行为的规则发现并解释隐式负样本候选，从而提高负样本质量与下游模型性能并增强可解释性与业务对齐。

Abstract: Recommender systems learn from observed user-item interactions, but explicit negative feedback is often unavailable. Since deep learning models require negative signals for training, negative sampling methods typically treat selected unobserved interactions as negatives. However, a missing interaction does not explain why a user is uninterested in an item or whether there is sufficient evidence to label it negative. This is especially important in business recommendation, where negative signals should be interpretable and aligned with business objectives. We formulate implicit negative candidate discovery to identify unobserved interactions supported by observed customer behavior. We encode these patterns as symbolic rules, score them based on support, informativeness, and product relevance, and rank the retained rules by evidence. An LLM then interprets the retained rules using business objectives and domain knowledge; the interpretations are combined with the statistical evidence in the final report. We evaluate our method in an industrial B2B setting and across five public recommendation datasets. Candidate-quality evaluations in the industrial setting and three public datasets show higher precision than the evaluated baselines, while symbolic selection improves downstream test PR-AUC by 12.5% over random selection with four negatives per positive example in the industrial task. Our results show that negative candidate validity can be evaluated separately from downstream recommendation performance. This distinction enables evidence-based, business-aligned, and explainable negative selection, improving both interpretability and model training in sparse, skewed, real-world recommendation settings.

</details>


### [84] [PERSIST: Who-What-When Memory Across Sessions for Full-Duplex Spoken Dialogue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07725)
*Achira Lin,Siyuan Hou,Wenyi Yu,Xinnian Zhao,Haoyu Niu,Wang Geng,Longshuai Xiao,Shihai Xiao,Mangsuo Zhao,Chao Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PERSIST 通过结构化事件记录与3W联合检索，并复用对话中间表示，实现了对多会话多说话人语音记忆的高准确率与低延迟检索；并在新基准 SpokenTrace 上显著优于对比方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 共享语音助手需要基于历史对话回答关于早期会话的问题或按用户历史调整行为，要求不仅检索语义相似片段，还要识别当前说话人、恢复相关历史状态并区分之后的修改。现有方法在说话人归属、时间状态或延迟方面存在缺陷。

Method: 提出 PERSIST 系统：将跨会话历史结构化为可读的事件记录，使用3W（语义内容、声学说话人身份、时间状态）联合评分进行检索；为实时全双工交互重用对话骨干网络的中间表示，避免对查询音频重编码以大幅降低检索延迟。并引入诊断基准 SpokenTrace，对记忆任务与说话人-查询类型进行解耦评估。

Result: 在 SpokenTrace 基准上，PERSIST 达到85.08%端到端任务准确率；将 all-support EM@3 从 BGE-large 的49.01%提升到82.10%；检索延迟从578.42 ms降至7.03 ms，展现出更好的召回、说话人鉴别和时间状态追踪能力。

Conclusion: PERSIST 通过显式建模“谁（Who）”、“什么（What）”、“何时（When）”并构建可读事件记录，有效解决了多会话、多说话人的语音对话持久化记忆问题，显著提升了回忆、说话人归属与时间状态追踪的性能与检索时延。

Abstract: Modern voice assistants may be shared by multiple users and should be able to answer questions about earlier conversations such as "When did I originally plan to leave?" or adapt their behavior to individual users based on past interactions. This requires more than retrieving a topically similar passage: the assistant must identify the current speaker, recover the relevant past state, and distinguish it from later revisions. We present PERSIST, a persistent memory system for multi-session, multi-speaker spoken dialogue that explicitly models Who, What, and When. PERSIST structures cross-session histories into readable event records and retrieves them with a 3W joint scoring mechanism that combines semantic content, acoustic speaker identity, and temporal state. For real-time full-duplex interaction, PERSIST further reuses intermediate representations from the dialogue backbone, avoiding query-audio re-encoding and reducing retrieval latency from 578.42 ms to 7.03 ms. We also introduce SpokenTrace, a diagnostic benchmark that factorizes evaluation along memory tasks and speaker-query types, exposing failures in recall, speaker attribution, and temporal-state tracking. On SpokenTrace, PERSIST achieves 85.08% end-to-end task accuracy and improves all-support EM@3 from 49.01% with BGE-large to 82.10%.

</details>


### [85] [How Well Do LLMs Reason with Noisy Evidence? An Active Visual Reasoning Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07751)
*Bach Nguyen,Zhaonan Li,Mau Son Nguyen,Sanika Chavan,Nilay Kumar,Hong Anh Nguyen,Khoa Vo,Ben Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: VisualNoiseQA通过将VLM视为随机传感器并用多样本自一致性暴露不确定性，建立了一个自动化、可扩展的测试床来研究文本LLM在噪声视觉反馈下的主动查询与停止策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实世界的推理需要主动从带噪声且不可靠的工具/传感器采集信息，而现有主动推理基准要么假定环境反馈可信，要么没有提供显式、校准的不确定性信号，缺乏研究LLM在证据本身不确定时应如何推理的环境。

Method: 将固定的、现成的视觉大模型(VLM)视为随机视觉传感器；文本LLM通过多次对同一图像查询并收集VLM的不同样本，利用样本间的一致性作为经验不确定性信号来引导后续查询和停止决策。数据集由多源VQA题目自动构造，保留对人类可解但传感器不一致的问题。对若干LLM进行了1000条跨感知、图表理解与知识密集型题目的评估。

Result: 构建了一个自动且可扩展的数据集（VisualNoiseQA），提供了一个受控的试验台来评估不同LLM如何利用不确定性信号进行鲁棒推理；并通过基准实验说明了传感器不一致性对推理流程与性能的影响。

Conclusion: 本文提出了VisualNoiseQA，一个用于研究在有噪声视觉反馈下主动推理的基准，展示了通过对同一视觉传感器多次采样并用自一致性作为不确定性信号，文本LLM可以在迭代查询中决定下一步如何提问及何时停止。

Abstract: Real-world reasoning rarely reduces to static question answering: agents must actively gather information from tools and sensors that are often noisy and unreliable. Yet most existing active reasoning benchmarks assume that environmental feedback is trustworthy, or introduce noise without exposing an explicit, calibrated uncertainty signal, leaving open how LLMs should reason when the evidence itself is uncertain. We introduce VisualNoiseQA, a novel benchmark for active reasoning under noisy visual feedback. A text-only LLM must solve VQA problems by iteratively querying a fixed, off-the-shelf VLM treated as a stochastic visual sensor. For each query, we draw multiple samples and expose an empirical uncertainty signal via self-consistency, enabling the reasoner to probe from different angles and decide what to ask next and when to stop. Our construction is automatic and scalable: starting from diverse VQA sources and two noisy VLMs, we retain only questions where the sensor is inconsistent yet human-solvable. We evaluate multiple LLM reasoners on 1,000 instances spanning perception, chart understanding, and knowledge-intensive reasoning. VisualNoiseQA thus provides a controlled playground to study how different LLMs exploit uncertainty signals for robust reasoning.

</details>


### [86] [ST-Bench: A Spatial-Temporal Benchmark for Multi-Agent System Generation on Scientific Research Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07763)
*Qi Cheng,Rongchao Dong,Shengyu Chen,Licheng Liu,Dan Lu,Zhengzhang Chen,Wei Cheng,Yiqun Xie,Haifeng Chen,Xiaowei Jia,Haoyu Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ST-Bench（2067条查询）显示多智能体系统通过提高覆盖率显著提升科学数据分析表现（最高近3×），但主要成本是更长的推理时间；在生成现实数值时质量并未显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作显示MAS在带有可执行测试的任务（编码、数学、问答）中优于单智能体，但尚不清楚这种优势能否迁移到真实科学数据分析——那里没有简单的二元成功信号，输出更复杂且需要数值可信度与领域一致性。ST-Bench旨在回答MAS是否在此类任务上更好、优劣幅度及其额外成本。

Method: 构建ST-Bench：从已发表的地球科学研究（涵盖水文、农业、湿地甲烷）选取100个数据科学任务，扩展成2067条以其它已发表研究为依据的查询，并经领域专家验证。在同一GPT-5骨干下，比较5种近期MAS生成方法（2种训练协议）与单智能体基线，使用复合得分与推理时间等指标评估性能。

Result: 9/10个MAS配置优于最便宜的单智能体基线，最强配置复合得分近3倍。性能提升主要来自覆盖率（更多查询产生现实数值），而在产出现实数值的情况下，数值质量与单智能体相当。最强配置的推理时间约为单智能体的4倍；存在一种成本更低的工作流，在不到2倍开销下获得大部分收益。总体结论为：MAS专业化对科学数据分析有可测益处，但不是普遍的、无条件的改进。

Conclusion: MAS（基于大型模型的多智能体系统）在科学数据分析任务上确实带来可测的收益，但这种收益是有条件的：主要来自覆盖率提升（更多查询产出‘现实的’数值结果），而在产出真实数值的条件下，其数值质量与单智能体相当。收益伴随显著的额外推理时间成本（最强配置≈4倍，经济配置＜2倍）。

Abstract: The rapid progress of LLM-based multi-agent systems (MAS) has shown that they largely outperform single agents on coding, math, and QA tasks, where executable tests provide a binary success signal. Whether this advantage transfers to real scientific data analysis remains untested. We introduce ST-Bench, a benchmark designed to answer two questions: whether MAS outperform single agents on complex scientific data analysis tasks, and if so, by how much and at what additional cost. ST-Bench contains 100 data science tasks adapted from published Earth science studies across hydrology, agriculture, and wetland methane research, expanded into 2,067 queries grounded in additional published studies and validated by domain experts. Using ST-Bench, we evaluate five recent MAS generation methods under two training protocols, against single-agent baselines on the same GPT-5 backbone. Nine of the ten MAS configurations exceed the cheapest single-agent baseline, with the strongest reaching nearly three times its composite score. This gain is primarily attributable to coverage: trained workflows produce realistic numerical metrics on a larger fraction of queries, while the quality of those metrics, conditional on producing realistic output, is comparable to that of the single-agent baseline. The strongest configuration requires approximately four times the single-agent inference time, whereas a more economical workflow captures the majority of the benefit at less than twice the cost. MAS specialization confers measurable benefit on scientific data analysis, but the benefit is conditional rather than universal.

</details>


### [87] [OTel: Open Telco AI Datasets, Benchmarks, and Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07766)
*Farbod Tavakkoli,Gregory Diamos,Kenneth Church,David Kanter,Mark Austin,Imtiaz Karim,Mirza Masfiqur Rahman,Merouane Abdelkader Debbah,Zeinab Nezami,Ali Maatouk,Leandros Tassiulas,Rex Ying,Nick Sorros,Louis Powell,Nikolaos Vasiloglou,Ashish Vaswani,Somanshu Singla,Adarsh Chaluvaraju*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: OTel is a reproducible open resource that post-trains open-weight models on telecom-specific data, improves retrieval/rerank/LM performance substantially, and invites community extension.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Unify prior open telecom datasets and benchmarks, lower the barrier to building context-grounded telecom LLMs, and provide data and models for retrieval, reranking, instruction tuning, and safety/abstention.

Method: Start from open-weight models, post-train them on OTel-derived datasets using an open training recipe, produce 30 full-parameter baselines across embeddings, rerankers, and LLMs, and evaluate on held-out OTel partitions.

Result: OTel post-training yields embedding retrieval NDCG@10=93.1%, reranking MRR@10=0.947, language-model correctness=87.8%; the release has large community uptake (16M+ downloads, 157+ media mentions) and includes documented sources, evaluation partitions, and open training recipes.

Conclusion: Open Telco (OTel) provides a unified, reproducible open telecom AI resource and demonstrably improves post-trained embedding, reranking, and language models for telecom tasks, achieving high evaluation metrics and strong community adoption.

Abstract: We present Open Telco (OTel), an open telecom AI resource that releases derived telecom datasets for retrieval, reranking, instruction tuning, and safety/abstention, together with 30 full-parameter post-trained baselines spanning 10 embedding models, 3 rerankers, and 17 language models. The community has already engaged substantially with the resource: as of May 3, 2026, the released models have been downloaded over 16 million times and the project has received 157+ pieces of media coverage worldwide. Building on prior open telecom datasets and benchmarks, OTel provides documented telecom data sources, held-out evaluation partitions, trained embedding models, rerankers, context-grounded LLMs, and safety/abstention data in one unified resource. Each baseline starts from an open-weight model and is post-trained on OTel-derived data using an open training recipe, then evaluated on held-out OTel evaluation partitions. OTel post-training improves performance across all three model families: embedding retrieval reaches 93.1% NDCG@10, reranking reaches 0.947 MRR@10, and language-model correctness reaches 87.8%. We release OTel as a reproducible starting point and invite the community to expand the data, improve embedding and reranking models, and build stronger context-grounded telecom LLMs.

</details>


### [88] [Quantization Effects on Tool-Failure Recovery Vary Across Prompts and Evaluation Designs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07781)
*Yuhe Hu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 评估设计（提示、评分目标、解析严格度）能左右8-bit/4-bit模型在工具故障恢复比较的结论。为稳健判断应在匹配任务上比较、报告全流程成功率、声明评分策略并在任务层面量化不确定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 后训练量化（post-training quantization）能显著降低部署成本，但其对模型在运行时从工具临时故障中恢复能力的影响尚不明确。作者怀疑评估设计本身会主导观测结果，因此系统性比较不同量化级别、提示和评分策略的相互作用。

Method: 对比了两类模型及量化级别：Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct 与 Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct 的 8-bit 与 4-bit 变体；在20个确定性工具使用任务和5个不同提示上注入临时工具故障，考察模型恢复能力。使用多种评价目标（仅对各自‘干净通过’的任务计分、对相同任务计分以及全流程点估计）并测试不同的解析严格度（执行器宽松度）以量化评分策略对结论的影响。

Result: 结果显示比较方向会随提示与评价目标翻转：在相同提示下，若只看两者都在无故障时完成的任务，Llama的8v4差异在0到+20.2个百分点之间，Qwen在-50.0到+35.0之间。全流程点估计在五个提示下均偏好8-bit Llama，而Qwen的偏好随提示变化。对同一提示，按各自无故障任务计分可使Llama 4-bit领先17.5点；对相同任务计分则无差异；全流程计分则8-bit领先28.3点。将日志用严格输出解析重评分（8-bit Llama 在该提示下更多违反严格解析）会把 +28.3 变成 -15.0，而对 Qwen 影响不大。这些发现说明评估选择能显著改变结论。

Conclusion: 论文结论是：量化后（8-bit vs 4-bit）语言模型在从临时工具故障中恢复的表现高度依赖于评估设计——提示（prompt）、评价目标（evaluation target）和执行器宽松度（executor leniency）都会改变比较方向。因此单一提示、单一任务筛选集或单一评分策略不能得出稳定结论。作者建议在比较时使用匹配任务、报告全流程成功率以支撑部署决策、明确评分策略，并在任务维度上量化不确定性。

Abstract: Post-training quantization reduces the cost of deploying language-model agents, but its effect on recovery from temporary tool failures can depend on how recovery is evaluated. We compare 8-bit and 4-bit variants of Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct and Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct on twenty deterministic tool-use tasks and five prompts. The 8-bit-4-bit recovery comparison changes direction across prompts and evaluation targets. On tasks that both variants complete without faults under the same prompt, the difference ranges from 0 to +20.2 percentage points for Llama and from -50.0 to +35.0 points for Qwen. Full-pipeline point estimates favor 8-bit Llama under all five prompts, whereas the Qwen comparison changes direction across prompts. The evaluation target can also reverse the result. For Llama under one prompt, scoring each variant only on its own clean-passing tasks favors 4-bit by 17.5 points; scoring the same tasks for both variants gives no difference, while scoring the full pipeline favors 8-bit by 28.3 points. Executor leniency is a third such choice. Rescoring the same logs with strict output parsing, which 8-bit Llama violates far more often than 4-bit Llama under that prompt, turns that +28.3 into -15.0 while leaving Qwen essentially unchanged. These findings show that one prompt, one screened task set, and one scoring policy do not establish a stable conclusion about quantized-agent robustness. Evaluations should compare variants on matched tasks, report full-pipeline success for deployment decisions, state the scoring policy, and quantify uncertainty across tasks rather than injected fault sites.

</details>


### [89] [Persistent Memory in Multi-Agent LLM Inference: What It Costs, What It Buys, and When You Can Tell](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07782)
*Hochan Son,Kyungdoe Han,Jaehan Koh,Xiaowu Dai,Wenlu Xu,Guang Cheng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 分解式能显著减小每次调用的 KV 峰值占用；持久化记忆增加了很小的缓存开销但并未改善准确率；单题独立基准使持久化检索无信息可用，需特别的消融设计与检测流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估把长上下文推理分解并跨多个协作代理分摊是否能将每次调用的活动 KV 缓存量限定在较低水平，以及评估为代理添加持久化记忆层（保存与回忆推理痕迹）是否真实带来精度提升。

Method: 在一个三层代理架构上，对比三种方案：分解式（decomposition）、单次直接推理（single-pass）和检索增强（retrieval-augmented）。度量每次调用的峰值 KV 工作集和准确率；在八对受控数据集上，每臂 n=100，应用了四次测量修正以纠正偏差并提升可分辨性。

Result: 分解式将峰值 KV 工作集降到 14.3 MiB/查询，而单次和检索增强基线分别是 35.5 和 35.3 MiB。持久化层额外增加峰值缓存 +0.368 MiB（95% CI [+0.167, +0.590]），但在准确率上没有可检测变化（差值 +0.015，95% CI [-0.011, +0.046]）。作者判定零效应是基准与实验设计的结构性不匹配导致的。

Conclusion: 分解式推理在峰值 KV 工作集上有显著优势，而持久化推理痕迹（persistent tier）并未带来可检测的精度提升，反而带来微小的峰值缓存增加。作者认为对单题基准（每题独立证据且需要在条件间重置存储痕迹）的结构性限制导致回忆没有信息性可检索，因此出现零效应。

Abstract: Decomposing long-context inference across cooperating agents bounds the active KV cache per call rather than total evidence, which matters when KV-cache memory binds. Many such systems add a persistent tier storing and recalling reasoning traces, usually validated by an ablation reporting an accuracy gain. We measure both on one three-tier agent architecture. Decomposition delivers: peak KV working set of 14.3 MiB per query against 35.5 and 35.3 MiB for single-pass and retrieval-augmented baselines. The persistent tier does not: across eight controlled dataset pairs at n=100 per arm it costs +0.368 MiB [+0.167, +0.590] of peak cache and produces no detectable accuracy change (+0.015, 95% CI [-0.011, +0.046]). We argue the null is structural: single-question benchmarks supply each item with its own evidence and score it independently, and correctness requires resetting stored traces between conditions, so recall has nothing informative to retrieve. Reaching it took four measurement corrections -- three inflating the apparent benefit, the fourth making an effect that size look resolvable -- none visible in the results table. We give the conditions an agent-memory ablation must satisfy and detection procedures that need no knowledge of the specific defect.

</details>


### [90] [Attacca: Goal-Directed Control under State Continuity for Long-Horizon Embodied Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07785)
*Gyusik Seo,Jaehong Yoon*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 通过语境解耦的目标采样、视图内目标掩码预测和行为阶段条件化，在完整搜索到交互的轨迹上训练，Attacca显著提高了在Minecraft中面向长时任务的视觉目标条件化策略性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉目标条件策略通常在目标已在视野内的孤立交互上评估，不能覆盖连续长时任务中：位置/朝向改变、世界被修改、下一目标可能不在当前视野内等问题，导致策略在继续执行下一个任务时难以找到并对齐目标。

Method: 1) 语境解耦目标采样：将每个示例与来自其他世界但类别兼容的掩码目标图像配对，去除直接的场景和位姿对应；2) 目标掩码预测头：为当前观察学习密集的目标可视化落地（grounding），作为对动作模仿之外的辅助监督；3) 行为阶段条件化：在策略输入中加入阶段信号以区分Search、Approach、Interact并随执行进展调整控制。训练在完整的search-to-interact轨迹上进行。

Result: 在Minecraft的多种短时和长时任务上，Attacca取得39.0-47.5%的clean success，比最强基线高1.7-2.4倍；在长时任务上分别达到54%、30%和28%的完成率，单项最多带来7倍提升。

Conclusion: Attacca通过在完整的search-to-interact轨迹上训练视觉目标条件化策略，结合语境解耦的目标采样、目标掩码预测以实现视图内的密集定位监督，以及行为阶段条件化（Search/Approach/Interact），显著提升了在Minecraft中短时和长时任务的成功率和任务完成率。

Abstract: A central capability of embodied agents is to accomplish complex objectives through sequences of interdependent tasks. Yet existing visual goal-conditioned policies underlying these agents are typically evaluated on isolated interactions where the target is already visible, and thus do not capture the conditions that arise during continuous long-horizon task execution. In such settings, each task begins from the state left by the previous one: the agent may end at a different position and orientation, the world may have been modified, and the next interaction target may lie outside the current field of view. As a result, agents relying on such policies may struggle to proceed to the next task when they cannot ground their target in the current observation. To address this challenge, we propose Attacca, a new approach that trains visual goal-conditioned policies on complete search-to-interact trajectories using goal images decoupled from the execution environment. Attacca uses context-decoupled goal sampling to pair each demonstration with a class-compatible masked goal image from another world, removing direct scene and pose correspondence. It learns dense current-view grounding through a target-mask prediction head, providing auxiliary supervision beyond action imitation. We further introduce behavioral-phase conditioning that teaches the policy to distinguish Search, Approach, and Interact stages and adapt its control as execution progresses. We evaluate Attacca on multiple short- and long-horizon embodied tasks in Minecraft. Our method achieves 39.0-47.5% clean success, improving over the strongest baseline by 1.7-2.4x. On long-horizon tasks, it attains 54%, 30%, and 28% completion, yielding up to a 7x improvement.

</details>


### [91] [OOPMAS: Object-Oriented Multi-Agent Systems for Query-Level Workflow Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07787)
*Qi Cheng,Shengyu Chen,Wei Cheng,Yiqun Xie,Xiaowei Jia,Haoyu Wang,Haifeng Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: OOPMAS：按查询生成面向对象代理与可执行工作流，配合动态技能库，无需训练即可在混合任务上显著优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多智能体系统设计多在任务级别生成固定工作流，忽视了单次查询间难度差异与现实混合任务负载，导致在实际场景下泛化与效率受限。

Method: 提出 OOPMAS 框架：将代理表示为具有角色、工具与持久状态的面向对象类定义；将工作流表达为针对这些对象的可执行 main 函数；引入动态技能库，在多轮优化中从执行反馈中积累结构化经验以实现 in-context 改进，无需梯度或微调。

Result: 在涵盖代码、数学与问答的混合任务基准上，OOPMAS 达到 89.6% 准确率，比最强基线高出 18.1 个百分点；在对四个 LLM 骨干模型的替换测试中表现一致随模型规模提升，最强模型达 92.4%。

Conclusion: OOPMAS 在无需训练的前提下，通过按查询粒度自动生成面向对象的代理集合与可执行的协调工作流，并结合动态技能库进行基于执行反馈的上下文改进，从而显著提升多任务场景下的性能与适应性。

Abstract: Multi-agent systems (MAS) powered by large language models have shown strong performance across code generation, mathematical reasoning, and question answering. However, existing methods for automating MAS design mostly operate at the task level, producing a single fixed workflow per benchmark that is applied uniformly to all queries. This assumption fails under realistic conditions. Query difficulty varies widely within a task, and real-world workloads mix heterogeneous task types. We introduce OOPMAS, a training-free framework that generates both the agent set and the coordination workflow at the granularity of individual queries. Agents are represented as object-oriented class definitions with dedicated roles, tools, and persistent state, and workflows are expressed as executable main functions over these agent objects. A dynamic skill library accumulates structured lessons from execution feedback across optimization rounds, enabling in-context improvement without any gradient updates or fine-tuning. On a mixed-task benchmark of queries spanning code, math, and QA, OOPMAS achieves 89.6% accuracy, outperforming the strongest baseline by 18.1 percentage points. A model-swap study across four LLM backbones shows consistent scaling, reaching 92.4% with the strongest model.

</details>


### [92] [Illusory Pattern Perception Drives Spurious Inference in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07791)
*Peihua Mai,Zhuoyan Shao,Xinbao Qiao,Meng Zhang,Xinyue Zhou,Yan Pang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: This first systematic study shows LLMs can suffer from illusory pattern perception—stronger than humans—driven by representation-level properties identified via SAEs, which risks systematic reasoning errors in applications.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: To determine whether illusory pattern perception—humans’ tendency to infer structure from random data—also appears in LLMs, and to quantify its magnitude, forms, and internal mechanisms because such perception could produce systematic reasoning failures in applications.

Method: Adapted three classic psychological paradigms into LLM tasks with direct empirical human–model comparisons; analyzed model internal representations with a Sparse Autoencoder (SAE)–based feature interpretability framework to link behavioral patterns to representation properties.

Result: Models often exhibit stronger illusory perception than humans: they over-associate frequent positive attributes with majority groups/large organizations and construct causal narratives from ambiguous events. SAE analyses indicate that holistic frequency perception and an analytic cognitive orientation in internal features correlate with these illusory behaviors.

Conclusion: LLMs display a cognitive-like illusory pattern perception that in many cases is stronger than humans’, leading to systematic errors in attribute association and causal inference; this phenomenon is tied to internal representation properties and has implications for downstream reliability and mitigation.

Abstract: Illusory pattern perception is a well-documented human cognitive tendency to infer meaningful relationships in data that is actually random. Such a tendency, often described as "connecting the dots" where none exist, can result in systematic reasoning errors. This paper investigates whether Large Language Models (LLMs) exhibit such perceptual tendencies, which can lead to systematic errors in downstream applications. To our knowledge, this work presents the first systematic study of illusory pattern perception in LLMs, adapting classic psychological paradigms to three tasks with direct empirical comparison to human behaviors. We find that LLMs frequently exhibit stronger illusory pattern perception than humans. In particular, models tend to over-associate frequent positive attributes with majority groups or large organizations, and show increased tendencies to construct causal narratives from ambiguous events. To uncover the mechanism behind these behaviors, we develop a feature interpretability framework based on Sparse Autoencoders (SAEs) to analyze internal representations. Our results reveal that holistic frequency perception and analytic cognitive orientation are linked to the emergence of illusory perceptions. These findings highlight a previously underexplored cognitive-like illusion that may affect the reliability of LLM reasoning. Code available at https://github.com/NusIoraPrivacy/illusory.

</details>


### [93] [Thin Evidence, Thick Priors: How Language Models Substitute Identity for Missing Financial Facts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07798)
*Saanvi Khetan,Sankar Balasubramanian*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 在部分或无财务披露时，LLM的理财建议高度受“身份”影响并常常编造未披露的财务信息，提示需要按用户真实披露水平进行审计和部署防护。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估在真实用户常发生的“部分披露/不披露”情况下，LLM理财建议对人格/身份信息的敏感性与潜在偏差来源，指导审计与部署策略。

Method: 以固定的100个财务配置为基线，构造138种人格（persona）和7种披露条件（从完整披露到无财务事实），共生成96,600条提示，向Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct询问股权配置并用两类聚类自举方法估计差异与显著性；并结合网络内部可解码性分析与层级消融验证表征。

Result: 完整披露时，同一财务下不同人格间的平均差距为4.78个百分点；无披露时上升到10.34个百分点（比值约2.16，95%区间1.69–2.79）；身份对建议变异的解释率从完整披露时约5%增至无披露时约96%；家庭规模是唯一随着证据撤回而影响力稳步上升的属性；模型在无事实时会编造收入/债务/储蓄，并且这些虚构项对大家庭更不利；性别可以在每层线性解码，但在消融若干层对应方向后，总体“身份摆动”并未减小。

Conclusion: 当用户不披露财务细节时，LLM在给出理财建议时会大幅依赖“身份”信息，从而导致同一财务状况下不同人格得到显著不同的股票配比建议。

Abstract: People increasingly ask large language models what to do with their money, yet seldom describe their finances in full. This paper asks what a model does with the gap. Holding finances fixed and changing only who the investor is said to be, we grade the financial evidence in the prompt from eight facts to none and measure how far the recommended equity allocation moves. Across 96,600 prompts to Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct, built from 100 financial profiles, 138 personas and seven disclosure conditions, the average gap between two personas with identical finances rises from 4.78 percentage points at full disclosure to 10.34 points with no financial facts. A two-way cluster bootstrap counting duplicated prompts once places the ratio at 2.16 (95% interval 1.69 to 2.79), and the rise is already 1.69-fold with a single fact left. Identity explains 5% of within-profile variation in advice at full disclosure and 96% with no disclosure. Household size is the only attribute whose influence grows reliably as evidence is withdrawn. Once standard errors are clustered on the persona, the unit to which identity was assigned, most attribute-specific interactions reported in the conference version lose significance, and gender instead appears as a small standing gap that full disclosure does not close. Stating risk appetite alone brings the swing into the range seen with two to seven generic facts. With no facts, the model's one-line rationale cites incomes, debts and savings it was never told, and these invented finances turn adverse more often for larger households. Inside the network, gender is linearly decodable at every layer, and ablating the gender direction at five layers leaves the aggregate identity swing unchanged.
