Advisory systems built on such models should be audited at the disclosure levels users actually reach, and judged across the whole identity space rather than one attribute at a time.

</details>


### [94] [ThinkFuse: Trajectory-Aware Test-Time Fusion for Small Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07803)
*Myunghoon Kang,Jungseob Lee,Jaehyung Seo,Heuiseok Lim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ThinkFuse 是一种训练-free 的测试时选择性融合框架，通过比较段级与轨迹级不确定性来识别并修正不稳定的推理片段，能提升小型推理模型的准确性与生成效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小型推理模型通过长链式思维提高复杂推理能力，但一旦进入错误轨迹难以自我恢复；现有基于局部融合信号的方法易被瞬时不确定性波动误导并可能加强不稳定路径，需一种更稳定且高效的测试时干预策略。

Method: 在测试时对生成的链式思维按段切分，计算段级不确定性变化并与整个轨迹的不确定性趋势比较，基于不稳定点触发对辅助模型的推理路径进行融合（即将辅助路径插入或替换主轨迹的片段），该流程为训练无关的在线干预机制，力求最小化触发次数与生成长度。

Result: 在数学和知识密集型推理基准上优于基线方法，在不同模型族组合下保持一致性提升，即使使用更小的主模型也稳健；分析显示 ThinkFuse 触发次数更少、生成的 tokens 更少，证明了选择性触发的效率。

Conclusion: ThinkFuse 能在不需再训练的情形下，通过对推理轨迹的段级与轨迹级不确定性比较，选择性地将辅助推理路径融合进主模型生成的链式思维，从而有效纠正小型推理模型在走入错误路径时的失误，且在算力与生成长度上更高效。

Abstract: Small reasoning models (SRMs) have shown strong performance on complex reasoning tasks by generating extended chain-of-thought trajectories, but they often fail to recover once their reasoning enters an erroneous path. Existing test-time fusion methods rely on local fusion signals to determine when to trigger fusion, which can be misled by transient uncertainty fluctuations and may reinforce unstable reasoning trajectories. We propose ThinkFuse, a training-free test-time fusion framework that selectively intervenes in unreliable reasoning segments. ThinkFuse compares segment-level uncertainty shifts with trajectory-level uncertainty trends to identify unstable reasoning points and fuse auxiliary reasoning paths into the primary model's trajectory. Extensive experiments demonstrate that ThinkFuse outperforms baselines on mathematical and knowledge-intensive reasoning benchmarks, with consistent gains across model-family combinations, and remains robust with a smaller primary model. Our analysis shows that ThinkFuse requires fewer fusion triggers and generates fewer tokens, highlighting the efficiency of selective triggering. Our code is available at https://github.com/js-lee-AI/ThinkFuse.

</details>


### [95] [Do I Need the Cloud? Uncertainty-Aware Step-Level Handoff for Small Language Model Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07816)
*Abolfazl Younesi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Small language models (SLMs) are attractive as local agent controllers because they reduce remote inference, latency, and deployment footprint, yet structured tool errors can cause an agent step to fail. Existing routers typically select a model once per query. However, agents expose sequential decision points whose difficulty dynamically changes based on intermediate observations. We propose STEPGATE, an uncertainty-aware handoff framework that scores each local SLM action and selectively escalates challenging steps to a stronger model. On a 52-task held-out single-step BFCL-derived test split, the Qwen2.5-1.5B/7B pair attains 82.7% task success with 30.8% escalation, versus 67.3% local-only and 75.4% random escalation (which uses 33.8% escalation). In a separate multi-turn evaluation, STEPGATE achieves 69.0% trajectory success and 84.0% action success using only 30.0% cloud actions, compared with 48.0%/70.5% local-only, 60.0%/78.2% random escalation, and 57.0%/77.1% query-level routing (strong-only achieves 82.0% trajectory success at 100% cloud actions). These results suggest that step-level escalation recovers a large share of the performance gap to the stronger Qwen2.5-7B backend at a matched cloud-action rate while transmitting fewer tokens remotely. However, our evaluation is limited to one model family, a single stronger backend, and scripted tasks. Furthermore, the test sets are small, multi-turn comparisons rely on paired intervals and statistical tests, and our risk tiers serve as research annotations rather than formal safety guarantees.

</details>


### [96] [Agentic Semantic Sensing for Resource-Adaptive AI-RAN](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07829)
*Zhongqin Wang,Xiaoqi Zhang,Nan Yang,Kai Wu,J. Andrew Zhang,Y. Jay Guo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出闭环 Agentic SemS：用条件因果 Transformer、KV 缓存与语义收益网络在受限配置集内动态选择感知配置与早退，从而显著降低感知成本且保持高任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语义感知通常为开环（感知配置与调度固定），无法基于实时任务证据动态调整；在受通信/资源约束的 AI-RAN 场景下，需要一个能在可通信配置集内自适应选择感知策略与终止时刻的闭环方案以提高资源效率。

Method: 设计了 profile-conditioned causal Transformer 用于从流式观测中递增更新语义信念；引入 key-value 缓存机制以在切换感知配置时高效重用历史状态而无需重处理完整历史；提出 semantic utility network 估计在每个可行配置下采集下一观测块的任务收益并折算感知成本，通过计算 continuation utilities 联合用于下一个配置选择与语义早退决策。同时将期望语义增益与条件互信息关联，提供价值-信息的解释。

Result: 在 Widar3.0 数据集、六种模拟感知配置上，与始终用 High 全序列基线相比，Agentic 模式在达到可比语义效用点时将累计归一化感知成本降低了 25.33%，同时取得 85.79% 的 Macro-F1；在相同效用检查点下，启用语义早退相比仅自适应感知进一步降低 12.35% 的成本，代价是 Macro-F1 下降 0.97 个百分点。

Conclusion: Agentic SemS 提出了一种闭环、面向任务的感知控制框架，使 AI-RAN 中的语义感知能在允许的通信配置集内动态选择感知配置与时长，从而在大幅降低感知资源消耗的同时维持较高的任务级性能。

Abstract: Semantic sensing (SemS) acquires task-relevant information rather than reconstructing complete physical information. Existing SemS formulations typically operate open loop: sensing configurations and observation schedules are fixed before inference and cannot respond to evolving task-level evidence. We propose Agentic SemS, a closed-loop framework for AI-enabled radio access networks (AI-RANs) that controls sensing within a communication-feasible profile set. A profile-conditioned causal Transformer updates the semantic belief from streaming observations, while key-value caching enables efficient state updates across profile changes without repeatedly processing the complete history. A semantic utility network estimates the task-level benefit of acquiring the next observation block under each feasible profile after accounting for sensing cost. The resulting continuation utilities jointly support next-profile selection and semantic early exit, adapting sensing configuration and duration to evolving evidence. The expected semantic gain is further related to conditional mutual information, providing a value-of-information interpretation of continued online sensing. Experiments on Widar3.0 with six emulated sensing profiles show that, in comparison with full-sequence High, the resource-efficient Agentic setting reduces normalized cumulative sensing cost by 25.33% while achieving 85.79% Macro-F1. At the same utility checkpoint, semantic early exit provides a further 12.35% cost reduction over adaptive sensing without early exit, with a 0.97-percentage-point Macro-F1 decrease.

</details>


### [97] [DHCG: Dynamic Construction of Hierarchical Collaboration Graphs for LLM-Based Multi-Agent Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07835)
*Jie Ren,Jiakang Yuan,Chenyu Huang,Hezeer Ma,Jiayuan Fan,Tao Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出DHCG：基于POMDP的动态分层协作图框架，通过Planner/Worker/Generator三模块与动作感知偏好优化，在执行中可动态生成并扩展互补角色与路由，实现在多任务上的显著性能提升与良好泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MAS在组件组合、依赖对齐和规模灵活性方面受限，难以在执行中根据实际推理需求自适应调整系统结构与规模。

Method: 将MAS视作POMDP，协同三个模块（Planner、Worker、Generator）逐步从零构建动态分层协作图；Planner基于执行反馈生成互补角色并选择性路由信息，可提前终止或扩展图；引入“动作感知偏好优化”训练Planner以提升决策质量。

Result: 在代码生成、数学推理和领域专属推理基准上，DHCG平均性能领先单智能体基线13.06点，较静态和其他动态MAS基线提升2.77–8.02点；并表现出对不同Planner骨干与未见Worker模型的泛化能力。

Conclusion: DHCG通过将多智能体系统设计重构为部分可观测马尔可夫决策过程，提出了一种能在推理过程中动态确定组合与规模的框架，从而在多项任务上取得SOTA级别的平均性能提升。

Abstract: LLM-based multi-agent systems (MAS) have demonstrated strong capabilities in solving complex problems across diverse domains. Recently, the dynamic orchestration of agent systems has become an important research direction. However, existing methods suffer from limited composition, misaligned dependencies, and inflexible scale, restricting their ability to adapt to reasoning requirements during execution. To address these limitations, we reframe MAS design as a partially observable Markov decision process, in which both the composition and scale of the MAS are dynamically determined. We propose DHCG, a novel framework that coordinates three modules (Planner, Worker, and Generator) to progressively construct a dynamic hierarchical collaboration graph from scratch based on the query and evolving execution feedback. At each step, guided by feedback, the Planner generates a set of distinct and complementary roles tailored to the current reasoning needs and selectively routes relevant information to each role. It can also finalize the hierarchical collaboration graph early or progressively expand it when additional reasoning is required. We further introduce action-aware preference optimization to train the Planner to make more effective decisions when constructing hierarchical collaboration graphs. We systematically evaluate DHCG across code generation, mathematical reasoning, and domain-specific reasoning benchmarks. DHCG achieves state-of-the-art average performance among the compared methods, improving over the single-agent baseline by 13.06 points and outperforming both static and dynamic MAS baselines by 2.77-8.02 points. Additional experiments further demonstrate its generalization across different Planner backbones and unseen Worker models.

</details>


### [98] [RA-MoWE: Workflow-Affinity Embeddings for Query Clustering and Agentic Workflow Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07851)
*Qi Cheng,Shengyu Chen,Wei Cheng,Yiqun Xie,Haoyu Wang,Haifeng Chen,Xiaowei Jia*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: RA-MoWE通过构建和预测“工作流亲和力”嵌入来聚类查询并生成可复用的专家工作流，从而在提高解题效果的同时降低推理开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 整体优化单一工作流会忽略查询间所需推理策略的差异，而为每个查询单独搜索工作流又昂贵。需要在通用性与个性化之间取得折衷，既能重用工作流又能满足不同查询的策略需求。

Method: 为每个查询使用一组参考工作流执行并记录其解题表现，构造工作流亲和力嵌入；基于嵌入对查询聚类；用簇内查询与平均嵌入初始化并通过执行反馈细化专用工作流；训练嵌入编码器从查询文本预测嵌入，以便新查询无需先运行参考工作流即可选择合适专家。

Result: 在300条测试查询（来自数学、科学、编程四个基准）上，RA-MoWE相比在参考工作流间直接选择，平均任务得分提升4.04个百分点，并在推理时减少27.7%的语言模型调用。

Conclusion: RA-MoWE通过对查询进行基于工作流亲和力的嵌入与聚类，生成并复用针对簇的专家级工作流，从而在性能提升与推理成本之间取得更好平衡。

Abstract: Agentic workflows enable large language models (LLMs) to solve complex tasks by coordinating reasoning, tool use, and verification. However, a workflow optimized for an entire task collection can overlook differences in the reasoning strategies that individual queries need, while searching for a new workflow for every query repeats costly optimization. To address this tradeoff, we introduce RA-MoWE, a framework that uses workflow-affinity embeddings to cluster queries and guide the generation of reusable expert workflows. Each embedding records how well a fixed set of reference workflows solves a query, revealing similarities in which reasoning strategies are effective. RA-MoWE uses each cluster's queries and average embedding to initialize and refine a specialized workflow through execution feedback. An embedding encoder predicts these embeddings from query text, allowing new queries to select a generated expert without first executing the reference workflows. On a 300-query test set drawn from four benchmarks spanning mathematics, science, and programming, RA-MoWE improves average task score by 4.04 percentage points over selecting among the reference workflows, while using 27.7% fewer language-model calls at inference.

</details>


### [99] [WorkflowOps: Learning Agent Collaboration Priors for Multi-Agent Workflow Orchestration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07860)
*Qi Cheng,Shengyu Chen,Wei Cheng,Zhengzhang Chen,Xiaowei Jia,Haoyu Wang,Haifeng Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: WorkflowOps以协作先验矩阵、按需代理生成与分层语义匹配三合一，提升多代理工作流成功率并显著节省LLM调用成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前多代理编排通常无记忆性——每次任务都从零分解与分配，浪费先验协作信息；需要一种能从历史工作流中学习并复用协作模式，同时能扩展并减少昂贵LLM调用的方法。

Method: 引入三耦合机制：1) 用转移概率矩阵捕捉代理间协作频率，并在DAG构建时通过层内排序优化、概率阈值边建议和传递约简实现软性引导与并行化最大化；2) 通过语义匹配检测能力缺口，使用LLM生成专用代理并将其注入协作矩阵，实现即时可用的预测协作先验；3) 分层语义匹配：首轮用预训练句子嵌入做快速确定性匹配，低置信情况才调用LLM验证，从而大幅降低LLM路由调用。

Result: 在混合编码、数学与问答任务集上，相较近期工作流构建基线，WorkflowOps提升了端到端通过率；在结构化可分解任务上增益最大；与纯LLM路由方法相比，LLM路由调用减少超过80%。

Conclusion: WorkflowOps通过从历史工作流中学习代理协作先验、按需扩展代理池并采用分层语义匹配，大幅提升多代理工作流的构建效率与成功率；尤其在结构化、可分解任务上效果显著，同时显著减少对LLM路由调用。

Abstract: Multi-agent systems are increasingly deployed for complex knowledge work, yet their orchestration layers remain largely memoryless: each new task is decomposed, assigned, and executed from scratch with no benefit from prior successful executions. We present WorkflowOps, a multi-agent workflow orchestration framework that learns agent collaboration priors from historical workflows and expands its agent pool on demand to cover new capability requirements. Our approach introduces three coupled mechanisms. First, a transition probability matrix captures pairwise agent collaboration frequencies from past workflows and applies them as soft guidance during DAG workflow construction through intra-layer ordering optimization, probability-thresholded edge suggestion, and transitive reduction for parallelism maximization. Second, a sufficiency-driven agent creation loop detects capability gaps via semantic matching scores, generates specialized agents through an LLM, and simultaneously injects them into the collaboration matrix, so that newly created agents are immediately usable with predicted collaboration priors. Third, a layered semantic matching strategy uses pre-trained sentence embeddings for fast, deterministic capability matching as a first pass, invoking LLM verification only for low-confidence cases, thereby reducing LLM routing calls by over 80\% compared to pure-LLM approaches. Experiments on mixed code, math, and question-answering suites show that WorkflowOps improves end-to-end pass rates over recent workflow-construction baselines, with the largest gains on structured, decomposable tasks where past agent handoff patterns transfer.

</details>


### [100] [Self-Referenced Social Preferences: Cooperation without Observing Others Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07881)
*Mohamed Ayman Mohamed,Harshil Kotamreddy,Marcos Menon Jose*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Introduce self-referenced social preferences: agents use a self-learned reward model to assess others from their own perspective and incorporate those assessments into social preferences (via reward modification or policy-update weighting), enabling cooperation in sequential social dilemmas without access to peers' rewards.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Many multi-agent social-preference methods require observing peers' rewards, but in real-world settings agents often only observe behavior and outcomes. The paper aims to enable cooperation without access to others' private rewards by emulating human-like self-referenced judgements.

Method: Each agent learns a model of its own reward function and applies that model to other agents' observed transitions to estimate those agents' outcomes from its own perspective. These self-referenced assessments are integrated either by modifying the agent's learning reward or by weighting policy updates; for inequity aversion a value look-ahead is used. The approach is evaluated on three sequential social dilemmas.

Result: Across Escape Room, Clean Up, and Commons Harvest, agents using self-referenced social preferences learn cooperative strategies without observing others' rewards, sometimes achieving more equitable divisions than agents with true reward access. Integration choice depends on preference type; policy-update weighting remains effective under partial observability.

Conclusion: Explicit access to other agents' private reward signals is not necessary for learning cooperation: social preferences can be grounded in self-referenced assessments of others' observed transitions, enabling cooperative and often more equitable outcomes.

Abstract: Social preferences can promote cooperation in multi-agent reinforcement learning, but existing approaches often require agents to observe the rewards of their peers. In many real-world interactions, however, an agent can, as humans do, observe others' behavior and outcomes without access to their private reward signals. We introduce self-referenced social preferences, in which each agent learns a model of its own reward, applies it to other agents' observed transitions to assess their outcomes from its own perspective, and feeds these self-referenced assessments into standard social preferences. We study two ways to incorporate these assessments: modifying the learning reward, or using them to weight policy updates. We evaluate the approach on three sequential social dilemmas, Escape Room, Clean Up, and Commons Harvest, which require volunteering, public-good contribution, and resource restraint, respectively. Across all three environments, agents learn cooperative behavior without observing others' rewards, including in settings where independent learners fail to cooperate, and frequently achieve more equitable divisions of jointly produced returns than agents with access to true rewards. The effective integration point depends on the social preference: inequity aversion works best in the reward together with a value look-ahead, whereas a purely benevolent preference benefits from policy-update weighting. Under partial observability, the policy-update approach continues to support cooperation. These results show that explicit access to other agents' reward signals is not necessary for learning cooperative behavior: social preferences can instead be grounded in self-referenced assessments of others' outcomes derived from their observed behavior.

</details>


### [101] [ShanLiangRen: A Nutrition Agent for Personalized Daily Meal Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07886)
*Miao Xie,Xiao Zhang,Yuan Wang,Ruixin Zhu,Chunli Lv*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MDP并实现ShanLiangRen：结合RAG与帕累托引导的LLM迭代精修，向用户输出具份量与合规证明的个性化多目标膳食计划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实应用中个性化约束（偏好、过敏、成本等）与多维营养目标（热量、微量营养素区间等）经常冲突，且用户约束会随反馈动态变化，导致通用指南难以直接转化为可执行、可验证的膳食计划。

Method: 1) 问题建模：定义个性化全量化多目标膳食规划（MDP）。2) 规范化输入：将规范、营养成分表、用户特征与自然语言需求编码为约束规划实例。3) 检索增强生成（RAG）：从大规模食材/菜谱库中严格检索并缩减可行候选集。4) 帕累托引导精修：在确定性营养计算与约束验证回路下，用大语言模型迭代修改候选计划以提高多目标可行性/效率。5) 输出：带明确食材与份量的量化餐单及营养合规性报告。

Result: 系统能产出全量化、可验证的个性化膳食计划并展示约束满足与营养区间达成情况；已实现为微信小程序并公开演示视频。

Conclusion: 本文提出并实现了一个面向个性化、多目标、可量化的膳食规划问题（MDP）并给出工程化系统 ShanLiangRen：将饮食规范、营养数据、用户属性及自然语言需求转为约束规划实例，采用检索增强生成缩减候选集，随后基于帕累托原则用大模型在确定性营养计算和约束验证反馈下迭代精修，最终产出含明确食材与份量的可执行食谱与营养合规报告，并已作为微信小程序上线。

Abstract: Dietary nutrition planning plays an important role in chronic disease management and maintaining a healthy body. In applications, it must simultaneously satisfy personalized constraints and reasonable multidimensional nutritional goals. These two aspects often conflict, and user constraints evolve with feedback, resulting in a substantial gap between generic guidelines and executable plans. To bridge this gap, we first propose the personalized fully quantified multiobjective dietary planning problem (MDP). To tackle MDP, we develop a nutrition agent, ShanLiangRen. The system first transforms dietary specifications, nutrient data, user attributes and natural language requirements into an individualized constrained planning instance. It then employs an exact retrieval-augmented generation method to shrink the feasible candidate set from a large scale ingredient and recipe space. Finally, it adopts a refinement guided by Pareto principles, where an LLM iteratively revises candidate plans under deterministic nutrition computation and feedback from constraint verification. The system outputs fully quantified meal plans with explicit ingredients and portion sizes, together with reports on nutrition compliance that show constraint satisfaction and nutrient interval attainment. We have released the system online as a WeChat Program, ShanLiangRen. A demo video is available at https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=652OtY5VlGA.

</details>


### [102] [Textual Environmental Context and Spatial Graphs for LLM-Based Regional SST Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07895)
*Xiong Li,Xiaowei Zhou,Yanwei Yu,Qian Cui,Junyu Dong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种将文本化环境上下文与图神经网路生成的连续空间前缀共同注入LLM的区域多步SST预报框架；静态与动态图分别编码长期地理—气候关系与短期相关/热带气旋影响，实验显示在南海数据上优于比较方法，并提供规则化的来源可追溯解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了在不将全格网序列逐字串行化为文本的情况下，把异质的时间持续性、区域空间相关性与随预测日期变化的环境条件高效呈现给LLM，以实现区域多步SST预报。

Method: 把SST预报建成条件数值生成任务：以文本上下文输入历史SST/异常序列、与日期对齐的环境记录和静态海洋知识；区域空间状态通过连续的、由两类图神经网络（静态地理-气候图与动态SST相关性及热带气旋影响图）生成的前缀表示供给LLM。对目标节点的表示经空间前缀融合后注入LLM；预测结果并经规则匹配模块与知识条目对应以返回可追溯的解释。

Result: 在南海SST预报任务上：完整系统在十步预测中取得最优的平均绝对误差（MAE）和决定系数（R^2）；系统还能输出与环境因子方向和趋势匹配的、带来源链接的后验文本化解释。

Conclusion: 将文本化的历史序列与图神经网路生成的连续空间前缀结合进入大语言模型，可用于区域多步海表温度（SST）条件数值生成；在南海数据集上，完整配置在十步预测中相较于对比方法实现了最佳MAE与R^2，并通过基于规则的模块给出来源关联的后验解释。

Abstract: Sea surface temperature (SST) forecasting depends on local temporal persistence, regional spatial dependence, and environmental conditions that evolve with the forecast date. We study how these heterogeneous conditions can be presented to a large language model (LLM) for regional multi-step forecasting without serializing the full SST grid as text. We formulate forecasting as conditional numerical generation: historical SST and anomaly sequences, date-aligned environmental records, and static ocean knowledge form a textual context, while regional spatial state is supplied through continuous graph-derived prefixes. A static graph encodes persistent geographic--climatological relations, and a dynamic graph encodes recent SST correlations and localized tropical-cyclone influence. Two graph neural networks produce a target-node representation that is mapped by a spatial-prefix fusion and injected into the LLM input. On SST forecasting in the South China Sea, the complete configuration achieves the best MAE and $\Rtwo$ among the compared methods over ten forecast steps. Alongside the numerical forecast, a rule-based module matches predicted trends and environmental-factor directions with knowledge entries to return source-linked, post-hoc contextual explanations.

</details>


### [103] [Isotropic Yet Undecodable: The Sequential Content-Sufficiency Gap in Latent-Predictive Text Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07906)
*K. P. Santoso,N. Z. Fadil,F. P. Harsanti,R. V. H. Ginardi,G. N. Iyer*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 仅靠几何正则或秩/一致性指标无法保证序列信息可恢复，需规范化令牌监督与针对性读出（CANOPE）来实现可用的序列表征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验表征是否保留输入中按序目标的信息，揭示常用表征诊断（对齐指标、几何正则）在序列内容可恢复性上的局限，从而设计能保证下游可访问性的学习框架。

Method: 信息论分解（输入歧义、表示损失、读出不匹配），构造‘可恢复视图’反例（在完美一致性与联合各向同性高斯性下仍为零目标信息），基于确定性规范锚点的限制证明，令牌对数损失的一边界信息损失界，固定惩罚岭回归分析说明秩不足以决定预测风险；据此提出CANOPE框架（有序潜变量画布、规范化令牌监督、几何正则化）并做大量实证。

Result: 理论上构造并证明存在表面上满足一致性与几何规范但不含目标信息的表征；实证上在4万条验证序列与LJSpeech语音数据上展示：在正确目标长度已知的强自然腐扰下，PL0与PL2在池化秩上相似，但PL0的Positional Recall@1为13.5%而PL2为98.8%；冻结PL2并训练MatchaTTS读出在损坏文本上WER为21.54%，而冻结PL0为99.22%，端到端MatchaTTS为10.93%。

Conclusion: 几何正则性或表示对齐指标并不能保证表示保留可恢复的序列性内容；需要对齐到“规范化”标记并结合特定读出机制来实现可恢复性。

Abstract: We study sequential content sufficiency by investigating whether a representation retains the ordered target information available in its input. An information-theoretic decomposition separates input ambiguity, representation loss, and readout mismatch. We construct recoverable views where perfect agreement and joint isotropic Gaussianity coexist with zero target information, and establish limits imposed by deterministic canonical anchors. Token log-loss provides a one-sided information-loss bound; a fixed-penalty ridge analysis shows why rank alone cannot determine prediction risk. These results motivate CANOPE, a nonautoregressive framework with ordered latent canvases, canonical-token supervision, and geometric regularization. On 40,000 validation sequences, latent-agreement (PL0) and token-grounded (PL2) have nearly identical pooled ranks but reach 13.5% and 98.8% positional Recall@1, respectively, under strong natural corruption when the correct target length is provided. On 3,930 LJSpeech validation utterances, frozen PL2 with a trained MatchaTTS readout yields 21.54% word error rate (WER) on corrupted text, versus 99.22% for frozen PL0, while end-to-end MatchaTTS reaches 10.93%. These results show that geometric regularity alone does not guarantee recoverable sequential content or effective downstream access in the text settings studied here.

</details>


### [104] [Continuous Memory Machines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07907)
*Ciaran Regan,Kai Arulkumaran,Luke Darlow,Stefania Druga,Sebastian Risi,Llion Jones*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CMM通过矩阵化短/长期记忆与Transformer双向更新，将快速神经元级计算与持久记忆分离，带来更强的泛化与可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统RNN将信息压缩到单向向量状态，短期计算与长期保持共享表示，限制了并行的快速处理与长期记忆分离。CMM旨在引入类似生物系统中神经元级快速处理加上长期保持的组合。

Method: 在Continuous Thought Machine (CTM) 基础上，引入矩阵值的短期记忆（跟踪近期神经活动，由参数化的神经元级模型使用）和持久的长期记忆；使用Transformer同时更新两种记忆，提供双向读写使得每个存储单元可自我重组并互相读写。

Result: CMM在算法性任务、上下文学习（in-context learning）和递归推理任务上均超过多种基线，展示更强泛化能力并保留CTM的可解释注意力。代码已公开。

Conclusion: 提出了Continuous Memory Machine (CMM)，使用矩阵化的短期和长期记忆分别承担不同功能，配合Transformer进行双向读写更新，实验证明在多种任务上优于一系列基线并保持可解释的注意力模式。

Abstract: Recurrent neural networks typically compress information into a single vector-valued recurrent state, forcing short-term computation and long-term retention to share the same representation. Past extensions alleviate this bottleneck by increasing the memory capacity or separating timescales, but lack the combination of rapid neuron-level processing and longer-term retention found in biology. To that end, we introduce the Continuous Memory Machine (CMM), a recurrent architecture with matrix-valued short- and long-term memory states serving distinct functional roles. Building on the Continuous Thought Machine (CTM), the CMM's short-term memory tracks recent neural activity, with uniquely parameterized neuron-level models learning to use these activity patterns for computation. A persistent long-term memory stores information for later use, with a Transformer jointly updating both memory stores, providing an expressive bidirectional read--write mechanism such that each store can reorganize its own contents and both read from and write to the other. Across algorithmic, in-context learning, and recurrent reasoning tasks, the CMM outperforms a broad suite of baselines, exhibiting stronger generalization than prior memory-augmented networks while preserving the CTM's interpretable attention patterns. Code is available at https://github.com/SakanaAI/continuous-memory-machines.

</details>


### [105] [SIGMA: Self-Improving Alignment Generalization from a Model Spec](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07935)
*Jingyu Zhang,Shruti Palaskar,Daniel Khashabi,Benjamin Van Durme,Leon A. Gatys,Joseph Yitan Cheng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SIGMA enables LLMs to self-generate alignment training data and self-evaluate via rubric-based rewards, producing large safety gains and generalization to multi-turn agent settings while keeping capabilities.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: There is a growing mismatch between rapidly improving LLM capabilities (including self-improvement and agentic behaviors) and slower, costly alignment supervision, creating risk of misaligned powerful models; the work asks whether models can bootstrap their own safety alignment to reduce dependence on external supervision.

Method: SIGMA has two main stages: (1) Spec-guided task synthesis — the candidate model acts as a task designer to generate diverse alignment-dilemma scenarios and transform them into training tasks that probe the Model Spec; (2) Self-judged alignment training — supervised fine-tuning plus rubric-based reinforcement learning using the same model as the reward model for training signals.

Result: Training only on single-turn chat data, SIGMA significantly reduces harmfulness and misalignment in multi-turn agentic tests (AgentHarm: 22.6 → 14.8; Agentic Misalignment: 79.1 → 3.8), outperforms Deliberative Alignment and Constitutional AI baselines, and maintains general capability. Ablations show that a balanced Model Spec, test-time safety deliberation, and high-quality rubrics are critical.

Conclusion: Given a clear Model Spec and leveraging model-internal reasoning, a candidate LLM can substantially improve its own safety alignment via an automated data-generation and training loop (SIGMA), reducing harmful agentic behaviors while preserving capabilities.

Abstract: LLM agents are increasingly capable of executing complex tasks and of recursively improving themselves on easy-to-verify objectives such as software engineering and mathematics. Since alignment is much harder to verify, this creates a growing risk of capabilities increasing without appropriate safety alignment, especially as capabilities expand to auto-research and cybersecurity. Existing approaches focus on capability self-improvement using verifiable feedback or on alignment training with supervision from stronger models or curated data, creating an external supervision bottleneck for alignment. We ask whether current models can improve their own safety alignment, and propose SIGMA, a data generation and training pipeline enabling alignment self-improvement that generalizes to out-of-distribution settings. Given only a "Model Spec" stating the model's desired behavior, SIGMA leverages a model's reasoning capabilities to strengthen its own safety reasoning. SIGMA first performs spec-guided task synthesis, using the candidate model as a task designer agent to generate diverse alignment dilemma scenarios and convert them into training tasks that stress-test its understanding of the Model Spec. Next, SIGMA conducts self-judged alignment training through supervised fine-tuning and rubric-based reinforcement learning with the model itself as the reward model. Despite training only on single-turn chat data, SIGMA improves safety alignment in multi-turn agentic environments (AgentHarm harmfulness decreases from 22.6 to 14.8; Agentic Misalignment decreases from 79.1 to 3.8), outperforms Deliberative Alignment and Constitutional AI baselines, and retains general capability. Analyses show that a Model Spec balancing harmlessness and helpfulness, test-time reasoning for safety deliberation, and high-quality rubrics from SIGMA's task designer agent are crucial for effective self-improvement.

</details>


### [106] [Confidence Reasoning Graphs: Structured Confidence Estimation for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07948)
*Brendan King,Farima Fatahi Bayat,Jean-Flavien Bussotti,Pouya Pezeshkpour,Estevam Hruschka*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CRG在无训练数据与无模型内部访问的条件下，通过将任务成功断言分解为证据支持的子断言并对其分别估计置信度再聚合，能从单次轨迹得到更可靠、可审计的成功概率估计，优于多种基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在关键领域部署LLM代理时，需要对其输出是否可信或是否应人为干预做出校准判断。但成功证据分布在代理轨迹的异构、相互依赖步骤中；同时最先进LLM常限制内部信号访问、轨迹采样昂贵且训练数据可能不可用或过时，因而需要一种可在推理期提供可靠置信度估计的方法。

Method: 在推理时构建CRG：将代理完成任务的主张分解为若干上下文化的子主张（terminal claims）并与轨迹证据对应，使用模型或黑箱打分机制为每个终端主张估计置信度，随后通过聚合规则融合子主张置信度得到总体成功概率。该方法不依赖训练数据或内部信号访问，适用于单次轨迹评估。

Result: 在三个代理基准、三种主干模型和三类代理框架上，CRG在校准质量和风险感知的决策制定上均优于口头化（verbalized）、基于采样和白盒替代基线。进一步发现仅看校准误差可能误导；一个白盒替代方法虽然表观校准良好，但判别能力接近随机。消融实验表明，性能提升源于对单个主张的置信估计及其聚合，而非仅仅图结构构建。CRG同时能够在决策时暴露支撑置信估计的主张与轨迹证据以便审计。

Conclusion: 本文提出的Confidence Reasoning Graphs (CRGs) 能在没有特权模型访问或训练数据的条件下，从单次轨迹推断代理任务成功概率，通过将整体成功断言分解为基于轨迹证据的子断言并对终端断言估计置信度，再聚合为总体置信度，提高了置信度校准与风险感知决策的性能，并提供可审计的证据链。

Abstract: When using an LLM agent in a consequential domain, making an informed decision about whether to trust its output or intervene requires calibrated confidence in the agent's success. Confidence estimation for agents is difficult because evidence about success is distributed across heterogeneous, interdependent steps of an agent's trajectory. Practical agentic deployments introduce further challenges: frontier LLMs often provide limited access to internal signals, agent roll-outs are costly, and training data may be unavailable or quickly become outdated. To address these challenges, we introduce Confidence Reasoning Graphs (CRGs), an inference-time framework that estimates the probability an agent accomplished its task from a single trajectory, without privileged model access or training data. Rather than compressing an execution into a single holistic judgment, a CRG begins with the claim that the agent accomplished its task, decomposes it into contextualized sub-claims grounded in trajectory evidence, estimates confidence for each terminal claim, and finally aggregates these into an overall confidence estimate. Across three agentic benchmarks, three backbone models, and three agent frameworks, CRGs yield better-calibrated confidence and stronger risk-aware decision making than verbalized, sampling-based, and white-box surrogate baselines. We further find that calibration error alone can be misleading: a white-box surrogate baseline appears well calibrated while providing near-chance discrimination. Ablations attribute CRG's improvements to claim-level confidence estimation and aggregation rather than graph construction alone. Finally, a CRG exposes the claims and trajectory evidence underlying each confidence estimate, enabling it to be audited at decision time.

</details>


### [107] [Can Agents Work for Everyone? Cross-User Reliability for Mobile GUI Agents in Personalized User Interfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07972)
*Yeji Park,Jaeyun Shim,Taesik Gong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Introduce PAIR for constructing user-conditioned app states and RePAIR (RL with personalization-aware rewards); user conditioning hurts performance but RePAIR partly recovers robustness, showing promise for personalization-aware GUI agents.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Mobile GUI agents face variable UIs influenced by individual user content and histories; current evaluations often ignore cross-user variability, so reliability across users is underexplored. The work aims to build evaluation tools and training methods that explicitly account for personalization to improve cross-user robustness.

Method: Propose PAIR: a pipeline to instantiate and render application states conditioned on user histories/preferences, enabling controlled cross-user evaluation. Propose RePAIR: a reinforcement learning approach that uses personalization-aware interaction rewards derived from cross-user subgoal outcome differences to train agents more robust across personalized UI contexts. Compare six agents and evaluate subgoal and task success across user-conditioned environments and personal-target settings.

Result: Across six agents, substantial inter-user variation in task success observed; user-conditioned contexts lowered subgoal achievement by 6.98–15.4 percentage points, personal targets by 8.77–22.0 pp. Common failures include selecting wrong items before target exposure. RePAIR improves user-conditioned SAR by +5.87 pp, all-success by +7.50 pp, and overall Task SR by +9.42 pp vs supervised fine-tuning on unseen users, suggesting benefits from learning cross-user variation.

Conclusion: PAIR enables controlled creation of user-conditioned app states to evaluate agent performance across users; RePAIR leverages cross-user subgoal outcome differences via RL to improve reliability. Empirically, user-conditioning reduces subgoal success (drops of ~7–15 pp; up to 22 pp for personal targets), and RePAIR yields notable gains over supervised fine-tuning (+5.87 pp SAR, +7.50 pp all-success, +9.42 pp Task SR) on unseen users.

Abstract: Mobile GUI agents increasingly operate on interfaces influenced by users' histories and preferences, but their reliability across different users remains underexplored. We introduce PAIR (Personalized Application-state Instantiation and Rendering), a pipeline for constructing user-conditioned application states that enables controlled evaluation of the same task across different users. We further introduce RePAIR (Reinforcement learning with Personalization-Aware Interaction Rewards), a training approach that learns from cross-user differences in subgoal outcomes to improve reliability across user-conditioned mobile environments. Across six agents, we find substantial variation in task success across users and consistently lower subgoal achievement in user-conditioned UI contexts (6.98 to 15.4 pp). This gap further increases for personal targets drawn from each user's own content (8.77 to 22.0 pp). Failures in these contexts frequently involve selecting another item instead of the intended target, particularly before target exposure. Finally, RePAIR improves user-conditioned SAR (+5.87 pp), all-success (+7.50 pp), and overall Task SR (+9.42 pp) over its supervised fine-tuning parent on unseen users, providing initial evidence that explicitly learning from cross-user variation can improve GUI-agent reliability.

</details>


### [108] [Learning from Revision Consequences: Hindsight Meta-Experience Distillation for Self-Improving Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07979)
*Qianhan Feng,Zhongzhen Huang,Yakun Zhu,Xiaofan Zhang,Qi Dou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: HMED通过在相同恢复状态下重放并对比旧/新Meta-Skills，将差异蒸馏为可复用的Meta-Experience，有效消除状态-修订混淆，提升了Meta-Skill学习与Skill发现性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原有基于分支结果的Meta-Skill更新会将初始发现状态与Meta-Skill修订的效应混淆，导致偏向利用有利起点而非改进搜索过程本身。需要一种方法把修订的因果影响在受控条件下分离出来并保存为学习信号。

Method: 对每一次修订的来源事件，恢复该事件的发现状态，然后在相同起点重复运行旧的和修订后的Meta-Skills；观察并比较产生的搜索过程与分支性能差异，将比较结果结构化为Meta-Experience并用于后续学习更新。

Result: 在三套交互式代理基准上、并针对开源与闭源模型，HMED相较强基线稳定提高了Skill发现效率和质量，且能利用未被保留的修订贡献有价值的学习信号。

Conclusion: HMED通过在相同的“发现状态”下对比再执行（incumbent）与修订后的Meta-Skills，把二者差异蒸馏为可重用的Meta-Experience，从而将Meta-Skill学习从分支最终结果转向修订对改进过程本身的影响，显著提升Skill发现性能。

Abstract: As agents continuously improve by generating and revising Skills, the process that discovers and refines those Skills becomes a learnable object in its own right. Task-Skills directly act on task execution, whereas Meta-Skills govern how agents discover and improve future Skills; their value therefore emerges through the subsequent search processes they induce. Existing approaches improve Meta-Skills from observed raw Skill-search trajectories and branch outcomes. However, branch performance entangles the effects of the initial discovery state and the Meta-Skill revision that generated the search process, making it difficult to characterize what a particular revision actually changed, and pushing updates toward revisions that benefit from favorable states rather than those that improve the process. We introduce HMED (Hindsight Meta-Experience Distillation), a mechanism for constructing Meta-Experience for self-improving agents. HMED revisits the completed event from which a revision originates and re-executes the incumbent and revised Meta-Skills from the same restored discovery state, so that the changes associated with the revision can be observed under a shared condition. Each comparison is distilled into a Meta-Experience, a structured record that can be reused by future updates, so that even revisions that are not ultimately retained still contribute a learning signal. Across three interactive agent benchmarks and both open-source and closed-source models, HMED consistently improves Skill discovery performance over strong baselines, shifting Meta-Skill learning beyond branch outcomes toward the consequences of changing the improvement process.

</details>


### [109] [Learning in Dreams, Winning in Reality: A Continuous Dyna Loop for a Ten-Hero MOBA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08033)
*Jordy Kieto*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 在大型MOBA上，外部评估与异步Dyna闭环能够揭露并修复世界模型在策略分布下的失真，从而在真实对局中产出高胜率策略，但模型保真度在细节机制上仍不足，导致战术偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界模型通常靠内部指标（预测损失、想象回报、画面逼真度）判断，但这些指标无法发现模型被策略利用或在策略分布下的失真；作者提出从外部（真实游戏胜率）评判，并通过Dyna闭环保持模型在策略分布上的准确性，以获得在真实环境中稳健的策略。

Method: 构建一个结构化的、多智能体的完整十英雄MOBA世界模型（206个单位，每刻每英雄动作，最高6000刻），在世界模型中以1400刻自由演想（dream）对PPO策略训练，并以连续异步Dyna循环把策略在真实游戏中的自我博弈作为世界模型训练数据与在线评估锚定策略；最终在未见种子上对游戏自带对手进行离线/在线评估。

Result: 在未见种子上，经过Dyna闭环训练的策略作为天辉（Radiant）赢得70.2%真实对局（421/600，95% CI 66.4–73.7）；仅梦境训练为0%，进入Dyna循环前为33.7%；作为夜魇（Dire）无胜绩；发现四条主要结论：模型利用在梦内不可见；训练集上准确的模型在策略自我博弈上也可能严重偏差；Dyna在策略分布处修复模型，带来+9.2点真实胜率提升；策略继承了模型的保真度特征，模型描述宏观态势但忽略控制、施法时序和杀伤力，导致策略以地图压迫而非协同战斗取胜。

Conclusion: 使用外部（真实游戏）评估并将世界模型置于连续异步Dyna闭环中，可以产生在真实MOBA环境中高度有效的策略；仅在“梦境”中训练会产生不可见的模型利用和策略崩溃，而Dyna通过把策略的自我博弈作为世界模型的训练数据并在线锚定评估，修复了这些偏差。

Abstract: World models are usually judged from the inside: by prediction loss, by the return a policy earns in imagination, or by how convincing their frames look. We judge one from the outside. We learn a structured, multi-agent world model of a complete ten-hero MOBA (206 units, every hero acting every tick, games of up to 6,000 ticks), train a policy only inside it with 1,400-tick free-running imagined episodes, and measure that policy in the real game against the opponent the game ships with. The real game never provides a gradient; it provides the policy's own games as training data for the world model, and an online evaluation that selects and anchors the policy. Run as a continuous asynchronous Dyna loop, the policy wins 70.2% of real games as radiant (421 of 600; 95% CI 66.4-73.7) on seeds never used for any decision, up from 0% for dream training alone and 33.7% before the loop. It wins none as dire, and neither does the shipped opponent when it plays itself. Four findings explain the result. Model exploitation is invisible from inside the dream: every unanchored run collapsed within a few updates while no in-dream metric tracked the collapse. A world model that is accurate on its training corpus is badly wrong on the policy's own games, and Dyna repairs it there, which is worth +9.2 points of real win rate with the policy recipe held fixed. Finally, the policy inherits its world model's fidelity profile mechanic by mechanic: the model represents the macro game but not crowd control, cast timing or lethality, and the policy wins by map-wide pressure with almost no coordinated fighting. We release the world model, the dream-PPO harness, a world-model debugger, the evaluation protocol, and every policy and log.

</details>


### [110] [Same Feedback, Different Answer: Measuring Run-to-Run Instability in Frontier-Model Customer Feedback Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08036)
*Viraj Bagal,Raviraja Ganta,Prabhath Chellingi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: A repeat-run evaluation plus taxonomy grounding yields much more stable and repeatable categorizations and counts from LLM agents than unguided generation or taxonomy-free hierarchical approaches.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: When automating knowledge work over unstructured data, outputs must be repeatable: categories, priorities, and counts should not shift materially between runs when evidence is unchanged. LLM outputs are often plausible but unstable, motivating a framework and method to increase consistency.

Method: Proposes a repeat-run evaluation framework with two operating metrics—theme churn (normalized change in returned category set) and volume disagreement (change in counts for persistent categories). Evaluates three customer-feedback tasks across eight frontier models, corpus sizes 100–5,000, multiple prompts, and three execution designs: raw generation, taxonomy-free hierarchical decomposition, and a taxonomy-grounded agent using persistent themes/subthemes with record-level predictions.

Result: With Claude Opus 4.8 on a 1,000-record corpus, TGA reduced theme churn by 86–88% versus the other designs and produced zero volume disagreement for matched themes. TGA remained the most stable method across corpus sizes, models, and different theme-matching strictness levels. Authors claim applicability beyond customer feedback to other unstructured domains.

Conclusion: Taxonomy-grounded agents (TGA) substantially improve repeatability of LLM-based syntheses over unstructured corpora: they sharply reduce theme churn and eliminate matched-theme volume disagreement in key conditions, outperforming raw generation and taxonomy-free hierarchical decomposition across models and corpus sizes.

Abstract: AI agents are increasingly being programmed to automate knowledge work over large collections of unstructured data. Such automation requires repeatability: when the underlying evidence is unchanged, the agent's categories, priorities, and counts should not shift materially between runs, even if each individual answer appears plausible. We introduce a repeat-run evaluation framework that aligns semantically equivalent categories and focuses on two operating metrics: theme churn, the normalized change in the returned category set, and volume disagreement, the change in counts for categories that persist. We evaluate three recurring customer-feedback tasks across eight frontier models, corpus sizes from 100 to 5,000 records, multiple prompts, and three execution designs: raw generation, taxonomy-free hierarchical decomposition, and a taxonomy-grounded agent (TGA) using persistent themes, subthemes, and record-level predictions. With Claude Opus 4.8 and the 1,000-record corpus fixed, TGA reduces theme churn by 86--88% relative to both raw generation and hierarchical decomposition, while matched-theme volumes have zero disagreement. The taxonomy-grounded agent is more stable than every raw model in the screen, remains more stable at each corpus size, and keeps this advantage when theme matching is made stricter or looser. Although evaluated on customer feedback, the framework targets repeated synthesis of unstructured corpora more broadly, including financial reports, legal documents, incident records, and scientific literature. Overall, these results show that taxonomy grounding produces more consistent and repeatable outputs for recurring knowledge work.

</details>


### [111] [DAEDALUS: Bootstrapping Agent Memory from Self-Generated Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08048)
*Antoine Edy,Max Conti,Victor Xing,Marc-Antoine Allard,Nawfal Benhamdane,Gautier Viaud*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 通过探索者—解题者循环从自生成练习中提炼并验证启发式，DAEDALUS 在无先验的条件下构建可复用代理记忆，从而显著改善 LLM 代理在新环境中的可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于 LLM 的代理在新环境中缺乏操作性知识（具体工具行为与约定），容易重复相同错误；现有解决方案依赖人工规则或基于训练集及 oracle 的记忆构建，需先验环境知识。目标是无先验、自动化地自举（bootstrap）可重用的代理记忆。

Method: 将系统拆分为“探索者（生成有挑战但可解的任务）”与“解题者（尝试完成任务）”两端：对每次解题失败从解题轨迹中提取启发式策略；仅在解题者在该启发式指导下反复成功后接受并存入记忆库；同时用成功/失败信号调整探索者生成任务的难度；所获启发式在测试时被检索并复用。

Result: 在 AppWorld、τ^2-bench 与 AutomationBench 上，DAEDALUS 相较于无记忆基线，平均成功率最高提升 15.9 个百分点，pass^5 提升最多达 2.2 倍；与基于训练任务的方法相比具有可竞争的表现且推理成本更低；小规模探索预算即可见收益；所得启发式对其他模型家族也有增益；消融显示解题轨迹是提取有效启发式的关键，早期发现的因子化保存提高探索效率；另外，DAEDALUS 自生成任务可作为模型性能排序的代理基准。

Conclusion: DAEDALUS 能在没有现成任务或 oracle 验证器的情况下，通过自我生成的“练习—失败—提炼—验证”循环构建可重用的代理记忆，从而减少重复错误并提升跨环境的执行成功率。

Abstract: LLM agents often lack the operational knowledge to act reliably in new environments, as they must discover specific tool behaviors or environment conventions on their own. Without memory of past attempts, they repeat the same mistakes across tasks, leading to more task failures and longer trajectories. To address this, agentic systems typically rely on human-written guidelines or on procedural memory built from training tasks and an oracle verifier, both of which require prior knowledge of the environment. We present DAEDALUS, a method for bootstrapping reusable agent memory from self-generated practice without existing tasks or oracle verifiers. DAEDALUS pairs two agents: an explorer that interacts with the environment to generate challenging yet solvable tasks, and a solver that attempts them. A heuristic is derived from each solver failure and accepted only after the solver repeatedly succeeds with that heuristic in context. These outcomes also provide feedback for the explorer to refine the difficulty of future tasks. Accepted heuristics are then consolidated into a memory bank for test-time use. Across AppWorld, $τ^2$-bench, and AutomationBench, DAEDALUS improves mean success rates by up to 15.9 points and pass^5 by up to 2.2x over a no-memory baseline, and is competitive with methods using training tasks, at a lower inference cost than most. We show that performance gains already emerge with a small exploration budget, and that its heuristics also benefit agents from other model families. Our ablations further reveal that solver traces provide the key information needed to derive effective heuristics, while factorizing early discoveries makes exploration more cost-efficient. Beyond memory construction, we find that the tasks generated by DAEDALUS can serve as a proxy for benchmark tasks when ranking models by performance. Code and artifacts: www.github.com/illuin-tech/daedalus.

</details>


### [112] [SpeedrunBench: Challenging LLM Agents with Video Game Speedrunning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08076)
*Yoshinari Fujinuma,Keisuke Kamahori,Ryuto Koike,Abdelrahman Madkour,Varun Prashant Gangal,Monty Bichouna,Martyna Markiewicz,Shivani Jain,Duncan Curtis,Rebecca Qian,Anand Kannappan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Introduces SPEEDRUNBENCH (9 games) to evaluate LLM agents' strategy formation in speedrunning; agents perform well on short games but not on complex long-horizon tasks, making the benchmark a robust testbed for ongoing progress.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: To test whether LLM agents can develop sophisticated, novel strategies that go beyond established human solutions, especially for problems with limited prior data or context. Use speedrunning as a communal, measurable domain that rewards discovery of unorthodox strategies.

Method: Constructed a 9-game benchmark (SPEEDRUNBENCH) and evaluated frontier LLM agents on repeated self-improvement tasks requiring long-horizon planning, reflection, and exploitation. Compared agent completion times against human world records and measured performance under limited budgets.

Result: Empirical results show agents nearly match human world records in simple platformers but lag on longer/more complex games within realistic budgets. The benchmark remains useful because faster times are always potentially discoverable, preventing early saturation.

Conclusion: SPEEDRUNBENCH is a useful, saturation-resistant benchmark for evaluating LLM agents' strategy formation in video game speedrunning; frontier agents can approach human-level times on short/simple platformers but fall short on longer, more complex games under practical compute budgets.

Abstract: Frontier LLM agents have been shown to be capable of solving increasingly complex tasks for which humans have measurable solutions. This begs the pertinent question of whether LLM agents can go beyond what humans have already solved. The ability to develop sophisticated strategies to tackle consequential problems becomes paramount as well-trodden, human-developed solutions become insufficient for problems for which we lack context or enough training data. We study agents' capability of such strategy formation through the communal practice of video game speedrunning. In speedrunning, practitioners compete to find the fastest way to complete a video game under certain conditions, and in so doing uncovering interesting unorthodox play styles that require a thorough understanding and mastery of the underlying game mechanics. We introduce SPEEDRUNBENCH, a benchmark that evaluates frontier LLM agents across 9 different games. To perform well in this benchmark, agents must repeatedly improve their strategy, reflect on their performance, exploit their gained knowledge, and reason across a long-horizon of actions to improve on an increasingly difficult problem: being faster than themselves and everyone else. Our experiments show that while frontier agents approach human world records in simple platformer games, they remain behind human performance on longer, more complex games under practical budgets. These results suggest that SPEEDRUNBENCH is a useful testbed for studying agents' strategy formation capabilities as well as being a saturation-resistant evaluation measure, as there is almost always a faster completion time waiting to be discovered.

</details>


### [113] [Self-Retrospection Distillation: Turning Post-hoc Experiences into Prior Foresight](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08077)
*Haoxiang Zhang,Qinglin Chen,Hiroaki Hayashi,Zhuofeng Li,Siming Zhang,Jiaxin Zhang,Jixuan Chen,Fang Wu,Pan Lu,Silvio Savarese,Julian McAuley,Chien-Sheng Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出SRD：用事后轨迹作为特权监督蒸馏到事前的前瞻预测，从而在回报对比稀缺或回报一致情形下显著提升强化学习代理的效率与成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在以标量结果回报为主要学习信号的强化学习设定中（RLVR），当一组rollout获得相同回报时（组相对目标或回报对比稀缺），标量信号消失但轨迹中依然包含可学习的信息。问题是：能否利用事后经验教会代理在行动前预测有用信息，从而弥补回报信号的匮乏？

Method: 引入了“前瞻学习”（prospective learning）范式，并提出了具体实现Self‑Retrospection Distillation（SRD）。SRD以完整轨迹的事后观察（hindsight）作为“特权监督”，训练同一策略在交互前的视角上预测这些事后信息。前瞻预测仅作为训练目标，不在推理时生成。

Result: 在10个与工具集成的推理与长时序代理任务上，SRD在多数基线之上带来最高24.2个百分点的提升。尤其在回报对比极为稀缺的情况下（37%–98%的组回合回报一致），SRD仍能从样本轨迹中提取学习信号；在2B参数规模下，原始RLVR在98%全失败的组上成功率为0.0%，加入SRD后在相同采样预算下达到了60.6%。

Conclusion: 本文结论是：通过将事后轨迹中获得的“特权”信息蒸馏到事前不可见的前瞻预测（trajectory‑blind foresight），可以在回报对比稀缺或组内回报一致的情形下，显著恢复或提升学习信号，从而大幅提高下游长期推理与代理任务的成功率。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) turns agent experience into learning signals primarily through scalar outcome rewards after interaction. For group-relative objectives, however, this signal vanishes when all rollouts receive the same reward, even though their trajectories may reveal useful information about what the task requires and how the agent fails. We ask a complementary question: can hindsight teach an agent what it could have anticipated before acting? We introduce prospective learning, which uses post-hoc experience to supervise foresight predictions from the pre-interaction view, and instantiate it with Self-Retrospection Distillation (SRD). Intuitively, a completed trajectory reveals knowledge that would have been useful and pitfalls that should be avoided; SRD distills this privileged hindsight into trajectory-blind foresight of the same policy. Foresight serves only as a training target and need not be explicitly generated at inference time. Across 10 tool-integrated reasoning and long-horizon agentic tasks, SRD complements RLVR and self-distillation baselines with gains of up to $24.2$ pp. Its advantage is especially pronounced when reward contrast is scarce: when $37$--$98\%$ of rollout groups are reward-uniform across model scales, yet SRD can still exploit learning signal from sampled trajectories. In the 2B setting, where $98\%$ of groups are all-failure, the RLVR training ends up at $0.0\%$ success, while adding SRD reaches $60.6\%$ under the same rollout budget. Our results suggest that post-hoc agent experience is useful not only for evaluating or improving behavior, but also for shaping predictive representations before available interaction.

</details>


### [114] [POLAR: Ontology-Guided Risk Prevention for Tool-Calling LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08082)
*Yunju Kang,Seonghyeon Cho,Irene Li,Yeo-Chan Yoon,Chanjun Park*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: POLAR prunes low-reversibility tool calls using a two-layer ontology to provide auditable pre-emptive safety, showing limited domain-dependent reward gains but also regressions and unclear harm reduction.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Tool-using LLM agents act in dynamic, risky environments but existing safety mechanisms are mostly reactive or non-auditable; need a pre-emptive, structural, and auditable mechanism to avoid irreversible harmful actions.

Method: Construct a two-layer ontology to represent reversibility, generate a candidate inverse sequence for each candidate action, compute a graded reversibility score, apply a threshold to prune actions before execution; evaluate on τ^2-bench across six agent models and multiple domains, comparing task rewards.

Result: On τ^2-bench, POLAR increased mean task reward by 0.11–0.18 points on the airline domain for four of six agents, but only 8 out of 18 model–domain cells showed improvement overall; retail domain and stronger agents frequently regressed; highlights trade-off between task utility and pre-emptive safety; emphasizes auditability and the fact that reward is not a direct proxy for prevented harm.

Conclusion: POLAR is a pre-emptive, auditable guardrail framework that scores action reversibility via a structured two-layer ontology and prunes low-scoring tool calls before execution; it yields modest reward gains in some settings but also causes regressions in others, and reward improvements do not directly imply harm reduction.

Abstract: LLM tool-use agents operate in dynamic environments where many actions carry operational risk. However, most safety mechanisms react only after errors manifest. Existing pre-emptive approaches either fine-tune the agent on chain-of-thought deliberation or compile natural-language guardrails into runtime checks, but they do so without exposing a structural, auditable verdict. We propose POLAR, a guardrail framework for small tool-calling agents that assesses reversibility through a structured two-layer ontology. POLAR assigns each action a graded reversibility score by deriving a candidate inverse sequence; calls failing a threshold are pruned before execution. Evaluated on $τ^2$-bench across six agent models, POLAR improves mean task reward by 0.11 to 0.18 points on airline for four of six agents, but only eight of eighteen model--domain cells improve overall; retail and stronger agents often regress. POLAR provides an auditable structural check and characterizes its task-utility trade-offs. Reward is not a direct measure of prevented harm.

</details>


### [115] [Natural Language Questions as an Interface for Knowledge Graphs: QRAKEN Graph Distillation and Semantic Self-Healing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08095)
*Remo Grillo,Lukas Klic,Giovanni Colavizza*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: QRAKEN通过离线抽取的填充数据驱动描述（TTQL）对LLM的Text-to-SPARQL生成进行约束与诊断，在CK25上大幅提高严格F1，表明经验模式比仅暴露schema更能防止语义错配；但当前证据限于单一小基准，跨域、在线与可扩展性仍需验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 消除LLM在未知或未暴露模式的图上基于模式或schema假设产生表面上合法但语义上错误的SPARQL查询的倾向，用图的经验性填充模式替代单纯的schema暴露来约束生成。

Method: 训练无关的神经符号流水线：1) 离线蒸馏器从已填充的知识图（QLever快照）提取TTQL——多跳模式、条件频率、路径条件下的literal示例和类-属性共现矩阵；2) 在线阶段把TTQL注入到LLM的提示中，结合确定性语法/词汇/数据模型检查进行迭代细化和诊断；还评估了在本地小模型（35B、4-bit量化）上的零边际成本变体。

Result: 在CK25上（对QLever快照的匹配条件重算）：GPT-4.1 mini取得严格F1 0.643±0.026，GPT-5.4取得0.652±0.012，分别比最强的重算参赛系统提高约30%和32%。消融显示TTQL模式是主导因子（对比shape-only基线提高严格F1 +0.31），TTQL相比自动导出SHACL严格F1高64%。用两台本地35B量化模型也能匹配最强参赛者且保留TTQL优势。

Conclusion: QRAKEN通过把LLM的Text-to-SPARQL生成以离线抽取的经验性图模式（TTQL）为依据、并在在线阶段加入确定性语法/词汇/数据模型检查，显著降低了在未见图上生成与填充数据不一致查询的概率，从而在CK25基准上取得了明显的严格F1增益。主要限制是对单一较小基准的评估以及在非常开放的跨域图上直接注入大型单体TTQL的泛化能力。

Abstract: Natural-language access to RDF knowledge graphs is a core Semantic Web ambition. Large language models (LLMs) have advanced Text-to-SPARQL, yet on unfamiliar graphs they often generate valid queries that misrepresent the populated data model. QRAKEN is a training-free, ontology-agnostic neurosymbolic pipeline grounding generation in empirical graph evidence rather than schema expectations. An offline distiller produces TTQL, a compact description of populated multi-hop patterns, conditional frequencies and path-conditioned literal examples, plus a class-property co-occurrence matrix. Online, TTQL guides the LLM, while deterministic syntax, vocabulary and data-model checks provide diagnostics for iterative refinement. On CK25 (First International Text2SPARQL Challenge), under matched-condition recomputation on a QLever snapshot, QRAKEN achieves strict F1 of 0.643 $\pm$ 0.026 with GPT-4.1 mini and 0.652 $\pm$ 0.012 with GPT-5.4: relative gains of 30% and 32% over the strongest recomputed participant, outperforming systems using the same base model family. Ablations identify TTQL patterns as the dominant driver (+0.31 strict F1 over a shape-only baseline); the refinement loop provides a cheap safety net, rejecting triple patterns unsupported by the co-occurrence matrix. Compared with auto-derived SHACL, TTQL yields 64% higher strict F1, supporting the value of empirical patterns beyond schema exposure. With two local 35B 4-bit open-weight models at zero marginal cost, the same pipeline matches the strongest recomputed participant, and TTQL advantages over shape-only and SHACL baselines persist. Results on a single, relatively small benchmark provide an initial empirical signal; monolithic TTQL injection on very open cross-domain graphs remains the main limitation.

</details>


### [116] [Beyond Corrected Memory: Execution Consistency in Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08101)
*Zhe Yu,Zixuan Wang,Peidong Wang,Hehai Lin,Ruochen Zhao,Chengwei Qin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 定义并验证了“执行一致性”及其证据条件，证明日志须保存特定执行关系；CAVERT能从不完备日志中提取关系并优于现有基线进行诊断与恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 共享/保留的记录只表明发生了事件而非事件是否满足任务要求；需要形式化的判断标准与可操作的提取工具，以识别隐蔽的违规或误判。

Method: 提出“执行一致性”定义（以状态使用、信息交接和最终状态一致性为职责维度），设计证据条件；通过“受控移除/恢复证据”的实验、实际日志的自然日志注释验证定义；提出并实现CAVERT框架，从日志中提取执行关系并基于定义做诊断与恢复；与合约提示的大型模型和基于规则的基线在12个诊断设置和4个恢复环境上比较。

Result: 在移除关键证据后，82.4%的相反标签对无法区分；在恢复证据后，97.9%的合并对被成功分离。自然日志注释识别出定义的违规，但现有日志常缺失必要执行关系。CAVERT在所有12个诊断基准-执行器设置上均优于合约提示的LLM和规则基线，在4个评估环境的恢复任务下也优于规则引导的恢复方法。

Conclusion: 共享内存的记录本身不足以证明执行符合任务义务；必须定义并检查执行一致性和明确的证据条件，才能可靠判断合规性。

Abstract: Shared memory coordinates agents' actions, but correct records do not establish that those actions satisfy task requirements. Memory governance and failure diagnosis regulate or inspect recorded information; they do not by themselves establish whether it is sufficient to judge task duties. We define execution consistency through duties governing state use, information handoffs, and final-state agreement, with explicit evidence conditions for judging fulfillment. Our core claim is that identical retained records can correspond to compliant and violating executions under the same task rule. Controlled removal of evidence such as receipt, action dependence, or response validity leaves 82.4% of opposite-label pairs indistinguishable; restoration separates 97.9% of the merged pairs. Natural-log annotations identify the defined violations in actual executions. However, existing logs do not always explicitly represent the execution relationships needed for these judgments. To assess the definition's practical value, we use CAVERT, a framework for consistency diagnosis and recovery, to extract supported relationships from logs and apply these criteria. It consistently outperforms contract-prompted LLM and rule-based baselines in diagnosis across all 12 benchmark-executor settings. Under the same gate and executor limits, it also outperforms rule-guided recovery in all four evaluated environments. These findings identify execution evidence that agent-memory and execution interfaces should preserve for reliable judgment.

</details>


### [117] [DSV-Mem: Evaluating Multimodal Memory in Professional Workflows for MLLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08102)
*Jike Zhong,Ritwick Chaudhry,Xuanbai Chen,Tianchen Zhao,Linghan Xu,Yifan Xing,Nishant Sankaran*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出DSV-Mem基准评估多模态代理在复杂、多版本、结构化视觉文档里的


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态对话/LLM基准偏向日常、静态、摄影类视觉内容和回忆导向问题，难以反映专业场景下文档频繁更新、结构化信息密集、需跨版本合成并精确追踪状态的需要。为推动多模态代理在科研、工程设计、产品管理等专业工作流中的实际可用性，需新的基准来考察“稠密有状态视觉记忆”能力。

Method: 通过构造并发布DSV-Mem基准：1) 采集并由专家审核的场景与1000条问题，覆盖五类用户导向问题（当前状态、过去状态、派生状态、变更历史、冲突/拒绝）；2) 采用Hartley启发的题目筛选标准，偏好需要广泛检查视觉证据的问题；3) 引入生成机制，将状态转换（state-transition）合成与对话填充解耦，以自动生成评估套件；4) 对27种模型配置（含前沿闭源与开源模型）和多种记忆管理方法进行评测与分析。

Result: DSV-Mem基准显示当前模型能力有限：最强基线得分低于45%。分析发现：信息密度和多模态性均增加难度，但决定性因素是状态演化的复杂度（尤其是管辖性更新数量）；会话长度、OCR与算术并非主要瓶颈；模型常常在回答前不去核验用户命题与历史状态的一致性；增加推理努力或改进记忆管理带来的收益有限，而引入状态感知机制更为有效。作者将公开发布基准与代码。

Conclusion: 该工作提出了面向“稠密有状态视觉记忆”（DSV-Mem）的基准，针对专业工作流中多版本、结构化、信息密集的视觉文档和组合型查询进行评估。现有模型与记忆管理方法在该任务上表现较差（最强基线<45%），并暴露出模型未能在回答前核验前提、受状态演化复杂度影响最大等问题。基于分析，作者认为需朝“状态感知”设计而非仅增加推理或记忆容量的方向改进模型。

Abstract: Conversational MLLM agents are increasingly expected to assist in professional workflows, from AI research and engineering design to product management and business operations. Yet this capability remains underexplored: existing benchmarks largely focus on informal, everyday interactions and personal-life scenarios featuring photographic natural images, isolated static artifacts, and recall-oriented questions. In contrast, professional scenarios often involve structured, information-heavy artifacts that undergo frequent revisions and authority updates, and compositional queries requiring reconciliation of many artifact versions while tracking state precisely. To address these challenges, we introduce DSV-Mem, a benchmark for evaluating Dense Stateful Visual Memory. DSV-Mem comprises expert-reviewed scenarios and 1,000 questions across five user-oriented categories (Current State, Past State, Derived State, Change History, and Conflict/Refusal). A Hartley-inspired criterion favors questions with broader visual-evidence inspection demands. We also introduce a generation harness that produces evaluation suites by decoupling state-transition synthesis from conversation filling. Evaluation over 27 configurations spanning frontier and open-weight models and memory management methods reveals that the strongest baseline scores below 45% on DSV-Mem. Analysis surfaces findings: 1) multimodality and information density both contribute to difficulty, but state evolution, particularly the number of governing updates, is the dominant tested factor. Raw conversation/haystack length, OCR, and arithmetic are not the primary bottlenecks; 2) models often fail to verify user premises against prior state updates before answering; 3) increased reasoning effort and memory management methods yield limited gains, whereas state-aware designs prove more effective. The benchmark and code will be publicly released.

</details>


### [118] [ChartBmkAgent: Harness-Governed Multi-Agent Construction of Chart QA Benchmarks from Sparse Error-Taxonomy Specifications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08106)
*Langxi Huang,Pingping Zhang,Lanyun Zhu,Chunyang Jiang,Jiawei Shao,Haocheng Yuan,Peilin Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ChartBmkAgent通过一个有证据链的代理体系，将错误分类说明自动转为高质量的、目标明确的Chart QA诊断样本，能有效揭示并验证MLLM的能力短板。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着多模态大模型快速发展，传统基准滞后导致难以及时定位新出现的能力缺陷；需要一种表现力强且可按需构造的任务（如Chart QA）来快速把发现的缺陷转化为可测样本。

Method: 提出一个中央控制的构造流程（harness）来协调多个专门化子代理，按阶段要求提供对齐于原始错误类别的证据并记录每次验收的依据，从而从零开始生成满足指定诊断目标的Chart QA样本。

Result: 在300个覆盖整个错误分类的样本上，测试模型准确率区间为32.7%–84.3%，显示不同类别的差异性；针对性构造的跟进样本在三组模型对比中表现出显著差异（50.0% vs 82.2%，p=8.96×10^{-6}），并且86.4%的样本经多模型评估器判定确实测试了指定错误类别。

Conclusion: ChartBmkAgent能够将稀疏的错误分类说明转化为完整的、面向诊断的Chart QA样本，生成的数据在保持目标错误类别方面表现良好，并能揭示不同MLLM在图表理解与推理上的能力差异。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) advance rapidly, while conventional benchmark development lags behind, delaying investigation of newly observed capability gaps. Such investigation requires an expressive task format and an on-demand construction process: information-rich charts make chart question answering (Chart QA) suitable for probing coupled perception and reasoning. Automated Chart QA construction is intended to shorten the benchmark-development cycle by turning identified gaps into targeted samples on demand. Current methods, however, commonly separate target guidance from scratch generation: target-guided systems often require prepared data, charts, or templates, while scratch-generation systems primarily ensure artifact validity, without explicitly controlling whether newly synthesized requirements and content remain aligned with an externally specified diagnostic target. We introduce ChartBmkAgent, which turns an identified capability gap into targeted diagnostic evidence by constructing complete Chart QA samples from sparse error-taxonomy specifications. Throughout construction, a central harness governs specialized agents, requires stage-specific evidence of alignment with the original error category, and records the basis for each acceptance decision. On 300 taxonomy-wide samples, MLLM accuracies ranged from 32.7% to 84.3% with distinct category profiles, showing that generated samples reveal capability differences. Across three source-model comparisons, targeted follow-ups scored 50.0% versus 82.2% on matched controls ($p=8.96\times10^{-6}$); all six cross-model comparisons had the same direction, demonstrating targeted validation and diagnostic-data generation. Multiple evaluator models assessed whether each sample tested its specified error category; 86.4% met this criterion, providing empirical evidence of target preservation.

</details>


### [119] [Test-Time Agent Evolution for Long-Horizon Legal Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08138)
*Haotian Chen,Shuaicheng Niu,Haocong Rao,Kaisong Song,Jun Lin,Lizhen Cui,Zhiqi Shen,Yonghui Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 不改动模型参数，利用测试时记忆演化与规则对齐协作能提高长时序法律决策系统的整体可靠性与效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实法律场景中案件事实、证据与程序高度异质，静态策略难以适应；且法律推理跨角色、跨阶段相互依赖，单独提升某一角色能力并不能保证全局决策可靠性，因此需要在部署时不断利用先前案例与交互信息进行适配与协调。

Method: 提出训练时不更新参数的测试时自适应框架（\method\），包括：1）Test-Time Memory Evolution：检索先前案件中的可复用经验，针对当前事实与程序语境进行适配，并将整合后的经验进行凝练以供后续使用；2）Rubric-Aligned Collaboration：基于行为与程序要求验证并修正各角色动作，实现跨角色、跨阶段的协同决策。该方法依赖部署期信号和交互而非模型微调。

Result: 在J1-EVAL和LegalWorld两个基准上，对五个不同骨干模型开展广泛实验，证明所提方法在准确性、可靠性和交互/计算成本之间取得平衡，并在消融与案例分析中验证两模块分别带来的经验适配与跨角色协调收益。

Conclusion: 在不更新模型参数的前提下，通过部署时记忆检索与演化（Test-Time Memory Evolution）以及与行为/程序规则对齐的跨角色协作（Rubric-Aligned Collaboration），能显著提升面向长时序、角色相关的法律推理任务的整体可靠性与效率。两部分模块在经验复用与跨角色协调上互为补充，且在多个数据集与骨干模型上均优于代表性推理与代理基线。

Abstract: Legal intelligence aims to support reliable decision-making across long-horizon legal processes involving evolving case states and multiple roles. However, real-world legal deployment exhibits substantial case heterogeneity in facts, evidence, and procedural contexts, exposing the limitations of static agent strategies. Moreover, legal reasoning is inherently interdependent across roles and procedural stages, making global reliability fundamentally different from isolated role competence. To address these challenges, we study training-free test-time agent adaptation, where agents continuously exploit deployment-time signals from preceding cases and ongoing interactions without updating model parameters. We propose \method, which introduces \emph{Test-Time Memory Evolution} to retrieve reusable experience from previous cases, adapt it to the current factual and procedural context, and consolidate accumulated experience for subsequent decision-making. Further, \emph{Rubric-Aligned Collaboration} verifies and revises role-specific actions according to behavioral and procedural requirements, enabling coordinated decision-making across roles and stages. Extensive experiments on J1-EVAL and LegalWorld across five backbone models demonstrate consistent improvements over representative reasoning and agent baselines with reasonable interaction and computational costs. Ablation and case studies further show that the two components provide complementary benefits in experience adaptation and cross-role coordination, improving the reliability and efficiency of long-horizon legal reasoning.

</details>


### [120] [Partially Observable Zero-shot coordination by Predicting Intention of Partner](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08142)
*Jinnyeong Yang,Yuhwan Jeong,Hoyong Kwon,Minseok Kim,Jihun Kim,Kuk-Jin Yoon*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PIP结合联合视图蒸馏与信念推断，从本地观测有效推断被遮挡伙伴的意图与位置，从而提高零样本协同性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在具身协同中，伙伴会间歇性看不见，导致现有方法对伙伴表示模糊且无法确定被遮挡的伙伴状态，需要能从可见数据推断隐藏信息以实现零样本协同。

Method: 提出Predicting Intention of Partner (PIP)：1) Joint-view VAE在训练时融合两智能体的局部观测，蒸馏出能由单方本地观测重构的丰富伙伴表示；2) Partner-state Belief网络基于自我交互历史推断伙伴的隐藏位置与行为倾向。

Result: 在Burrito-PO、Overcooked-PO和Melting Pot基准上（并在Burrito-PO上做了人类评测），PIP在平均性能上超过比较方法。诊断分析显示两部分模块在伙伴遮挡情形下都对性能有贡献，并支持与未见过的伙伴协调。

Conclusion: PIP通过联合视图VAE与伙伴状态信念网络，显著改善在伙伴被遮挡时的零样本协同，能从仅有本地观测推断伙伴表示和隐含状态，且在多个基准和人类评测上优于比较方法。

Abstract: Zero-shot coordination in embodied settings requires acting while the partner is intermittently out of view, leaving existing methods with ambiguous partner representations and uncertainty over hidden partner states. We propose Predicting Intention of Partner (PIP) to jointly address these challenges. PIP uses a Joint-view VAE to distill richer training-time evidence from the union of both agents' local observations into a partner representation available from local observations alone. Partner-state Belief networks further infer the partner's hidden location and behavioral tendencies from the ego agent's interaction history. We evaluate PIP in Burrito-PO, Overcooked-PO, and a Melting Pot substrate, together with a human evaluation in Burrito-PO. PIP attains the highest mean performance among the compared methods across all three benchmarks. Human evaluation and diagnostic analyses further support coordination with unseen partners and the contributions of both components under partner occlusion.

</details>


### [121] [Which alloy composition,what process parameters? Inferring the recipe from optimized metallic microstructure and texture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08165)
*Mahish K. Guru,Jan Bohlen,Louam Lemjid,Marius Tacke,Roland Aydin,Noomane Ben Khalifa*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 在小规模镁合金挤压数据集上，传统的晶粒与织构统计比预训练视觉嵌入或GNN更能反演合金类别，微观结构可帮助恢复部分工艺参数，离散/顺序感知输出方式优于自由回归。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 逆向材料设计——从目标微观结构/织构反推能产生该结构的合金配方与加工参数，目前仍高度依赖专家经验，研究目的是判断是否可以通过数据驱动方法学习这一反演过程。

Method: 在107个镁合金挤压条件、14种合金的内部数据集上，比较三类微观结构与织构描述符（传统晶粒/织构统计、预训练图像编码器的视觉嵌入、基于晶粒网络的图神经网络），并为两项任务（A：已知工艺条件预测合金成分；B：已知成分预测工艺参数）配备预测头。使用5折交叉验证评估。

Result: 在5折交叉验证下，传统描述符在识别合金上达到65%的准确率（基线多数类策略为17%），预训练视觉嵌入低于30%；工艺参数预测有一定成功，使用微观结构信息将温度误差大致减半；由于数据集中铸造/压制设置有限，离散且顺序敏感的分类头表现优于连续回归头。

Conclusion: 微观结构和织构的信息能够部分反演出合金配方和工艺参数，传统的晶粒和织构统计描述比预训练视觉嵌入和图神经网络在识别合金类别上表现更好；工艺参数可回收但存在噪声，微观结构可以显著降低温度预测误差；离散且顺序敏感的输出头优于自由值回归。

Abstract: The mechanical properties of a metallic alloy are set by its microstructure and texture: the size and shape of its grains and the orientation of their crystals. That structure is in turn set by a recipe, the alloy composition together with the processing parameters. Alloy development runs this chain forwards, tuning the structure until a target property is met. Running it backwards, from an optimized structure to the recipe that would produce it, still relies on expert knowledge. We ask whether this backwards step can be learned. On an in-house dataset of 107 magnesium alloy extrusion conditions across 14 alloys, each with optical micrographs and an X-ray texture measurement, we compare three descriptors of microstructure and texture: conventional grain and texture statistics, a vision embedding from a pretrained image encoder, and a graph neural network on the grain network. Each is paired with prediction heads for two tasks: the alloy composition given the process (Task A), and the process parameters given the composition (Task B). Under 5-fold cross-validation, the conventional descriptors identify the correct alloy for 65% of held-out conditions, against 17% for always guessing the most common alloy, while the learned embeddings stay below 30%. The process parameters are recoverable but noisier: compared with using the composition alone, the microstructure roughly halves the temperature error. Because only a few alloys were cast and only a few press settings were used, both answers are discrete, and heads that pick from these known options, while respecting their order, worked better than heads that predict a free value.

</details>


### [122] [LFHE: Local-First Heuristic Evolution for Bounded Local Topology Search in Decentralized Learning with Non-IID Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08176)
*Yin-Kuan Liang,Yan Gao,Yang Long*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LFHE在仅使用ego+FoF信息的受限本地搜索下，通过Dirichlet能量导向的状态驱动重连，在稀疏度受限场景中实现了有效的去中心化拓扑优化，体现了局部性与性能间的可操作折衷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 去中心化学习在非IID数据下对通信拓扑高度敏感。现有方法在局部对等选择与全局谱优化之间存在权衡：广泛的候选发现需要大规模状态，而谱方法依赖全图信息。研究介于二者之间的受限本地拓扑搜索。

Method: 提出Local-First Heuristic Evolution(LFHE)，用表示驱动的重连框架：候选发现与打分仅基于ego-neighborhood和friend-of-a-friend信息；结构评分可解释为图Dirichlet能量；结合早期探索和度数控制，利用表示状态进行局部拓扑重连。

Result: 在四个图像、语音和文本基准上，LFHE在去中心化训练中达到具有竞争力的性能；对照实验表明结构项是主要的拓扑选择信号；与更广泛的候选发现相比，LFHE体现出性能与发现状态局部性之间的权衡。

Conclusion: 提出并验证了在受限本地拓扑搜索（ego+FoF）条件下，状态感知的重连策略LFHE能在稀疏最大度限制下显著提升去中心化学习的性能，兼顾局部发现与全局优化之间的折衷。

Abstract: Decentralized learning is highly sensitive to communication topology under non-IID data. Adaptive peer-selection methods can exploit local model information, but broader peer discovery may require increasingly large control state, whereas direct spectral optimization typically relies on graph-wide information. We study the intermediate setting of bounded local topology search and propose Local-First Heuristic Evolution (LFHE), a representation-driven rewiring framework whose candidate discovery and scoring use only ego-neighborhood and friend-of-a-friend (FoF) information. The structural score admits an exact interpretation through graph Dirichlet energy: its sum across clients equals twice the representation Dirichlet energy, which under standard linear consensus dynamics governs the instantaneous dissipation of representation disagreement. LFHE combines this state-dependent structural signal with early exploration and degree control, while algebraic connectivity remains an offline graph diagnostic. Under bounded sparse degree, its FoF candidate state remains local rather than expanding toward population-wide peer tracking. Across four image, speech, and text benchmarks, LFHE achieves competitive decentralized learning performance. Matched-protocol controls identify the structural term as the principal empirical topology-selection signal, while comparison with broader peer discovery exposes a trade-off between predictive performance and discovery-state locality. Together, these results motivate state-aware bounded local topology search between pairwise peer selection and globally informed topology optimization.

</details>


### [123] [Mathematical Proof Assistants for Teaching Logic: The LogiKEy Methodology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08214)
*Christoph Benzmüller,David Fuenmayor,Luca Pasetto*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LogiKEy: teach many logics by semantically embedding them in HOL and using a single proof assistant to let students learn, experiment, and compare logics from puzzles to research topics.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: To teach logic to mixed cohorts (computer science, mathematics, philosophy) in a way that supports experimentation, comparison of logics, mechanized reasoning, and progression from basic puzzles to research-level arguments while handling classical and non-classical logics uniformly.

Method: Use classical HOL as a universal metalogic and encode object logics by defining their semantics (semantical embeddings). Employ a proof assistant (Isabelle/HOL) with automated theorem provers and model finders; teach via a graded sequence of examples that progressively motivate richer logics and modelling resources.

Result: A decade-long LogiKEy methodology that has been successfully used in courses, summer schools, and tutorials; a concrete graded example sequence (liars puzzle → modal logic; Wise Men → dynamic epistemic logic; Boolos inference → higher-order meta-logic; Chisholm’s paradox → dyadic deontic logic; Gödel’s ontological argument → metaphysical research) and arguments rebutting the claim that this is monism rather than pluralism; reflections on three years of teaching and notes on portability beyond Isabelle.
