Experiments on driving and robot navigation tasks demonstrate continuous self-improvement in both policy and judge capability across reinforcement and supervised fine-tuning. These results show how scaling verification supports continuous self-improvement as policy failure patterns evolve.

</details>


### [155] [Agent in a Bottle: Can LLM Agents Turn Their Capabilities Into Cheap, Scalable Artifacts?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08775)
*Ankit Sonthalia,Haritz Puerto,Alexander Rubinstein,Martin Gubri,Seong Joon Oh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: BOTTLED是一个评估代理如何在预算内把LLM能力“装瓶”为可重用廉价解法的基准；实验显示零样本好并不等于能有效bottle，但成功的bottling能大幅降本，提示需要改进自动化蒸馏/程序合成与预算分配策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对数百万条相关实例逐一调用大型语言模型代价高昂，研究能否让智能代理以有限资源自动创造可重用、低成本的解决方案，从而在质量与均摊成本间取得平衡。

Method: 提出了BOTTLED基准：代理在已知但未标注的大规模工作负载下，在固定的时间、计算和LLM API预算内自主选择策略（如训练小模型、编写可重用程序等）来完成整个负载；在三项任务与十个模型上进行评估，并与小模型蒸馏基线及为廉价推理优化的“Jev”模型比较。

Result: 强零样本性能并不保证强“bottling”能力：模型在bottling后的表现常显著低于其零样本置信区间下界；许多运行也不及具有相同token预算的强小模型蒸馏基线。但bottling可实现巨大成本节约：例如在查询-商品相关性分类上，Opus 5在成本约降低657倍时仍保留约82%零样本macro-F1；与Jev比较，Opus 5以约四分之一的预计全负载成本恢复了约94%的Jev macro-F1。

Conclusion: BOTTLED表明“bottling”（把通用LLM能力转化为任务专用、可重复使用的廉价解决方案）是可行且在许多情境下能显著降本，但现有模型与代理在自动实现这一能力上仍不稳定且常常表现欠佳。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can solve many narrow tasks, but querying them separately for millions of related instances can be prohibitively expensive. Can LLM agents autonomously create cheaper solutions for such workloads? We call this ability "bottling": the ability to turn general capabilities into task-specific solutions that balance answer quality and amortised cost. We introduce BOTTLED, a benchmark in which agents receive an entire unlabelled workload and must complete it under fixed time, compute and LLM API budgets. Agents choose their own approach, such as training a small model or writing a reusable program. Across ten models and three tasks, we find that strong zero-shot task performance does not reliably translate into strong bottling capabilities. Models with similar zero-shot scores can differ substantially after bottling, and 48 of 60 bottling runs score below the lower bound of the 95% confidence interval of their model's zero-shot performance. Moreover, 31 of 60 runs underperform the stronger of two small-model distillation baselines with the same token budget. Nevertheless, bottling can yield substantial savings: on query-product relevance classification, Opus 5 retains about 82% of its zero-shot macro-F1 at roughly 657 times lower reported cost. Bottling is also competitive with Jev, a "system one" model built especially for cheap, repetitive inference: Opus 5 on the same task recovers about 94% of Jev's macro-F1 at a quarter of Jev's projected full-workload cost. BOTTLED provides a basis for evaluating and improving agents' ability to invest limited resources in reusable solutions for large, repetitive workloads.

</details>


### [156] [Sherpa: Teaching LLMs to Teach Adaptively](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08778)
*Weixian Xu,Yanzhe Zhang,Zora Zhiruo Wang,Changyu Chen,Diyi Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Sherpa：一种基于多种模拟学生的多轮RL框架，使教师LLM以学习成果为回报自适应教学，显著提升教学效果并获得人类偏好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有教学型LLM训练依赖示范、偏好数据或预定义教学准则，这些信号通常未直接以个体学习效果为目标，忽视了不同学生间的教学策略差异。Sherpa旨在以学习成果为目标，训练能够面向多样学生自适应教学的教师模型。

Method: 使用以大模型实例化的多个学生原型（基于不同学习偏好），在多轮交互中以学生学习成果为直接回报信号，采用强化学习优化教师模型的教学策略；在MathTutorBench及人工对照实验上评估。

Result: 在模拟学生原型上，教师模型提升学生平均表现约20.5个百分点；在MathTutorBench上的整体教学评分从52.5%提高到79.2%；人工研究中在成对比较中被偏好79.6%。

Conclusion: Sherpa通过多轮强化学习，在多种模拟学生原型上训练教师型大模型，从而学会根据不同学习偏好自适应教学，提高了学生表现并获得更高的人类偏好评分。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have become increasingly capable problem solvers, but being able to solve a problem is not the same as being able to teach it. Existing approaches to training LLMs as teachers rely on demonstrations, preference data, or predefined pedagogical criteria that specify what good teaching looks like. However, these signals are often not grounded in individual student learning outcomes, where effective teaching strategies can vary substantially across learners. To address this, we introduce Sherpa, a multi-turn reinforcement learning framework that instantiates multiple student archetypes with LLMs conditioned on distinct learning preferences and trains a teacher model to adapt its instruction by directly maximizing their learning outcomes. Teacher LLMs trained with Sherpa improve instructed students' performance across all archetypes by an average of 20.5 percentage points. Under MathTutorBench's evaluation, Sherpa raises the overall pedagogy score from 52.5% to 79.2%, indicating better teaching responses. Our human studies show that the trained teacher is preferred over the base model in 79.6% of pairwise comparisons. Together, Sherpa trains LLM teachers to adapt to diverse simulated students and become better aligned with human teachers, paving the road towards AI tutors teaching real students.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [157] [SAFESHIELD: A Decision-Organization Framework for Deployment-Time Safety of Small Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07276)
*Xingru Zhou,Luis Sentis,Aarti Choudhary*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出将部署时安全视为决策组织问题并实现SAFESHIELD：通过职责分解与显式协调（及决策追踪）提升并可审计的端到端安全；实验证明协调性对最终安全结果至关重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有运行时防护（输入审查、路由、检索验证、输出过滤等）机制日益完善，但缺乏关于这些安全决策如何被明确组织、协调和审计的工程化指导；需要一种系统化框架来保证部署时的端到端安全。

Method: 将部署时安全建模为决策组织问题，引入责任导向的职责分解（入场、路由、证据、释放）并在系统中实现（SAFESHIELD）；为每次请求记录不可变的Decision Traces，以供审计；通过机制级测试、阶段去除实验、协调去除实验和压力测试集评估系统。

Result: 机制级实验表明各个防护组件具备所需功能；在整体消融实验中移除组织结构显著恶化端到端安全；专门的协调消融显示切断依赖会降低安全性能（例如移除入场门控增加误放行，将上游证据从释放决策中屏蔽使释放准确率从96.0%降至69.5%），证明了组织与协调的必要性。

Conclusion: 部署时安全不仅依赖于单个防护机制的能力，更取决于如何将这些机制以职责分解并通过显式协调组织起来；SAFESHIELD通过四类职责和可审计的决策追踪实现了这一观点，并在实验中验证了协调对整体安全性的关键作用。

Abstract: Deployment-time safety of language models is commonly implemented through runtime guardrails such as input moderation, routing, retrieval verification, and output filtering. Existing deployment frameworks provide increasingly capable mechanisms for these functions, but offer limited guidance on how the safety decisions they produce should be explicitly organized, coordinated, and audited. We formulate deployment-time safety as a decision-organization problem with two elements: responsibility-oriented decomposition of safety decisions and explicit coordination among them. We instantiate this formulation in SAFESHIELD, a deployment-time safety system for small language models that organizes four recurring decision responsibilities (admission, routing, evidence, and release) and records committed decisions in auditable Decision Traces. We evaluate SAFESHIELD through mechanism-level experiments, aggregate stage ablations, controlled coordination ablations, and a deployment-oriented stress suite. Mechanism-level results show that the instantiated safeguards provide the capabilities required by the decision process, while aggregate ablations show substantial degradation in end-to-end safety as the surrounding safety organization is removed. More importantly, dedicated coordination ablations preserve the participating safeguard mechanisms while selectively severing their dependencies: removing admission gating substantially increases false release, and withholding upstream evidence from the release decision reduces release accuracy from 96.0% to 69.5%. These results provide system-level evidence that deployment-time safety depends not only on the capability of individual guardrails, but also on how their decisions are organized and coordinated.

</details>


### [158] [Catching Developers in the Flow: Low-Latency Agentic Program Repair at Google Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07289)
*Celal Ziftci,Spencer Greene,Ray Liu,Livio Dalloro,Lorenzo Dini*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: FlowAgent is a deployed, low-latency AI agent for pre-submit automated program repair at Google that uses a generate-and-validate ReAct loop plus abstention filters; it shows strong manual-eval accuracy (67.18%) and real-world adoption (28.6k applied fixes).


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Reduce developer context-switching and manual effort by providing real-time automated repair suggestions for test failures in the pre-submit (outer-loop) continuous integration workflow where low latency is required.

Method: Integrated into internal developer tools (Critique and Cider), FlowAgent uses a ReAct-style generate-and-validate loop combined with pre-execution and post-execution abstention filters to generate candidate fixes under strict low-latency constraints and validate them via test execution before suggesting to developers.

Result: Manual evaluation on 195 real-world failures: 67.18% of suggested fixes judged correct. Production deployment: suggested fixes for 295,508 changes; 65,069 previews by developers; 28,554 applied. Developer interviews indicate usefulness and acceptance, with remaining challenges.

Conclusion: FlowAgent demonstrates that an AI agent can effectively and practically repair pre-submit test failures inside a large industrial CI ecosystem, achieving meaningful accuracy in manual evaluation and substantial real-world adoption after deployment.

Abstract: Manual repair of program failures is time-consuming and disruptive for software developers, particularly during the pre-submit phase where test failures occur within continuous integration systems. While Automated Program Repair has seen significant advancement through Large Language Models, existing state-of-the-art techniques primarily focus on post-submit workflows, operating offline without the low-latency requirements necessary to assist developers in real-time within their flow before they switch context.
  In this paper, we introduce FlowAgent, an AI agent deployed at Google to automatically repair test failures in the pre-submit outer-loop workflow inside continuous integration systems. Integrated into Google's internal developer tools, Critique and Cider,FlowAgent utilizes a ReAct-style generate-and-validate loop, as well as rigorous pre-execution and post-execution abstention filters to ensure high-quality suggestions under strict latency constraints.
  Based on our case studies, FlowAgent is highly effective. First, a manual evaluation conducted on 195 real-world test failures demonstrated 67.18% accuracy in suggesting correct fixes. Following its Google-wide deployment, FlowAgent suggested fixes on 295,508changes, of which developers previewed 65,069 and applied 28,554. Developer feedback from interviews indicate that the agent is useful in suggesting correct fixes, integration of autonomous repair agents into industrial software engineering workflows is received well, while interesting challenges and opportunities still remain.

</details>


### [159] [A Validated Dataset and Benchmark for Coherent Multi-Diagram SysML Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07356)
*Ardalan Aryashad,Yan Jin*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: SEMAADB是面向多视图SysML一致性问题的大规模数据集与基准；实验表明语法修复已趋于成熟，但语义修复与跨图一致性仍需改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前缺乏能规模化测量模型生成多图一致性能力的数据集与基准，系统工程师需要跨多个视图保持元素与语义一致性，故构建该数据集以填补空白并推动相关研究。

Method: 作者构建每个上下文包含5种SysML视图（Requirement, Block Definition, Activity, State Machine, Sequence）；对图集进行渲染和一致性检查；挑选100个人工验证的上下文作为基准测试；用三种语言模型在两个任务上评估（图修复与跨图更新），并以语义错误修复率和传播F1等指标报告性能。

Result: 数据集公开了15,000张图与3,000个上下文，基准显示最强模型在语义错误修复上达到64.3%，在跨图更新传播任务上最优传播F1为80.7%，提示语法问题可近乎完全修复但语义与跨图一致性仍具挑战。

Conclusion: 论文提出了SEMAADB数据集与基准，包含3,000个工程上下文和15,000张互联的SysML视图集合，并用100个人工校验的上下文作为测试集；评估表明语法修复已接近解决，但语义修复与跨图一致性仍是难点。

Abstract: Systems engineers use several diagrams to describe the structure and behavior of systems. Engineers create these diagrams together to make sure that they use the same elements and remain consistent with one another. Large language models can generate diagrams as text or code, which makes it possible to create system diagrams automatically. However, their ability to generate coherent sets of diagrams is not well understood, and existing datasets and benchmarks do not directly measure this ability at scale. We introduce SEMAADB (Systems Engineering Modeling Assistant with AI Dataset and Benchmark), a dataset of 3,000 engineering contexts and 15,000 diagrams. Each context contains five connected SysML views: Requirement, Block Definition, Activity, State Machine, and Sequence. Here, a view is a diagram that presents one aspect of a system. We checked the diagram sets for consistency and valid rendering. A set of 100 contexts is also human-verified and forms the benchmark test set. We evaluate three language models on two tasks. In diagram repair, the strongest model repairs 64.3% of semantic errors . In cross-diagram update the best propagation F1 is 80.7% when a model applies one change across related diagrams. The results show that syntax repair is nearly solved, but semantic repair and consistency across diagrams are still challenging tasks for models. SEMAADB therefore provides both a large diagram resource and a set of benchmarks for measuring coherent multi-diagram SysML generation.

</details>


### [160] [CogAdapt: Cognition-informed Sparse Adaptation of Code LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07446)
*Yueke Zhang,Zihan Fang,Kevin Leach,Yu Huang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 通过把人类EEG/注意力先验与模型响应结合，按任务稀疏选择要更新的Transformer块，CogAdapt在大幅压缩可训练参数的同时显著提升代码生成效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全模型微调成本高昂，且先前使用人类认知信号的工作虽能指导训练但仍需更新大量参数。作者假设人类认知信号不仅能指示“学什么”，还能指示“在哪里适配”以降低训练成本并保持或提升性能。

Method: 提出CogAdapt框架：先从人类EEG和注意力数据中学习可迁移的程序级与Token级先验；在每个编码任务上，将这些先验与冻结模型的响应结合，用以决定分配给该任务的适应预算以及应更新的Transformer模块；微调阶段仅更新被选中的模块，推理不需额外人类数据。

Result: 在Qwen与GLM两类模型上均发现人类阅读行为与Mixture-of-Experts计算的一致性。CogAdapt在LiveCodeBench与BigCodeBench上取得最好pass@1，较常规模型微调在LiveCodeBench上分别提升约10.86与6.29个百分点，同时将可更新参数减少约86.21–87.21%。

Conclusion: 人类在阅读代码时的认知信号（如EEG与注视注意力）与大模型的内部计算存在一致性，这些信号可以用来指导选择性地、按任务分配参数更新，从而在显著减少可训练参数的前提下提升代码生成的质量。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have become increasingly capable of generating code. However, achieving stronger code-generation performance still often relies on costly model adaptation, i.e., fine-tuning pretrained model parameters. Prior studies have shown correspondence between human code processing and neural models' attention or internal computation. Human-aligned learning approaches use cognitive signals to guide training, but typically adapt a large portion of the model, leaving training costs largely unchanged. Human cognitive signals may indicate not only what the model must learn from, but also where adaptation is most useful. We investigate whether human responses during code reading correspond to code-model behavior and can guide selective adaptation without sacrificing performance.
  We present CogAdapt, a cognition-informed framework for task-dependent sparse adaptation of code models. CogAdapt first learns transferable program-level and token-level priors from human Electroencephalography (EEG) and attention data, then combines these priors with the frozen model's response to each coding task to determine how much adaptation to allocate and which transformer blocks should receive updates. During fine-tuning, only the selected blocks are updated, while no new human recordings are required for inference. Across Qwen and GLM, we find consistent correspondence between human reading behavior and Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) computation. CogAdapt achieves the best pass@1 across both LiveCodeBench and BigCodeBench, including gains of 10.86 and 6.29 percentage points over matched regular fine-tuning on LiveCodeBench, while reducing gradient-eligible adaptation parameters by 86.21-87.21%. These results suggest that human comprehension signals can provide useful guidance for making code-model adaptation both more selective and more effective.

</details>


### [161] [The EPIC Framework for Spec-Driven Development](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07534)
*Bruno Claudino,Ruizhe Xing,Yuanhao Zuo,Tyler Menezes,Anita Sarma,Kostadin Damevski,Zixuan Feng*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 通过对114个 SDD 开源仓库的实证评分与从业者验证，提出 EPIC（40项实践）帮助开发者在提交给编码代理前，让规格更明确、减少代理“自作主张”导致的问题，从而降低缺陷率并提升协作规模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前 SDD 模板帮助生成产物，但无法指导开发者判断规格是否充分或清晰，导致编码代理根据模糊指令自行填补空白，出现偏差或缺陷。研究旨在找出优秀 SDD 规格的构成并提供可操作的实践指引。

Method: 研究者对114个开源的 SDD（spec-driven development）代码库的产出物依据 ISO/IEC/IEEE 29148 标准进行评分，提取高分库的共性实践并归纳成框架；此外进行了N=15位从业者的验证和比较分析（如提交中 bug 修复比例和贡献者数）。

Result: 提出 EPIC 框架（40项实践，10个维度），大部分从业者认可；高质量规格的仓库在 bug 修复提交通比例更低（11.8% vs 20.4%）并拥有更多贡献者（中位数为低质量组的4倍），表明更清晰的规格与更少缺陷和更广泛协作相关。

Conclusion: 团队在使用编码代理时，明确、详尽的规范能显著降低后续缺陷并提高协作规模；EPIC 框架（40项实践，10个质量维度）可作为编写规格、计划和任务以约束代理行为的有效指南。

Abstract: One practitioner we interviewed said their team writes "must" instead of "should" when instructing a coding agent, because the agent may treat "should" as optional. Small wording choices matter because agents often fill gaps in their instructions with their own assumptions. Spec-driven development (SDD) asks developers to write a specification, plan, and tasks before the agent writes code. SDD frameworks provide templates for these artifacts, but the templates do not help developers judge whether they have written enough or clearly enough. We studied what good SDD specifications contain. We scored the artifacts of 114 open-source SDD repositories against ISO/IEC/IEEE 29148 and derived practices from the highest-scoring ones. The resulting framework, EPIC, has 40 practices in 10 quality dimensions that guide developers in making expectations and decisions explicit in specifications, plans, and tasks for coding agents. The majority of SDD practitioners (N=15) endorsed every practice. Repositories in the top third of specification quality spent 11.8% of their commits on bug fixes, compared with 20.4% in the bottom third, and had a median of 4x as many contributors. Developers can use EPIC to find and fill the gaps in a specification before the agent does.

</details>


### [162] [CheckerBench: Can Long-Horizon Agents Synthesize Static-Analysis Checkers?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07557)
*Hang He,Li Wang,Hao Chen,Yuchen Shao,Yuling Shi,Lisheng Wang,Peiyang Liu,Goose Lin,Zaiyuan Wang,Haiying Sun,Ting Su,Chengcheng Wan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出了面向静态分析检测器合成的CheckerBench与CheckerLab，可重建并自动评估提交检测器；实验表明当前编码模型在端到端生成可用检测器方面表现有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有编码智能体基准多集中于补丁生成或漏洞检测，缺少对从头到尾开发、构建并验证静态分析检测器这一完整流程的评估。需要一个可执行、可复现且细粒度度量的基准来衡量代理的实际工程能力。

Method: 构建CheckerBench：300个任务，来自297个CVE、167个仓库、85个CWE、五种语言生态；每个任务包含有漏洞与修复版本、固定的分析环境和检测器骨架；并实现CheckerLab评估框架，独立重建提交的检测器并度量漏洞-修复诊断对比、补丁定位、误报率及工具使用等指标。对21种模型-运行时配置进行三次独立重复评测并报告Pass@1等指标。

Result: 在21个配置与三次重复的实验中平均Pass@1为32.30%，最优为45.33%，显示多数配置未能稳定生成通过重建与验证的检测器，表明该任务对当前模型仍有较大难度。

Conclusion: 可靠且可重用的静态分析检测器开发对现有编码智能体仍具有挑战性，当前模型在端到端合成可运行检测器方面并不成熟。

Abstract: Static-analysis checker synthesis requires agents to interpret a defect specification, inspect a repository, implement analyzer-specific logic, and refine the checker through repeated compilation and analysis feedback. Existing coding-agent benchmarks focus on tasks such as patch generation or vulnerability detection and rarely assess whether an agent can develop a working checker in a repository from start to finish. We introduce CheckerBench, an executable benchmark of 300 tasks derived from 297 CVEs across 167 repositories, 85 CWEs, and five language ecosystems. Each task includes vulnerable and fixed revisions, a pinned analysis environment, and a checker scaffold. We further introduce CheckerLab, a common evaluation framework that independently rebuilds submitted checkers and measures vulnerable-fixed diagnostic contrast, patch localization, false positives, and tool use. Across 21 model-harness configurations and three independent repeats per configuration, mean Pass@1 is 32.30%, while the best reaches 45.33%. These results show that reliable, reusable checker development remains challenging for current coding agents.

</details>


### [163] [When Old Facts Return: Re-Reads, Reverts, and the Limits of Temporal Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07715)
*Neeraj Yadav*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 研究指出重读旧语句会使记忆系统把“观察到的值”误判为“当前有效值”，需要区分观察与变更证据，且简单的保护器能阻止误激活但无法在没有来源信息时识别真正的回退。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索记忆系统在面对“重读旧语句”和“真正回退”二义性时的错误类型与缓解策略，评估系统如何区分“看到某值”与“该值已被改变”的证据。

Method: 基于从软件修复中提取的130个原子变更转换，使用身份（identity）时序记忆模型在三种条件下评估：普通过渡、附加逐字重读旧语句的构造条件、以及拒绝重新激活已退休值的保护器；另有两项辅助研究分别将已退休历史暴露给答案模型与提供当前来源以解释行为变化；并对707个修复进行探索性抽取以提供范围背景。

Result: 在普通过渡下，身份时序记忆在文字陈旧值代理下达到98.5%模型评估准确率且观测到错误为0；引入逐字重读后准确率降至10.8%，陈旧值率上升到88.5%；加入不复活已退休值的保护器后，在构造重读条件中准确率恢复到97.7%，陈旧值率降至0.8%，但该保护器无法在无额外变更来源信息时识别合法回退。

Conclusion: 在考虑同一旧语句被重读与真正回退时，必须区分对某值的观察与该值已改变的证据；简单的身份时序记忆在常规场景下表现良好，但在重读构造条件下会严重误判，加入不复活已废弃值的保护器可恢复性能，但无法识别合法回退而不引入变更来源（provenance）信息。

Abstract: A memory system can retire an obsolete value and later restore it merely because the same old statement appears again. A re-read of an old source and a genuine revert can produce the same observed sequence of values while requiring opposite current answers. We study this ambiguity on 130 extractor-selected atomic transitions derived from software fixes. In the ordinary transition condition, identity-based temporal memory reaches 98.5% model-judged accuracy with zero observed errors under a literal stale-value proxy. Appending a verbatim re-read of the old statement reduces accuracy to 10.8% and raises the stale-value rate to 88.5%. A guard that refuses to reactivate a previously retired value restores accuracy to 97.7% and reduces that rate to 0.8% in this constructed re-read condition. The guard cannot also recognize a legitimate revert without additional change provenance. Two supporting studies examine exposing retired history to the answer model and supplying current source for changed behavior. An exploratory extraction study over 707 software fixes provides scope context, not a universal coverage estimate. The design implication is to distinguish an observation of a value from evidence that the value changed. Selected inputs, aggregate-only answer records, related-family judges and a post-failure guard evaluation limit the conclusions to the retained experiments.

</details>


### [164] [Acquiring and Verifying Repository Norms for Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07757)
*Kaifeng He,Xiaojun Zhang,Zhenxi Chen,Christoph Treude,Mingwei Liu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: RepoNorm通过验证仓库证据和历史来提取可适用的规范包，显著提升规范遵从性（特别是贡献相关指标）并保持较高精确率，但对功能性成功率的提升尚无统计显著性，需更多消融与显著性检验验证泛化性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代码改动可能通过功能测试但不满足仓库的贡献规范。需要一种方法识别并适配分散在仓库多处的规范和例外，以提高代理在开源协作环境中的贡献合规性。

Method: 提出独立于具体编码任务的RepoNorm流程：检索并归纳显性/隐性仓库规范；使用仓库证据与Git历史检查规范内容及其适用性；打包规范并向现有编码代理提供。通过对3个代码模型和RepoNormBench（121项任务）的评估，与未添加指导的基线（Raw）和CodeWiki文档进行比较。

Result: 相对于Raw，Overall NCR提升7.42–10.77%，Contribution NCR提升31.64–45.44%，Prompt-omitted NCR提升11.34–17.69%。RepoNorm优于CodeWiki在这些NCR指标上。功能性成功率比Raw提高5.79–9.09个百分点，但配对检验未达显著性阈值。抽样精度为86%（默认配置）。

Conclusion: RepoNorm能有效提升代码生成代理对仓库规范的遵从性，显著提高多项NCR指标并在样本精确度上表现良好，但对功能性成功率的提升未达到统计显著。

Abstract: Changes produced by coding agents can pass functional tests while leaving repository contribution requirements unmet. Following repository-specific norms requires identifying guidance dispersed across repository sources and interpreting its conditions and exceptions. Retrieval and documentation approaches supply general context, but agents must still determine which norms apply. We introduce RepoNorm to acquire explicit and implicit repository norms independently of coding tasks. It checks norm content and applicability using repository evidence, consults Git history when needed, and delivers norm packages to existing coding agents. Our evaluation uses three coding models and 121 tasks from RepoNormBench. Against the baseline with no additional generated guidance (Raw), relative improvements are 7.42-10.77% for Overall Norm Compliance Rate (NCR), 31.64-45.44% for Contribution NCR, and 11.34-17.69% for Prompt-omitted NCR. All three coding models also obtain higher values on these NCR measures with RepoNorm than with CodeWiki documentation. Functional success rates show observed gains of 5.79-9.09 percentage points over Raw; the paired comparisons do not reach the significance threshold. Sampled precision is 86% with RepoNorm's default configuration.

</details>


### [165] [ES-Trace: Auditing Ethical-Sourcing Disclosure of Code Generation Models Beyond Model Cards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07762)
*Zhuolin Xu,Haibo Wang,Shin Hwei Tan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 通过构建 MDTG 并解析声明引用，ES-Trace 发现现有模型披露普遍不足且分布不均，尤其是社会与劳动议题；建议采用引用感知的审计与更严格的模型-版本文档绑定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代码生成模型的广泛使用引发知识产权、隐私、公平性、劳动与环境影响等伦理来源问题，但尚不清楚现有模型在哪些文档中、以何种程度披露了相关证据。

Method: 提出 ES-Trace 框架，利用模型文档可追溯性图（MDTG）追踪模型、版本与文档工件间关系；对 10 个发布者的 26 个模型、77 份文档、20 个 ES-CodeGen 方面进行实证审计；比较仅 model-card 审计与解析声明引用后的披露评分。

Result: model-card-only 平均得分 1.77/5，解析声明引用后平均得分升至 2.82/5；大部分提升来自发布者在结构化元数据中声明的文档；发现社会与劳动相关方面披露最差、审计范围扩展能显著增加可观测证据、model-card-only 可误判发布级披露变化、文档不一致会导致证据被错误关联到模型或版本。

Conclusion: 研究表明当前模型的伦理来源披露普遍不足；仅用 model card 审计会低估披露量，解析声明的参考文档能显著提高可观测披露，但社会与劳动相关方面仍长期低报。论文呼吁采用引用感知的披露审计、显式的模型-版本绑定与文档一致性，以及加强对社会/劳动方面的文档化。

Abstract: Code generation models have been increasingly used in software development, but their development raises ethical-sourcing concerns involving intellectual property, privacy, fairness, labour practices, and environmental impact. Although prior work has defined ethical-sourcing criteria for code generation, it remains unclear how much evidence existing models disclose and where that evidence can be found. We introduce ES-Trace, a framework for ethical-sourcing disclosure audits that traces disclosed evidence beyond model cards using the Model Documentation Traceability Graph (MDTG), which represents relationships among models, versions, and documentation artifacts. We apply ES-Trace to 26 models from 10 publishers across 77 documents and 20 ES-CodeGen aspects. Model-card-only auditing yields a mean score of 1.77/5, while resolving the declared references increases it to 2.82/5, with most of the increase arising from documents that the publisher declares in structured metadata. The key findings of our study include: (1) social and labour-related aspects remain poorly documented, even when expanding the audit to the full documentation scope, (2) resolving documentation references substantially increases observed disclosure, raising the mean score from 1.77/5 to 2.82/5, (3) model-card-only audits can mischaracterize release-level disclosure changes, and (4) documentation mismatches can associate evidence with the wrong model or version, highlighting the need for explicit model--version binding and consistency across documentation artifacts. Our study calls for reference-aware ethical-sourcing disclosure audits, explicit model--version binding and consistency across documentation artifacts, and stronger documentation of currently underreported social and labour-related aspects.

</details>


### [166] [Harness Engineering for Software Engineering via Modular Executable Dev-Primitives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07832)
*Haibo Jin,Xinjie Li,Peng Kuang,Haohan Wang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 引入把代码等工件装备为带常驻LLM的可执行模块（Dev-Primitives），并用依赖感知激活+执行驱动诊断构建 HERMES，可在长程软件工程任务中降低上下文负担、提升修复能力，在多项基准上显著优于基线并在成本-性能上接近更大模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有配备终端访问的LLM代理在长程工作流中脆弱：需要反复重建分散于源码、配置、测试、依赖和运行行为中的程序状态，导致交互历史膨胀、上下文爆炸与语义漂移；大型仓库还使得识别任务相关组件变得困难。为此需一种模块化、可执行且具有本地语义感知的抽象来降低全局上下文负担。

Method: 提出 Dev-Primitives：将每个存储库工件与一个常驻LLM配对，使其具备本地化的自然语言接口、互组件通信和自我修改能力；在此基础上实现 HERMES 框架，包含依赖感知的动态激活机制与将运行时证据映射回需变更组件的错误诊断机制；在四个软件工程基准上进行实验与对比。

Result: HERMES 在四个基准上均优于匹配的基线 harness 平均 12.4%；当使用强激活与诊断模型时，即便 Dev-Primitives 使用 Qwen3-8B，也仅落后于同构 GPT-5.6 Sol 配置 4.5%，同时在 Terminal-Bench 4.0 上减少了 26.2% 的推理成本，表明结合良好 harness 设计可以在成本与性能间取得有效权衡。

Conclusion: 将存储库组件转变为具备常驻LLM的可执行模块（Dev-Primitives），并以依赖感知的动态激活与基于执行证据的诊断机制构建HERMES，可以显著提升长程软件工程任务中自动化代理的鲁棒性与效率。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) equipped with terminal access have demonstrated strong capabilities in automating software engineering tasks. However, existing agents remain brittle on long-horizon workflows, where they must repeatedly reconstruct program state scattered across source files, configurations, tests, dependencies, and runtime behavior, leading to increasingly long interaction histories, context explosion, and semantic drift. Large repositories further complicate the identification of task-relevant components. To address these challenges, we introduce \textbf{Dev-Primitives} (\emph{Development Primitives}), a modular and executable abstraction that transforms repository components from passive software artifacts into active participants in software engineering. Each Dev-Primitive pairs a repository artifact with a resident LLM, which gives the artifact an agent-native interface grounded in its own implementation and dependencies, enabling natural-language reasoning, inter-component communication, and localized self-modification. Building on Dev-Primitives, we propose \textbf{HERMES}, a Harness Engineering framework for software engineeRing via Modular Executable Dev-PrimitiveS, which instantiates these primitives at repository scale through a dependency-aware dynamic activation mechanism and a bug diagnosis mechanism that maps execution evidence back to the components that must be revised. Extensive experiments on four software engineering benchmarks demonstrate that HERMES outperforms matched baseline harnesses by 12.4\% on average. Moreover, when paired with strong activation and diagnosis models, HERMES, even with Qwen3-8B Dev-Primitives, remains within 4.5\% of the homogeneous GPT-5.6 Sol configuration across all four benchmarks, while reducing inference cost by 26.2\% on Terminal-Bench 4.0, highlighting the importance of harness design in software engineering agents.

</details>


### [167] [Beyond the Leaderboard: Multi-Dimensional Evaluation of Dense and Mixture-of-Experts Models for Automated Program Repair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08173)
*Anvi Kalpesh Shah,Umamaheswara Sharma B*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出基于ISO/IEC 25010的加权质量指标评估APR模型，发现评估结果随权重变化；MoE在激活参数更少情况下保持接近正确性，建议用激活参数而非总参数评估稀疏模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前APR评估通常以测试通过为准，忽略了可维护性、安全性与计算成本等实际软件质量维度；需要一种综合度量来反映多目标权衡并提供更全面的模型比较。

Method: 基于ISO/IEC 25010构建QI，定义可配置权重；在相同硬件上比较三种密集Qwen2.5-Coder（3B/7B/14B）与一款MoE（16B总参数、2.4B激活参数）的DeepSeek-Coder-V2-Lite；在40个QuixBugs与90个Defects4J缺陷上测评，使用McNemar精确检验比较正确性差异，并考察不同权重下的模型排序变化。

Result: QI显示模型排名随权重配置变化；MoE模型在正确性上与7B/14B密集模型无显著差异（McNemar检验），但激活参数仅为其一小部分（3-6倍更少），同时密集模型在尺度增大时正确性显著提升；提出活跃参数数目对稀疏模型评估比总参数更有信息量。

Conclusion: 提出的加权质量指标（QI）通过将功能正确性、可维护性、安全性和生成效率按权重组合，能够揭示单一通过测试套件指标掩盖的模型差异，并显示稀疏（MoE）模型在激活参数更少的情况下可保持接近的正确性表现。

Abstract: Automated Program Repair (APR) with language models is usually evaluated by whether a generated patch passes the test suite, which can hide differences in maintainability, security, and computational cost. We propose a Weighted Quality Index (QI), inspired by the ISO/IEC 25010 software quality model, that combines functional correctness, maintainability, security, and generation efficiency under configurable weighting schemes. We evaluate three dense Qwen2.5-Coder models (3B, 7B, 14B) and the 16B-parameter DeepSeek-Coder-V2-Lite Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) model (2.4B active parameters) on 40 QuixBugs and 90 Defects4J bugs, all run locally on identical hardware to control for infrastructure effects. Model rankings change with the weighting scheme, showing that single-metric evaluation can hide trade-offs. The MoE model shows almost no statistically significant difference in correctness from the 7B and 14B dense models (McNemar's exact test) while using 3-6 times fewer active parameters, whereas correctness increases significantly across the three dense scales. These results suggest that active parameter count can be a more informative lens than total parameter count for sparse code models.

</details>


### [168] [Newer and Bigger, but Safer? A Longitudinal Study of the Functionality-Security Gap in LLM-Generated Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08240)
*Thiago Santos de Moura,Fynn Matuschek,Flavio Toffalini,Yannic Noller*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Across 32 LLMs and three releases per family, safety improves in absolute terms but the functionality–security gap remains; openness is not a reliable separator; compact models are generally less secure; persistent CWE-specific and language-dependent risks call for continuous security testing and cautious deployment.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: To test whether prior findings—that LLMs become smarter but not safer and that open-weight families stagnate—generalize to more model families and include compact models, and to characterize CWE-level risks over time and across languages.

Method: Longitudinal empirical evaluation of 32 LLMs from seven families (five open-weight), covering three successive releases per family in flagship and compact variants. Used CWEval benchmark with 119 tasks across five programming languages and 31 CWEs to measure functional plausibility versus security (CWE-based) performance and compare trajectories across families, sizes, and languages.

Result: All considered open-weight families significantly narrowed the functionality–security gap; proprietary models show mixed outcomes (some regressions); compact models typically produce less secure code but some compact releases are exceptions; persistent weaknesses include log injection (CWE-117) and HTTP response splitting (CWE-113); memory and integer-related weaknesses regressed in newest proprietary models; CWE impact is language-dependent. Recommendations include rerunning security checks after model upgrades, not assuming proprietary models are safer, and providing secure APIs around model outputs.

Conclusion: Newer LLM releases are safer in absolute terms but the functionality–security gap persists across families; openness alone does not determine safety improvements; compact models are often less secure than flagship ones with notable exceptions; specific CWEs (e.g., CWE-117, CWE-113) remain persistent and CWE risk varies by language.

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are widely used to generate code. Although their functional plausibility keeps improving, the generated code often contains security vulnerabilities. The functionality-security gap captures code that passes functional tests but fails security tests. A recent longitudinal study of three model families concluded that LLMs become smarter but not safer, with the only considered open-weight family stagnating. Whether this holds for other (open-weight) families and particularly for compact models remains open. We present a longitudinal study of the gap across 32 LLMs from seven model families (five open-weight), covering three successive releases per family in flagship and compact variants. Using CWEval with 119 tasks in five programming languages and 31 CWEs, we compare trajectories across families, model sizes, and languages. Newer models do become safer in absolute terms, although no family closes the gap. Unlike prior work, we find that openness does not separate the families: every considered open-weight family narrows the gap significantly, while Gemini 3.1 Pro keeps a gap as wide as the one reported for Llama. Compact models usually produce less secure code than their flagship counterparts, with notable exceptions (e.g., Gemini 3.7 Flash). At the CWE level, we confirm persistent weaknesses such as log injection (CWE-117) and HTTP response splitting (CWE-113) and regressions in the newest proprietary models on memory and integer weaknesses, and show that the same CWE carries very different risk across languages. From these results, we derive implications for LLM vendors, researchers, and developers. In particular, developers should assume neither that upgrades improve security nor that proprietary models are more secure; they should rerun security checks after each model change and provide secure APIs in the model's context.

</details>


### [169] [Learning from Failures: A Failure-Driven Prompt Refinement for LLM-Based Vulnerability Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08405)
*Mandana Ghadamian,David Mohaisen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: FDPR以重复失败为切入点，通过失误分类驱动针对性提示修正，显著提升LLM漏洞分析的可靠性并产出可复用的提示工程原则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多以整体性能比较提示策略，缺乏对失败根因的剖析与基于证据的迭代改进方法。需要一种可重复、面向失败模式的流程来系统性提升LLM在漏洞检测中的可靠性。

Method: 在Damn Vulnerable Java Application (DVJA)上收集模型输出，进行失误分类（假阳性、假阴性、不支持的推理、CWE错误分类等），据此设计定向提示修正；随后在Juliet Test Suite上评估改进后的提示，并进行多模型交叉验证以检验泛化性。

Result: 通过FDPR，提示在Juliet套件上表现出更高的稳定性与更少的系统性错误，跨模型验证显示若干提示改动具备可迁移性，同时总结出若干通用提示设计原则（例如要求逐项证据、结构化输出与CWE映射校验）。

Conclusion: 基于失败驱动的提示改进（FDPR）能有效提升大语言模型在漏洞分析任务中的可靠性，并产出可复用的提示设计原则；通过识别并针对性修正重复出现的失败模式，可系统化改进提示而非仅依赖聚合性能指标。

Abstract: Large Language Models have emerged as promising tools for software vulnerability analysis, but their effectiveness depends heavily on prompt design. Existing research primarily compares prompting strategies using aggregate performance metrics, providing limited insight into why models fail or how prompts can be improved systematically. We propose Failure-Driven Prompt Refinement (FDPR), a methodology that analyzes recurring model failures to guide evidence-based prompt refinement. Using the Damn Vulnerable Java Application (DVJA), we identify recurring failure modes, including false positives, false negatives, unsupported reasoning, and CWE misclassification, and translate them into targeted prompt refinements. We then evaluate the resulting prompt on the Juliet Test Suite and perform cross-model validation to assess generalizability. The results show that failure-driven refinement improves the reliability of LLM-based vulnerability analysis while yielding reusable prompt design principles. More broadly, this work demonstrates that recurring model failures provide a principled foundation for prompt engineering, enabling the systematic development of more reliable LLM-based vulnerability analysis systems.

</details>


### [170] [RAPO-Sol: Retrieval-Augmented Preference Optimization for Repository-Level Solidity Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08429)
*Rongcun Wang,Shi Chen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出 RAFT（训练时检索增强）+ DPO（基于 SAP 的偏好优化）两阶段方法，专为仓库级 Solidity 合约生成设计，在多模型多指标上均显著优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Solidity 合约涉及资产、权限、不可逆状态变化，仓库级代码生成要求合成完整合约/库并在状态变量、modifier、事件、继承、外部调用和访问控制等方面保持一致性，单纯逐行/函数生成难以满足合约级别的一致性和安全性需求。

Method: 两阶段框架：1) Retrieval-Augmented Fine-Tuning (RAFT)：在微调时为每个训练样本检索并拼接若干相似的 Solidity 示例，使模型在训练中学习到合约层面的复用和一致性模式，但在推理时不使用检索；2) Direct Preference Optimization (DPO)：基于偏好优化，通过 Solidity Semantic-Anchor Perturbation (SAP) 对参考合约生成语义上有缺陷的候选（改变验证语句、可见性、数据位置关键字、上下文变量、支付操作和低级调用等），并用这些正负对来训练模型偏好正确合约。

Result: 在 SolidityBench 数据集上，使用 CodeLlama-7B-Instruct、DeepSeek-Coder-6.7B-Instruct、Qwen2.5-Coder-7B-Instruct 三个模型进行实验：RAFT 相较于普通监督微调稳定提升性能，SAP 构造的负样本结合 DPO 进一步提高 BLEU 和 SolidityScore，完整 RAFT+DPO 管线在所有三种模型和评测指标上取得最优表现，表明训练时检索增强与语义感知的偏好优化互为补充，且推理阶段无额外检索开销。

Conclusion: RAFT+DPO 在仓库级别的 Solidity 代码生成任务上显著提升了性能；RAFT 在训练阶段引入检索式相似示例帮助模型学习合约级别模式，而 DPO 通过使用 SAP 生成语义上接近但有缺陷的负样本来优化模型偏好，两者组合在多个基模型和度量上均取得最优结果。

Abstract: Smart contracts written in Solidity manage assets, permissions, and irreversible state changes, making code generation both useful and security-critical. Repository-level Solidity generation is challenging because models must synthesize complete contracts or libraries while preserving consistency across state variables, modifiers, events, inheritance, external calls, and access-control logic. We present RAPO-Sol, a two-stage training framework for repository-level Solidity code generation. First, Retrieval-Augmented Fine-Tuning (RAFT) augments each training input with similar Solidity examples, helping the model learn recurring contract-level patterns while remaining retrieval-free at inference time. Second, Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) trains the model to prefer reference contracts over close but semantically flawed alternatives. We construct rejected samples using Solidity Semantic-Anchor Perturbation (SAP), which perturbs validation statements, visibility modifiers, data-location keywords, context variables, payment operations, and low-level calls. Experiments on SolidityBench with CodeLlama-7B-Instruct, DeepSeek-Coder-6.7B-Instruct, and Qwen2.5-Coder-7B-Instruct show that RAFT consistently improves over supervised fine-tuning, while SAP-based DPO provides further gains in BLEU and SolidityScore. The full RAFT+DPO pipeline achieves the best performance across all three models, demonstrating complementary benefits from retrieval during training and Solidity-aware preference optimization without adding retrieval cost at inference.

</details>


### [171] [A Case Study in Assuring AI-Written Software](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08651)
*Lindsey Ferris,Sierra Bonilla*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 案例显示：当代理生成大量代码且操作由非工程师管理时，测试/监控/审计与自动化修复均会失灵。有效的人类控制需要将目标、度量证据、权限与最终决策紧密对齐，并提升对监控/审计本身的可验证性与鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索当非专家借助软件代理构建和维护系统时，如何在规模化和复杂化下保持人类控制与安全性，评估常用监督手段的有效性与薄弱环节。

Method: 对一套由编程代理（coding agents）构建并由无正式软件工程训练的操作员治理的生产级医疗平台进行案例研究，追踪其演化为由人领导的元代理系统——分工为编写、监督/审查和规则传承，并分析测试/监控/审计/自动修复在实践中的失效模式。

Result: 发现多类监督工具存在系统性问题：监控常测量代理指标而非真实结果、审计可能无声失败、检测项从报告中消失、自动修复有时会引发操作中断。维持人类控制依赖于一致的目标语义、可信的证据链、清晰的权限边界和终审人的最终把关。

Conclusion: 在人类无法逐行审查海量代码或无软件背景操作者治理自动化系统时，仅靠代码审查不足以保证控制；必须将目标、衡量证据、代理权限和最终人为决策绑定到同一目标上以维持可控性。

Abstract: Software-engineering agents can enable people without formal software training to build systems they could not otherwise implement and simultaneously can produce more code than even experts can meaningfully inspect. In both cases, exhaustive code review is not reliable as the sole basis for human control. We report a case study of a production healthcare platform built through coding agents and governed by an operator without formal software-engineering training. Over time, its workflow grew into a human-led meta-agent system where one agent wrote code, other agents supervised and reviewed it, and project rules carried lessons forward. The operator found that tests, monitors and reviewing agents used to supervise the system were fallible. Some monitors measured proxies rather than outcomes, some audits failed silently, missing checks disappeared from reported results and one automated repair caused operational disruption. In this case, human control depended on keeping the intended outcome, the evidence used to judge it, the agents' permissions and the final human decision were all tied to the same underlying objective.

</details>
